===== src/GenesisPlayerSale.sol =====
   1: // SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
   2: pragma solidity 0.8.26;
   3: 
   4: import {IERC20} from "@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/IERC20.sol";
   5: import {SafeERC20} from "@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/utils/SafeERC20.sol";
   6: import {MerkleProof} from "@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/cryptography/MerkleProof.sol";
   7: import {Math} from "@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/math/Math.sol";
   8: import {ReentrancyGuard} from "@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/ReentrancyGuard.sol";
   9: 
  10: interface IGenesisPlayerIntegration {
  11:     /// @return Final, graduated native ETH auction price in Q96 wei per token base unit; zero before finality.
  12:     function playerPriceX96() external view returns (uint256);
  13:     /// @notice Must verify successful pool migration and the common public claim cliff.
  14:     function playerClaimsOpen() external view returns (bool);
  15:     function playerMigrationSucceeded() external view returns (bool);
  16:     /// @notice Atomically complete migration; revert on failure, returning sale ETH on rollback.
  17:     function finalizePlayerProceeds() external payable;
  18: }
  19: 
  20: /// @notice Fixed snapshot player tranche. Off-chain gameplay determines the Merkle list, never this contract.
  21: /// @dev Exact per-wallet settlement avoids aggregate rounding releasing refundable ETH. Anyone may
  22: /// settle another wallet; all participants must settle before migration. No privileged refund override.
  23: contract GenesisPlayerSale is ReentrancyGuard {
  24:     using SafeERC20 for IERC20;
  25:     uint256 public constant Q96 = 1 << 96;
  26:     uint256 public constant PURCHASE_WINDOW = 48 hours;
  27:     IERC20 public immutable token;
  28:     IGenesisPlayerIntegration public immutable integration;
  29:     bytes32 public immutable integrationCodeHash;
  30:     bytes32 public immutable merkleRoot;
  31:     uint256 public immutable chainId;
  32:     uint256 public immutable inventory;
  33:     uint256 public immutable openDeadline;
  34:     uint256 public immutable migrationDeadline;
  35:     address public immutable unsoldRecipient;
  36:     uint256 public priceX96;
  37:     uint256 public closesAt;
  38:     uint256 public totalRequested;
  39:     uint256 public participantCount;
  40:     uint256 public settledCount;
  41:     uint256 public totalAccepted;
  42:     uint256 public totalAllocated;
  43:     bool public released;
  44:     bool public cancelled;
  45:     bool public unsoldRecovered;
  46:     mapping(address => uint256) public requested;
  47:     mapping(address => uint256) public accepted;
  48:     mapping(address => uint256) public allocation;
  49:     mapping(address => bool) public settled;
  50:     mapping(address => bool) public refunded;
  51:     mapping(address => bool) public claimed;
  52:     event Opened(uint256 priceX96, uint256 closesAt);
  53:     event Contributed(address indexed wallet, uint256 amount);
  54:     event Settled(address indexed wallet, uint256 accepted, uint256 tokens);
  55:     event Cancelled();
  56:     event ProceedsReleased(uint256 amount);
  57:     event Refunded(address indexed wallet, uint256 amount);
  58:     event Claimed(address indexed wallet, uint256 amount);
  59:     error InvalidConfiguration();
  60:     error WrongPhase();
  61:     error InvalidEligibility();
  62:     error TransferFailed();
  63: 
  64:     constructor(IERC20 token_, IGenesisPlayerIntegration integration_, bytes32 root_, uint256 inventory_,
  65:         uint256 openDeadline_, uint256 migrationDeadline_, address unsoldRecipient_) {
  66:         if (address(token_).code.length == 0 || address(integration_).code.length == 0 || root_ == bytes32(0)
  67:             || inventory_ == 0 || openDeadline_ <= block.timestamp
  68:             || migrationDeadline_ <= openDeadline_ + PURCHASE_WINDOW || unsoldRecipient_ == address(0)
  69:             || unsoldRecipient_ == address(this)) revert InvalidConfiguration();
  70:         token = token_; integration = integration_; integrationCodeHash = address(integration_).codehash;
  71:         merkleRoot = root_; chainId = block.chainid; inventory = inventory_;
  72:         openDeadline = openDeadline_; migrationDeadline = migrationDeadline_; unsoldRecipient = unsoldRecipient_;
  73:     }
  74:     function cap(uint8 daysPlayed) public pure returns (uint256) {
  75:         if (daysPlayed == 1) return 0.5 ether;
  76:         if (daysPlayed == 2) return 1 ether;
  77:         if (daysPlayed == 3) return 2.5 ether;
  78:         if (daysPlayed == 4 || daysPlayed == 5) return 5 ether;
  79:         revert InvalidEligibility();
  80:     }
  81:     /// @dev Standard double-hashed Merkle leaf. Predict the CREATE address from the deployer's nonce
  82:     /// before building the root; CREATE2 initcode includes the root and is not a simple prediction.
  83:     function leaf(address wallet, uint8 daysPlayed) public view returns (bytes32) {
  84:         return keccak256(bytes.concat(keccak256(abi.encode(chainId, address(this), wallet, daysPlayed))));
  85:     }
  86:     function open() external nonReentrant {
  87:         if (cancelled || closesAt != 0 || block.timestamp >= openDeadline
  88:             || block.chainid != chainId || address(integration).codehash != integrationCodeHash
  89:             || token.balanceOf(address(this)) < inventory) revert WrongPhase();
  90:         uint256 price = integration.playerPriceX96();
  91:         if (price == 0 || Math.mulDiv(inventory, price, Q96) == 0) revert InvalidConfiguration();
  92:         priceX96 = price; closesAt = block.timestamp + PURCHASE_WINDOW;
  93:         emit Opened(price, closesAt);
  94:     }
  95:     function contribute(uint8 daysPlayed, bytes32[] calldata proof) external payable nonReentrant {
  96:         if (cancelled || closesAt == 0 || block.timestamp >= closesAt || block.chainid != chainId) revert WrongPhase();
  97:         uint256 maximum = cap(daysPlayed);
  98:         if (msg.value == 0 || requested[msg.sender] + msg.value > maximum
  99:             || !MerkleProof.verifyCalldata(proof, merkleRoot, leaf(msg.sender, daysPlayed))) revert InvalidEligibility();
 100:         if (requested[msg.sender] == 0) participantCount++;
 101:         requested[msg.sender] += msg.value; totalRequested += msg.value;
 102:         emit Contributed(msg.sender, msg.value);
 103:     }
 104:     function settle(address wallet) external {
 105:         if (cancelled || released || closesAt == 0 || block.timestamp < closesAt
 106:             || block.timestamp >= migrationDeadline || requested[wallet] == 0 || settled[wallet]) revert WrongPhase();
 107:         uint256 capacity = Math.mulDiv(inventory, priceX96, Q96);
 108:         uint256 tokens = totalRequested > capacity
 109:             ? Math.mulDiv(requested[wallet], inventory, totalRequested)
 110:             : Math.mulDiv(requested[wallet], Q96, priceX96);
 111:         uint256 payment = Math.mulDiv(tokens, priceX96, Q96, Math.Rounding.Ceil);
 112:         settled[wallet] = true; settledCount++; allocation[wallet] = tokens; accepted[wallet] = payment;
 113:         totalAllocated += tokens; totalAccepted += payment;
 114:         emit Settled(wallet, payment, tokens);
 115:     }
 116:     function releaseProceeds() external nonReentrant {
 117:         if (msg.sender != address(integration) || cancelled || released || closesAt == 0
 118:             || block.timestamp < closesAt || block.timestamp >= migrationDeadline
 119:             || settledCount != participantCount || address(integration).codehash != integrationCodeHash
 120:             || block.chainid != chainId) revert WrongPhase();
 121:         released = true;
 122:         integration.finalizePlayerProceeds{value: totalAccepted}();
 123:         if (!integration.playerMigrationSucceeded()) revert WrongPhase();
 124:         emit ProceedsReleased(totalAccepted);
 125:     }
 126:     function cancel() external {
 127:         if (released || cancelled || block.timestamp < migrationDeadline) revert WrongPhase();
 128:         cancelled = true; emit Cancelled();
 129:     }
 130:     function refund() external nonReentrant {
 131:         if ((!cancelled && !released) || refunded[msg.sender] || requested[msg.sender] == 0) revert WrongPhase();
 132:         refunded[msg.sender] = true;
 133:         uint256 amount = requested[msg.sender] - (cancelled ? 0 : accepted[msg.sender]);
 134:         (bool ok,) = msg.sender.call{value: amount}("");
 135:         if (!ok) revert TransferFailed();
 136:         emit Refunded(msg.sender, amount);
 137:     }
 138:     function claim() external nonReentrant {
 139:         if (!released || cancelled || claimed[msg.sender] || allocation[msg.sender] == 0
 140:             || block.chainid != chainId || address(integration).codehash != integrationCodeHash
 141:             || !integration.playerClaimsOpen()) revert WrongPhase();
 142:         claimed[msg.sender] = true; token.safeTransfer(msg.sender, allocation[msg.sender]);
 143:         emit Claimed(msg.sender, allocation[msg.sender]);
 144:     }
 145:     function recoverUnsold() external nonReentrant {
 146:         if ((!released && !cancelled) || unsoldRecovered) revert WrongPhase();
 147:         unsoldRecovered = true; token.safeTransfer(unsoldRecipient, inventory - (cancelled ? 0 : totalAllocated));
 148:     }
 149: }
===== src/genesis-auction/OmertaGuardedAuction.sol =====
   1: // SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
   2: pragma solidity 0.8.26;
   3: 
   4: import {ContinuousClearingAuction} from "./vendor/cca/ContinuousClearingAuction.sol";
   5: import {AuctionParameters} from "./vendor/cca/interfaces/IContinuousClearingAuction.sol";
   6: 
   7: interface IOmertaGenesisClaimGate {
   8:     function playerClaimsOpen() external view returns (bool);
   9: }
  10: 
  11: /// @notice Pinned CCA with a common launch-success gate on both public token claim paths.
  12: /// @dev Deploy directly for the player-first route. The existing external CCA factory does not
  13: /// deploy this implementation. Bid exits and currency refunds retain upstream semantics.
  14: contract OmertaGuardedAuction is ContinuousClearingAuction {
  15:     IOmertaGenesisClaimGate public immutable launchGate;
  16:     bytes32 public immutable launchGateCodeHash;
  17:     uint256 public immutable launchChainId;
  18: 
  19:     error InvalidLaunchGate();
  20:     error LaunchClaimsClosed();
  21: 
  22:     constructor(address token_, uint128 totalSupply_, AuctionParameters memory parameters_,
  23:         IOmertaGenesisClaimGate gate_)
  24:         ContinuousClearingAuction(token_, totalSupply_, parameters_, address(0))
  25:     {
  26:         if (address(gate_).code.length == 0 || parameters_.fundsRecipient != address(gate_)
  27:             || parameters_.currency != address(0) || parameters_.validationHook != address(0))
  28:             revert InvalidLaunchGate();
  29:         launchGate = gate_;
  30:         launchGateCodeHash = address(gate_).codehash;
  31:         launchChainId = block.chainid;
  32:     }
  33: 
  34:     /// @notice Native auction-clock cliff; coordinator uses this without replacing the chain clock.
  35:     function claimsReady() external view returns (bool) {
  36:         return _getBlockNumberish() >= CLAIM_BLOCK;
  37:     }
  38: 
  39:     function blockNumberish() external view returns (uint256) {
  40:         return _getBlockNumberish();
  41:     }
  42: 
  43:     function _internalClaimTokens(uint256 bidId)
  44:         internal override returns (address owner, uint256 tokensFilled)
  45:     {
  46:         if (block.chainid != launchChainId || address(launchGate).codehash != launchGateCodeHash
  47:             || !launchGate.playerClaimsOpen()) revert LaunchClaimsClosed();
  48:         return super._internalClaimTokens(bidId);
  49:     }
  50: }
===== src/market-v2/OmertaGenesisCoordinatorV2.sol =====
   1: // SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
   2: pragma solidity 0.8.26;
   3: 
   4: import {IERC20} from "@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/IERC20.sol";
   5: import {SafeERC20} from "@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/utils/SafeERC20.sol";
   6: import {Math} from "@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/math/Math.sol";
   7: import {ReentrancyGuard} from "@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/ReentrancyGuard.sol";
   8: import {IPoolManager} from "v4-core/interfaces/IPoolManager.sol";
   9: import {PoolKey} from "v4-core/types/PoolKey.sol";
  10: import {PoolId, PoolIdLibrary} from "v4-core/types/PoolId.sol";
  11: import {IERC721} from "@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC721/IERC721.sol";
  12: import {Currency} from "v4-core/types/Currency.sol";
  13: import {IHooks} from "v4-core/interfaces/IHooks.sol";
  14: import {StateLibrary} from "v4-core/libraries/StateLibrary.sol";
  15: import {TickMath} from "v4-core/libraries/TickMath.sol";
  16: import {IAllowanceTransfer} from "permit2/src/interfaces/IAllowanceTransfer.sol";
  17: import {IPositionManager} from "../../lib/v4-periphery/src/interfaces/IPositionManager.sol";
  18: import {LiquidityAmounts} from "../../lib/v4-periphery/src/libraries/LiquidityAmounts.sol";
  19: import {Actions} from "../../lib/v4-periphery/src/libraries/Actions.sol";
  20: import {GenesisPlayerSale, IGenesisPlayerIntegration} from "../GenesisPlayerSale.sol";
  21: 
  22: interface IGenesisGatedAuction {
  23:     function checkpoint() external;
  24:     function lbpInitializationParams() external view returns (uint256, uint256, uint256);
  25:     function fundsRecipient() external view returns (address);
  26:     function launchGate() external view returns (address);
  27:     function token() external view returns (address);
  28:     function currency() external view returns (address);
  29:     function claimsReady() external view returns (bool);
  30:     function startBlock() external view returns (uint64);
  31:     function blockNumberish() external view returns (uint256);
  32:     function sweepCurrency() external;
  33: }
  34: 
  35: interface IGenesisMarketHook {
  36:     function authorized() external view returns (address);
  37:     function poolKey() external view returns (PoolKey memory);
  38: }
  39: 
  40: /// @notice Atomically joins both Genesis sale legs into the current market's initial liquidity.
  41: /// @dev Requires the guarded auction, not the deployed CCA/LBP factory. No best-effort migration:
  42: /// initialization, position minting and player proceeds all roll back together on any failure.
  43: contract OmertaGenesisCoordinatorV2 is IGenesisPlayerIntegration, ReentrancyGuard {
  44:     using SafeERC20 for IERC20;
  45:     using PoolIdLibrary for PoolKey;
  46:     using StateLibrary for IPoolManager;
  47:     IPoolManager public immutable poolManager;
  48:     IPositionManager public immutable positionManager;
  49:     IAllowanceTransfer public immutable permit2;
  50:     IERC20 public immutable omr;
  51:     address public immutable configurator;
  52:     address public immutable liquidityOwner;
  53:     address public immutable treasury;
  54:     address public immutable vig;
  55:     address public immutable founder;
  56:     uint128 public immutable tokenReserve;
  57:     uint256 public immutable chainId;
  58:     bytes32 private immutable _managerHash;
  59:     bytes32 private immutable _positionsHash;
  60:     bytes32 private immutable _permitHash;
  61:     bytes32 private immutable _tokenHash;
  62:     bytes32 public auctionCodeHash;
  63:     bytes32 public saleCodeHash;
  64:     bytes32 public hookCodeHash;
  65:     PoolKey private _key;
  66:     IGenesisGatedAuction public auction;
  67:     GenesisPlayerSale public playerSale;
  68:     uint256 public override playerPriceX96;
  69:     bool public override playerMigrationSucceeded;
  70:     bool private _migrating;
  71:     mapping(address => uint256) public residualCredit;
  72:     event Bound(address auction, address playerSale);
  73:     event AuctionFinalized(uint256 priceX96);
  74:     event Migrated(uint256 positionId, uint128 liquidity, uint256 publicProceeds, uint256 playerProceeds);
  75:     error BadConfiguration();
  76:     error WrongPhase();
  77:     error SettlementMismatch();
  78: 
  79:     constructor(IPoolManager manager_, IPositionManager positions_, IAllowanceTransfer permit2_,
  80:         IERC20 token_, IHooks hook_, uint24 fee_, int24 spacing_, uint128 reserve_,
  81:         address liquidityOwner_, address treasury_, address vig_, address founder_) {
  82:         if (address(manager_).code.length == 0 || address(positions_).code.length == 0
  83:             || address(permit2_).code.length == 0 || address(token_).code.length == 0
  84:             || address(hook_) == address(0) || spacing_ <= 0 || reserve_ == 0
  85:             || reserve_ > uint128(type(int128).max) || liquidityOwner_ == address(0)
  86:             || treasury_ == address(0) || vig_ == address(0) || founder_ == address(0)
  87:             || address(positions_.poolManager()) != address(manager_)) revert BadConfiguration();
  88:         poolManager = manager_; positionManager = positions_; permit2 = permit2_; omr = token_;
  89:         configurator = msg.sender; liquidityOwner = liquidityOwner_; tokenReserve = reserve_;
  90:         treasury = treasury_; vig = vig_; founder = founder_;
  91:         chainId = block.chainid;
  92:         _managerHash = address(manager_).codehash; _positionsHash = address(positions_).codehash;
  93:         _permitHash = address(permit2_).codehash; _tokenHash = address(token_).codehash;
  94:         _key = PoolKey(Currency.wrap(address(0)), Currency.wrap(address(token_)), fee_, spacing_, hook_);
  95:     }
  96: 
  97:     function poolKey() external view returns (PoolKey memory) { return _key; }
  98: 
  99:     /// @notice One-time deployment binding; addresses cannot be changed once the sale is configured.
 100:     function bind(IGenesisGatedAuction auction_, GenesisPlayerSale sale_) external {
 101:         if (msg.sender != configurator || address(auction) != address(0)
 102:             || address(auction_).code.length == 0 || address(sale_).code.length == 0
 103:             || auction_.fundsRecipient() != address(this) || auction_.launchGate() != address(this)
 104:             || auction_.currency() != address(0) || auction_.token() != address(omr)
 105:             || auction_.blockNumberish() >= auction_.startBlock()
 106:             || address(_key.hooks).code.length == 0
 107:             || address(sale_.integration()) != address(this) || address(sale_.token()) != address(omr)) {
 108:             revert BadConfiguration();
 109:         }
 110:         IGenesisMarketHook marketHook = IGenesisMarketHook(address(_key.hooks));
 111:         if (marketHook.authorized() != address(this)
 112:             || PoolId.unwrap(marketHook.poolKey().toId()) != PoolId.unwrap(_key.toId())) revert BadConfiguration();
 113:         auctionCodeHash = address(auction_).codehash; saleCodeHash = address(sale_).codehash;
 114:         hookCodeHash = address(_key.hooks).codehash;
 115:         auction = auction_; playerSale = sale_; emit Bound(address(auction_), address(sale_));
 116:     }
 117: 
 118:     /// @notice Finality and graduation are enforced by the auction's initialization-parameter read.
 119:     function checkpointAuction() external nonReentrant {
 120:         if (address(auction) == address(0) || playerPriceX96 != 0) revert WrongPhase();
 121:         auction.checkpoint();
 122:         (uint256 price,,) = auction.lbpInitializationParams();
 123:         if (price == 0) revert SettlementMismatch();
 124:         playerPriceX96 = price; emit AuctionFinalized(price);
 125:     }
 126: 
 127:     function playerClaimsOpen() external view override returns (bool) {
 128:         return playerMigrationSucceeded && auction.claimsReady();
 129:     }
 130: 
 131:     function migrate() external nonReentrant {
 132:         if (playerPriceX96 == 0 || playerMigrationSucceeded || _migrating) revert WrongPhase();
 133:         _migrating = true;
 134:         playerSale.releaseProceeds();
 135:         _migrating = false;
 136:         if (!playerMigrationSucceeded) revert SettlementMismatch();
 137:     }
 138: 
 139:     /// @dev Callback intentionally has no second reentrancy guard: migrate holds the outer guard.
 140:     function finalizePlayerProceeds() external payable override {
 141:         if (!_migrating || msg.sender != address(playerSale) || playerMigrationSucceeded
 142:             || !playerSale.released() || msg.value != playerSale.totalAccepted()) revert WrongPhase();
 143:         _initializeLiquidity(msg.value);
 144:     }
 145: 
 146:     /// @notice A timed-out player tranche refunds its buyers without permanently locking public buyers.
 147:     function migratePublicAfterCancellation() external nonReentrant {
 148:         if (playerPriceX96 == 0 || playerMigrationSucceeded || !playerSale.cancelled()) revert WrongPhase();
 149:         _initializeLiquidity(0);
 150:     }
 151: 
 152:     function _initializeLiquidity(uint256 playerProceeds) private {
 153:         if (block.chainid != chainId || address(auction).codehash != auctionCodeHash
 154:             || address(playerSale).codehash != saleCodeHash || address(_key.hooks).codehash != hookCodeHash
 155:             || address(poolManager).codehash != _managerHash || address(positionManager).codehash != _positionsHash
 156:             || address(permit2).codehash != _permitHash || address(omr).codehash != _tokenHash) revert WrongPhase();
 157:         uint256 beforeSweep = address(this).balance;
 158:         (uint256 price,, uint256 publicProceeds) = auction.lbpInitializationParams();
 159:         if (price != playerPriceX96) revert SettlementMismatch();
 160:         auction.sweepCurrency();
 161:         if (address(this).balance - beforeSweep != publicProceeds) revert SettlementMismatch();
 162:         uint256 total = publicProceeds + playerProceeds;
 163:         uint256 nativeBudget = Math.mulDiv(total, 3750, 10_000);
 164:         if (nativeBudget == 0 || nativeBudget > uint128(type(int128).max)
 165:             || omr.balanceOf(address(this)) < tokenReserve) revert SettlementMismatch();
 166:         uint160 sqrtPrice = uint160(Math.sqrt(Math.mulDiv(uint256(1) << 192, uint256(1) << 96, price)));
 167:         if (sqrtPrice < TickMath.MIN_SQRT_PRICE || sqrtPrice >= TickMath.MAX_SQRT_PRICE) revert SettlementMismatch();
 168:         PoolKey memory key = _key;
 169:         (uint160 existing,,,) = poolManager.getSlot0(key.toId());
 170:         if (existing != 0) revert WrongPhase();
 171:         poolManager.initialize(key, sqrtPrice);
 172:         int24 lower = TickMath.minUsableTick(key.tickSpacing);
 173:         int24 upper = TickMath.maxUsableTick(key.tickSpacing);
 174:         uint128 liquidity = LiquidityAmounts.getLiquidityForAmounts(sqrtPrice,
 175:             TickMath.getSqrtPriceAtTick(lower), TickMath.getSqrtPriceAtTick(upper), nativeBudget, tokenReserve);
 176:         // OMR must fund the ETH budget; insufficient matching tokens cannot silently reduce LP.
 177:         if (liquidity == 0 || liquidity != LiquidityAmounts.getLiquidityForAmount0(
 178:             sqrtPrice, TickMath.getSqrtPriceAtTick(upper), nativeBudget)) revert SettlementMismatch();
 179:         uint256 positionId = positionManager.nextTokenId();
 180:         bytes[] memory params = new bytes[](3);
 181:         params[0] = abi.encode(key, lower, upper, uint256(liquidity), uint128(nativeBudget), tokenReserve, liquidityOwner, bytes(""));
 182:         params[1] = abi.encode(key.currency0, key.currency1);
 183:         params[2] = abi.encode(key.currency0, address(this));
 184:         omr.forceApprove(address(permit2), tokenReserve);
 185:         permit2.approve(address(omr), address(positionManager), tokenReserve, uint48(block.timestamp));
 186:         uint256 nativeBefore = address(this).balance;
 187:         uint256 positionManagerDust = address(positionManager).balance;
 188:         uint256 tokenBefore = omr.balanceOf(address(this));
 189:         positionManager.modifyLiquidities{value: nativeBudget}(abi.encode(
 190:             abi.encodePacked(uint8(Actions.MINT_POSITION), uint8(Actions.SETTLE_PAIR), uint8(Actions.SWEEP)), params), block.timestamp);
 191:         permit2.approve(address(omr), address(positionManager), 0, 0);
 192:         omr.forceApprove(address(permit2), 0);
 193:         (PoolKey memory positionKey,) = positionManager.getPoolAndPositionInfo(positionId);
 194:         if (PoolId.unwrap(positionKey.toId()) != PoolId.unwrap(key.toId())
 195:             || IERC721(address(positionManager)).ownerOf(positionId) != liquidityOwner) revert SettlementMismatch();
 196:         if (positionManager.getPositionLiquidity(positionId) != liquidity
 197:             || address(this).balance >= nativeBefore + positionManagerDust
 198:             || omr.balanceOf(address(this)) >= tokenBefore) revert SettlementMismatch();
 199:         uint256 spent = nativeBefore + positionManagerDust - address(this).balance;
 200:         // Full-range liquidity arithmetic leaves rounding dust; bound it to 1% of the LP budget.
 201:         if (spent > nativeBudget || spent < Math.mulDiv(nativeBudget, 9900, 10_000)) revert SettlementMismatch();
 202:         uint256 residual = total - spent;
 203:         residualCredit[treasury] += Math.mulDiv(residual, 4000, 10_000);
 204:         residualCredit[vig] += Math.mulDiv(residual, 3600, 10_000);
 205:         residualCredit[founder] += residual - Math.mulDiv(residual, 4000, 10_000) - Math.mulDiv(residual, 3600, 10_000);
 206:         playerMigrationSucceeded = true;
 207:         emit Migrated(positionId, liquidity, publicProceeds, playerProceeds);
 208:     }
 209: 
 210:     function withdrawResidual() external nonReentrant {
 211:         uint256 amount = residualCredit[msg.sender]; residualCredit[msg.sender] = 0;
 212:         (bool ok,) = msg.sender.call{value: amount}("");
 213:         if (!ok) revert SettlementMismatch();
 214:     }
 215: 
 216:     function recoverTokenDust() external nonReentrant {
 217:         if (!playerMigrationSucceeded) revert WrongPhase();
 218:         omr.safeTransfer(treasury, omr.balanceOf(address(this)));
 219:     }
 220: 
 221:     receive() external payable {
 222:         if (msg.sender != address(auction) && msg.sender != address(positionManager)) revert WrongPhase();
 223:     }
 224: }
===== src/market-v2/OmertaHookV2.sol =====
   1: // SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
   2: pragma solidity 0.8.26;
   3: 
   4: import {IERC165} from "@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/introspection/IERC165.sol";
   5: import {IERC20Metadata} from "@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/extensions/IERC20Metadata.sol";
   6: import {ReentrancyGuard} from "@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/ReentrancyGuard.sol";
   7: import {IHooks} from "v4-core/interfaces/IHooks.sol";
   8: import {IPoolManager} from "v4-core/interfaces/IPoolManager.sol";
   9: import {Hooks} from "v4-core/libraries/Hooks.sol";
  10: import {StateLibrary} from "v4-core/libraries/StateLibrary.sol";
  11: import {PoolKey} from "v4-core/types/PoolKey.sol";
  12: import {PoolId} from "v4-core/types/PoolId.sol";
  13: import {Currency} from "v4-core/types/Currency.sol";
  14: import {BalanceDelta, BalanceDeltaLibrary} from "v4-core/types/BalanceDelta.sol";
  15: import {BeforeSwapDelta, BeforeSwapDeltaLibrary} from "v4-core/types/BeforeSwapDelta.sol";
  16: import {ModifyLiquidityParams, SwapParams} from "v4-core/types/PoolOperation.sol";
  17: import {IInitializerHook} from "../interfaces/IInitializerHook.sol";
  18: import {IOmrV4ObservationSource} from "../interfaces/IOmrV4ObservationSource.sol";
  19: 
  20: /// @notice Immutable canonical ETH/OMR market settlement and observation layer.
  21: /// @dev Sells retain the 9% base tax (2% dev, 1.6% RWA, 2.4% community, 3% POL).
  22: ///      Additional sell-pressure surcharge is separately owed to stability, never funding slices.
  23: ///      Exact-input fees use actual output; exact-output fees use actual input, including partial fills.
  24: ///      No strategy, oracle, recipient, or game callback executes during a swap. No owner can pause,
  25: ///      change taxes, extend the launch window, redirect accrued funds, or replace implementation.
  26: ///      Active liquidity is raw v4 L, not a claim of executable ETH depth or economic fair value.
  27: contract OmertaHookV2 is IHooks, IInitializerHook, IOmrV4ObservationSource, ReentrancyGuard {
  28:     uint160 public constant HOOK_FLAGS = uint160(
  29:         Hooks.BEFORE_INITIALIZE_FLAG | Hooks.AFTER_INITIALIZE_FLAG | Hooks.AFTER_ADD_LIQUIDITY_FLAG
  30:             | Hooks.AFTER_REMOVE_LIQUIDITY_FLAG | Hooks.BEFORE_SWAP_FLAG | Hooks.AFTER_SWAP_FLAG
  31:             | Hooks.AFTER_SWAP_RETURNS_DELTA_FLAG
  32:     );
  33:     uint16 public constant BASE_SELL_BPS = 900;
  34:     uint16 public constant MAX_SURGE_BPS = 100;
  35:     uint16 public constant MAX_HOOK_BPS = 1000;
  36:     uint16 public constant MAX_OPENING_BLOCKS = 200;
  37:     uint32 public constant PRESSURE_DECAY_SECONDS = 60;
  38:     uint8 public constant DEV = 0;
  39:     uint8 public constant RWA = 1;
  40:     uint8 public constant COMMUNITY = 2;
  41:     uint8 public constant POL = 3;
  42:     uint8 public constant STABILITY = 4;
  43: 
  44:     struct OpeningConfig {
  45:         uint16 blocks;
  46:         uint16 buyBps;
  47:         uint128 maxBuyQuote;
  48:     }
  49: 
  50:     /// @dev Integral fields cover `observedSeconds`, which can be short only in the genesis epoch.
  51:     ///      The record is finalized before it is published. Volumes are gross pool ETH deltas,
  52:     ///      exclude hook fees, and do not imply distinct traders or manipulation-resistant demand.
  53:     struct Epoch {
  54:         uint64 id;
  55:         uint64 end;
  56:         uint32 observedSeconds;
  57:         int128 tickSeconds;
  58:         uint128 tickSquaredSeconds;
  59:         uint256 liquiditySeconds;
  60:         uint128 minLiquidity;
  61:         uint128 buyQuote;
  62:         uint128 sellQuote;
  63:         uint64 swaps;
  64:     }
  65: 
  66:     IPoolManager public immutable poolManager;
  67:     address public immutable omr;
  68:     address public immutable authorized;
  69:     uint24 public immutable poolFee;
  70:     int24 public immutable tickSpacing;
  71:     uint32 public immutable epochDuration;
  72:     uint24 public immutable surgeFullTicks;
  73:     uint16 public immutable openingBlocks;
  74:     uint16 public immutable openingBuyBps;
  75:     uint128 public immutable openingMaxBuyQuote;
  76:     address[5] public recipients;
  77:     mapping(Currency => mapping(uint8 => uint256)) public owed;
  78:     mapping(Currency => uint256) public totalOwed;
  79: 
  80:     uint64 public openedAtBlock;
  81:     uint64 public openingEndsAtBlock;
  82:     bool public initialized;
  83:     int24 public lastTick;
  84:     uint128 public lastLiquidity;
  85:     uint64 public lastTimestamp;
  86:     int256 private _tickCumulative;
  87:     Epoch private _active;
  88:     Epoch private _latest;
  89:     uint24 public sellPressureTicks;
  90:     uint64 public pressureUpdatedAt;
  91:     bytes32 private constant PRE_TICK_SLOT = keccak256("omerta.v2.hook.preTick");
  92: 
  93:     error InvalidConfiguration();
  94:     error HookAddressMismatch();
  95:     error OnlyPoolManager();
  96:     error PoolNotAllowed();
  97:     error NotInitializer();
  98:     error OpeningBuyTooLarge();
  99:     error HookNotImplemented();
 100:     error InvalidRecipient();
 101:     error NotRecipient();
 102:     error OnlyPoolManagerETH();
 103: 
 104:     event PoolOpened(PoolId indexed id, uint64 blockNumber, uint64 openingEnd);
 105:     event FeesAccrued(address indexed sender, Currency indexed currency, bool sell, uint256 base, uint256 stability);
 106:     event FeesPaid(Currency indexed currency, uint8 indexed bucket, address indexed recipient, uint256 amount);
 107:     event EpochFinalized(uint64 indexed epoch, uint64 end, uint32 observedSeconds);
 108: 
 109:     constructor(
 110:         IPoolManager manager_,
 111:         address omr_,
 112:         address initializer_,
 113:         uint24 poolFee_,
 114:         int24 tickSpacing_,
 115:         address[5] memory recipients_,
 116:         OpeningConfig memory opening_,
 117:         uint24 surgeFullTicks_,
 118:         uint32 epochDuration_
 119:     ) {
 120:         if (
 121:             address(manager_) == address(0) || omr_ == address(0) || initializer_ == address(0)
 122:                 || poolFee_ > 100_000 || tickSpacing_ <= 0 || tickSpacing_ > 32767
 123:                 || opening_.blocks > MAX_OPENING_BLOCKS || opening_.buyBps > MAX_HOOK_BPS
 124:                 || (opening_.blocks == 0 && (opening_.buyBps != 0 || opening_.maxBuyQuote != 0))
 125:                 || surgeFullTicks_ == 0 || surgeFullTicks_ > 100_000
 126:                 || epochDuration_ < 60 || epochDuration_ > 1 days
 127:         ) revert InvalidConfiguration();
 128:         if (IERC20Metadata(omr_).decimals() != 18) revert InvalidConfiguration();
 129:         if (uint160(address(this)) & Hooks.ALL_HOOK_MASK != HOOK_FLAGS) revert HookAddressMismatch();
 130:         for (uint256 i; i < 5; ++i) {
 131:             if (recipients_[i] == address(0) || recipients_[i] == address(this)) revert InvalidRecipient();
 132:         }
 133:         poolManager = manager_;
 134:         omr = omr_;
 135:         authorized = initializer_;
 136:         poolFee = poolFee_;
 137:         tickSpacing = tickSpacing_;
 138:         recipients = recipients_;
 139:         openingBlocks = opening_.blocks;
 140:         openingBuyBps = opening_.buyBps;
 141:         openingMaxBuyQuote = opening_.maxBuyQuote;
 142:         surgeFullTicks = surgeFullTicks_;
 143:         epochDuration = epochDuration_;
 144:     }
 145: 
 146:     modifier onlyPoolManager() {
 147:         if (msg.sender != address(poolManager)) revert OnlyPoolManager();
 148:         _;
 149:     }
 150: 
 151:     receive() external payable {
 152:         if (msg.sender != address(poolManager)) revert OnlyPoolManagerETH();
 153:     }
 154: 
 155:     function poolKey() public view returns (PoolKey memory) {
 156:         return PoolKey(Currency.wrap(address(0)), Currency.wrap(omr), poolFee, tickSpacing, IHooks(address(this)));
 157:     }
 158: 
 159:     function supportsInterface(bytes4 interfaceId) external pure returns (bool) {
 160:         return interfaceId == type(IERC165).interfaceId || interfaceId == type(IInitializerHook).interfaceId
 161:             || interfaceId == type(IOmrV4ObservationSource).interfaceId;
 162:     }
 163: 
 164:     function getHookPermissions() external pure returns (Hooks.Permissions memory p) {
 165:         p.beforeInitialize = true;
 166:         p.afterInitialize = true;
 167:         p.afterAddLiquidity = true;
 168:         p.afterRemoveLiquidity = true;
 169:         p.beforeSwap = true;
 170:         p.afterSwap = true;
 171:         p.afterSwapReturnDelta = true;
 172:     }
 173: 
 174:     function beforeInitialize(address sender, PoolKey calldata key, uint160)
 175:         external onlyPoolManager returns (bytes4)
 176:     {
 177:         _checkPool(key);
 178:         if (sender != authorized) revert NotInitializer();
 179:         return IHooks.beforeInitialize.selector;
 180:     }
 181: 
 182:     function afterInitialize(address, PoolKey calldata key, uint160, int24 tick)
 183:         external onlyPoolManager returns (bytes4)
 184:     {
 185:         _checkPool(key);
 186:         if (initialized) revert PoolNotAllowed();
 187:         initialized = true;
 188:         openedAtBlock = uint64(block.number);
 189:         openingEndsAtBlock = uint64(block.number + openingBlocks);
 190:         lastTick = tick;
 191:         lastTimestamp = uint64(block.timestamp);
 192:         _active = _newEpoch(uint64(block.timestamp / epochDuration));
 193:         emit PoolOpened(key.toId(), openedAtBlock, openingEndsAtBlock);
 194:         return IHooks.afterInitialize.selector;
 195:     }
 196: 
 197:     function beforeSwap(address, PoolKey calldata key, SwapParams calldata, bytes calldata)
 198:         external onlyPoolManager returns (bytes4, BeforeSwapDelta, uint24)
 199:     {
 200:         _checkPool(key);
 201:         // Store actual pre-swap tick, never a router-supplied trader identity or price.
 202:         (, int24 tick,,) = StateLibrary.getSlot0(poolManager, key.toId());
 203:         bytes32 slot = PRE_TICK_SLOT;
 204:         assembly ("memory-safe") { tstore(slot, tick) }
 205:         return (IHooks.beforeSwap.selector, BeforeSwapDeltaLibrary.ZERO_DELTA, 0);
 206:     }
 207: 
 208:     function afterSwap(address sender, PoolKey calldata key, SwapParams calldata params, BalanceDelta delta, bytes calldata)
 209:         external onlyPoolManager returns (bytes4, int128)
 210:     {
 211:         _checkPool(key);
 212:         _advance();
 213:         _adoptPoolState(key.toId());
 214:         return (IHooks.afterSwap.selector, _collectFee(sender, key, params, delta));
 215:     }
 216: 
 217:     function _collectFee(address sender, PoolKey calldata key, SwapParams calldata params, BalanceDelta delta)
 218:         private returns (int128)
 219:     {
 220:         bool sell = !params.zeroForOne; // currency0 ETH; currency1 OMR, always.
 221:         uint256 quote = _abs(delta.amount0());
 222:         if (sell) _active.sellQuote = _saturatingAdd(_active.sellQuote, quote);
 223:         else _active.buyQuote = _saturatingAdd(_active.buyQuote, quote);
 224:         if (_active.swaps < type(uint64).max) ++_active.swaps;
 225:         uint16 extra = sell ? _surgeRate() : _openingRate(quote, params.amountSpecified > 0);
 226:         bool currency0 = params.amountSpecified < 0 ? !params.zeroForOne : params.zeroForOne;
 227:         Currency currency = currency0 ? key.currency0 : key.currency1;
 228:         uint256 amount = _abs(currency0 ? delta.amount0() : delta.amount1());
 229:         uint256 base = sell ? amount * BASE_SELL_BPS / 10_000 : 0;
 230:         uint256 stability = amount * extra / 10_000;
 231:         uint256 total = base + stability;
 232:         if (total == 0) return 0;
 233:         _accrueBase(currency, base);
 234:         owed[currency][STABILITY] += stability;
 235:         totalOwed[currency] += total;
 236:         emit FeesAccrued(sender, currency, sell, base, stability);
 237:         poolManager.take(currency, address(this), total);
 238:         // <=10% of the magnitude of an int128, including its negative minimum, fits int128.
 239:         return int128(uint128(total));
 240:     }
 241: 
 242:     function afterAddLiquidity(address, PoolKey calldata key, ModifyLiquidityParams calldata, BalanceDelta, BalanceDelta, bytes calldata)
 243:         external onlyPoolManager returns (bytes4, BalanceDelta)
 244:     {
 245:         _afterLiquidity(key);
 246:         return (IHooks.afterAddLiquidity.selector, BalanceDeltaLibrary.ZERO_DELTA);
 247:     }
 248: 
 249:     function afterRemoveLiquidity(address, PoolKey calldata key, ModifyLiquidityParams calldata, BalanceDelta, BalanceDelta, bytes calldata)
 250:         external onlyPoolManager returns (bytes4, BalanceDelta)
 251:     {
 252:         _afterLiquidity(key);
 253:         return (IHooks.afterRemoveLiquidity.selector, BalanceDeltaLibrary.ZERO_DELTA);
 254:     }
 255: 
 256:     /// @notice Anyone can pay one fixed recipient. A broken recipient cannot block other buckets.
 257:     function sweep(Currency currency, uint8 bucket) external nonReentrant returns (uint256 amount) {
 258:         if (bucket > STABILITY) revert InvalidRecipient();
 259:         return _pay(currency, bucket, recipients[bucket]);
 260:     }
 261: 
 262:     /// @notice A recipient may collect its own bucket to another destination without changing rights.
 263:     function claim(Currency currency, uint8 bucket, address destination) external nonReentrant returns (uint256 amount) {
 264:         if (bucket > STABILITY || destination == address(0) || destination == address(this)) revert InvalidRecipient();
 265:         if (msg.sender != recipients[bucket]) revert NotRecipient();
 266:         return _pay(currency, bucket, destination);
 267:     }
 268: 
 269:     function _pay(Currency currency, uint8 bucket, address destination) private returns (uint256 amount) {
 270:         amount = owed[currency][bucket];
 271:         owed[currency][bucket] = 0;
 272:         totalOwed[currency] -= amount;
 273:         if (amount != 0) currency.transfer(destination, amount);
 274:         emit FeesPaid(currency, bucket, destination, amount);
 275:     }
 276: 
 277:     /// @notice Finalizes elapsed epochs even when the pool has been idle; bounded O(1) work.
 278:     function checkpoint() external {
 279:         if (initialized) _advance();
 280:     }
 281: 
 282:     function latestEpoch() external view returns (Epoch memory) { return _latest; }
 283:     function activeEpoch() external view returns (Epoch memory) { return _active; }
 284: 
 285:     function currentTickCumulative(PoolId id) external view returns (int56 cumulative, uint32 timestamp, bool ready) {
 286:         if (!initialized || PoolId.unwrap(id) != PoolId.unwrap(poolKey().toId())) return (0, 0, false);
 287:         int256 value = _tickCumulative + int256(lastTick) * int256(block.timestamp - lastTimestamp);
 288:         // Deliberate v3-compatible wrapping; differences valid for windows <2^32 seconds.
 289:         return (int56(value), uint32(block.timestamp), true);
 290:     }
 291: 
 292:     function _checkPool(PoolKey calldata key) private view {
 293:         if (Currency.unwrap(key.currency0) != address(0) || Currency.unwrap(key.currency1) != omr
 294:             || key.fee != poolFee || key.tickSpacing != tickSpacing || address(key.hooks) != address(this)) {
 295:             revert PoolNotAllowed();
 296:         }
 297:     }
 298: 
 299:     function _afterLiquidity(PoolKey calldata key) private {
 300:         _checkPool(key);
 301:         _advance();
 302:         _adoptPoolState(key.toId());
 303:     }
 304: 
 305:     function _adoptPoolState(PoolId id) private {
 306:         (, lastTick,,) = StateLibrary.getSlot0(poolManager, id);
 307:         lastLiquidity = StateLibrary.getLiquidity(poolManager, id);
 308:     }
 309: 
 310:     function _openingRate(uint256 actualQuote, bool exactOutput) private view returns (uint16) {
 311:         if (block.number >= openingEndsAtBlock) return 0;
 312:         // Check actual ETH input AFTER swap, so exact-output buys and partial fills obey the same cap.
 313:         // Reverting here atomically undoes the entire swap. No sell path enters this function.
 314:         uint256 grossQuote = actualQuote;
 315:         // Exact-output buys pay their opening fee in ETH; exact-input buys pay it in OMR output.
 316:         if (exactOutput) grossQuote += actualQuote * openingBuyBps / 10_000;
 317:         if (openingMaxBuyQuote != 0 && grossQuote > openingMaxBuyQuote) revert OpeningBuyTooLarge();
 318:         return openingBuyBps;
 319:     }
 320: 
 321:     /// @dev Progressive bounded tick-pressure toll. Buy reversals do not erase recent sell pressure.
 322:     ///      Pressure decays on a real clock, not by calls or blocks. This raises repeated split-sale
 323:     ///      costs but is not sandwich protection or a proof of order/splitting-independent pricing.
 324:     function _surgeRate() private returns (uint16 rate) {
 325:         uint256 elapsed = block.timestamp - pressureUpdatedAt;
 326:         uint256 pressure = elapsed >= PRESSURE_DECAY_SECONDS
 327:             ? 0 : uint256(sellPressureTicks) * (PRESSURE_DECAY_SECONDS - elapsed) / PRESSURE_DECAY_SECONDS;
 328:         bytes32 slot = PRE_TICK_SLOT;
 329:         int256 beforeTick;
 330:         assembly ("memory-safe") { beforeTick := signextend(2, tload(slot)) }
 331:         uint256 movement = int256(lastTick) > beforeTick ? uint256(int256(lastTick) - beforeTick) : 0;
 332:         uint256 end = pressure + movement;
 333:         uint256 limit = surgeFullTicks;
 334:         // Integrate min(pressure/limit,1) over the traversed tick interval rather than charging the
 335:         // last rate to the entire move. Weighted trade amounts still make exact partitioning nontrivial.
 336:         if (movement == 0) rate = uint16(pressure * MAX_SURGE_BPS / limit);
 337:         else if (end <= limit) rate = uint16((pressure + end) * MAX_SURGE_BPS / (2 * limit));
 338:         else {
 339:             uint256 below = limit - pressure;
 340:             rate = uint16(MAX_SURGE_BPS - below * below * MAX_SURGE_BPS / (2 * limit * movement));
 341:         }
 342:         sellPressureTicks = uint24(end > limit ? limit : end);
 343:         pressureUpdatedAt = uint64(block.timestamp);
 344:     }
 345: 
 346:     function _accrueBase(Currency currency, uint256 base) private {
 347:         if (base == 0) return;
 348:         uint256 dev = base * 200 / BASE_SELL_BPS;
 349:         uint256 rwa = base * 160 / BASE_SELL_BPS;
 350:         uint256 community = base * 240 / BASE_SELL_BPS;
 351:         owed[currency][DEV] += dev;
 352:         owed[currency][RWA] += rwa;
 353:         owed[currency][COMMUNITY] += community;
 354:         owed[currency][POL] += base - dev - rwa - community;
 355:     }
 356: 
 357:     function _abs(int128 value) private pure returns (uint256) {
 358:         return value < 0 ? uint256(-int256(value)) : uint256(int256(value));
 359:     }
 360: 
 361:     function _saturatingAdd(uint128 value, uint256 addition) private pure returns (uint128) {
 362:         uint256 sum = uint256(value) + addition;
 363:         return sum > type(uint128).max ? type(uint128).max : uint128(sum);
 364:     }
 365: 
 366:     function _newEpoch(uint64 id) private view returns (Epoch memory e) {
 367:         e.id = id;
 368:         e.end = (id + 1) * epochDuration;
 369:         e.minLiquidity = type(uint128).max;
 370:     }
 371: 
 372:     function _integrate(Epoch memory e, uint32 seconds_) private view returns (Epoch memory) {
 373:         if (seconds_ == 0) return e;
 374:         int256 tick = lastTick;
 375:         e.observedSeconds += seconds_;
 376:         e.tickSeconds += int128(tick * int256(uint256(seconds_)));
 377:         e.tickSquaredSeconds += uint128(uint256(tick * tick) * seconds_);
 378:         e.liquiditySeconds += uint256(lastLiquidity) * seconds_;
 379:         if (lastLiquidity < e.minLiquidity) e.minLiquidity = lastLiquidity;
 380:         return e;
 381:     }
 382: 
 383:     function _advance() private {
 384:         uint64 now_ = uint64(block.timestamp);
 385:         if (now_ == lastTimestamp) return;
 386:         _tickCumulative += int256(lastTick) * int256(uint256(now_ - lastTimestamp));
 387:         uint64 epoch = now_ / epochDuration;
 388:         if (epoch == _active.id) {
 389:             _active = _integrate(_active, uint32(now_ - lastTimestamp));
 390:         } else {
 391:             Epoch memory finished = _integrate(_active, uint32(_active.end - lastTimestamp));
 392:             // There were no pool mutations/checkpoints in skipped intervals: the cached tick and
 393:             // active L prevailed for every second. Synthesize the LAST complete quiet interval;
 394:             // never iterate over an arbitrarily long gap or copy ancient volume into fresh epochs.
 395:             if (epoch > finished.id + 1) finished = _integrate(_newEpoch(epoch - 1), epochDuration);
 396:             _latest = finished;
 397:             emit EpochFinalized(finished.id, finished.end, finished.observedSeconds);
 398:             _active = _integrate(_newEpoch(epoch), uint32(now_ - epoch * epochDuration));
 399:         }
 400:         lastTimestamp = now_;
 401:     }
 402: 
 403:     function beforeAddLiquidity(address, PoolKey calldata, ModifyLiquidityParams calldata, bytes calldata) external pure returns (bytes4) { revert HookNotImplemented(); }
 404:     function beforeRemoveLiquidity(address, PoolKey calldata, ModifyLiquidityParams calldata, bytes calldata) external pure returns (bytes4) { revert HookNotImplemented(); }
 405:     function beforeDonate(address, PoolKey calldata, uint256, uint256, bytes calldata) external pure returns (bytes4) { revert HookNotImplemented(); }
 406:     function afterDonate(address, PoolKey calldata, uint256, uint256, bytes calldata) external pure returns (bytes4) { revert HookNotImplemented(); }
 407: }
===== src/market-v2/OmertaMarketStateV2.sol =====
   1: // SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
   2: pragma solidity 0.8.26;
   3: 
   4: import {Math} from "@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/math/Math.sol";
   5: import {TickMath} from "v4-core/libraries/TickMath.sol";
   6: import {OmertaHookV2} from "./OmertaHookV2.sol";
   7: import {IOmertaMarketStateV2} from "./IOmertaMarketStateV2.sol";
   8: 
   9: /// @notice Permissionless finalized-epoch market measurements, deliberately outside swap settlement.
  10: /// @dev A snapshot summarizes a complete FIXED interval, never the current partially observed one.
  11: ///      Refresh spam cannot accelerate epoch progression. Time-weighted tick variance is price
  12: ///      dispersion, not a statistical forecast; volume imbalance can be wash traded and is only a
  13: ///      bounded risk signal. Raw active liquidity minimum is a prerequisite, not executable depth.
  14: contract OmertaMarketStateV2 is IOmertaMarketStateV2 {
  15:     OmertaHookV2 public immutable source;
  16:     uint128 public immutable requiredLiquidity;
  17:     uint64 public immutable maxAge;
  18:     uint24 public immutable fullStressTicks;
  19:     Snapshot private _snapshot;
  20: 
  21:     error InvalidConfiguration();
  22:     event Updated(uint64 indexed epoch, int24 meanTick, uint24 volatilityTicks, uint16 stressBps, bool valid);
  23: 
  24:     constructor(OmertaHookV2 source_, uint128 requiredLiquidity_, uint64 maxAge_, uint24 fullStressTicks_) {
  25:         if (address(source_) == address(0) || requiredLiquidity_ == 0 || fullStressTicks_ == 0
  26:             || fullStressTicks_ > 100_000 || maxAge_ < source_.epochDuration() || maxAge_ > 7 days) {
  27:             revert InvalidConfiguration();
  28:         }
  29:         source = source_;
  30:         requiredLiquidity = requiredLiquidity_;
  31:         maxAge = maxAge_;
  32:         fullStressTicks = fullStressTicks_;
  33:     }
  34: 
  35:     function refresh() external returns (Snapshot memory reading) {
  36:         source.checkpoint();
  37:         OmertaHookV2.Epoch memory e = source.latestEpoch();
  38:         if (e.end == 0 || (_snapshot.observedAt != 0 && e.id <= _snapshot.epoch)) return snapshot();
  39:         reading.epoch = e.id;
  40:         reading.observedAt = e.end;
  41:         reading.minLiquidity = e.minLiquidity;
  42:         if (e.observedSeconds == source.epochDuration() && e.minLiquidity >= requiredLiquidity
  43:             && e.end <= block.timestamp && block.timestamp - e.end <= maxAge) {
  44:             int256 denom = int256(uint256(e.observedSeconds));
  45:             int256 mean = int256(e.tickSeconds) / denom;
  46:             if (e.tickSeconds < 0 && int256(e.tickSeconds) % denom != 0) --mean;
  47:             reading.meanTick = int24(mean);
  48:             // E[(tick - floor(mean))^2] avoids a negative variance caused by signed mean rounding.
  49:             int256 varianceNumerator = int256(uint256(e.tickSquaredSeconds)) - 2 * mean * int256(e.tickSeconds)
  50:                 + mean * mean * denom;
  51:             reading.volatilityTicks = uint24(Math.sqrt(uint256(varianceNumerator / denom)));
  52:             uint256 sqrtPrice = TickMath.getSqrtPriceAtTick(reading.meanTick);
  53:             reading.omrPerEth = Math.mulDiv(sqrtPrice, sqrtPrice * 1e18, uint256(1) << 192);
  54:             uint256 volume = uint256(e.buyQuote) + e.sellQuote;
  55:             if (e.sellQuote > e.buyQuote && volume > 0) {
  56:                 // A single dust sell must not declare a crisis. Scale directional imbalance by
  57:                 // turnover against 1% of minimum virtual ETH depth at the mean price. This is a
  58:                 // bounded flow-intensity signal, not actual executable depth through price ranges.
  59:                 uint256 virtualQuote = Math.mulDiv(e.minLiquidity, uint256(1) << 96, sqrtPrice);
  60:                 uint256 meaningfulVolume = Math.max(virtualQuote / 100, 1);
  61:                 uint256 imbalance = (uint256(e.sellQuote) - e.buyQuote) * 10_000 / volume;
  62:                 reading.sellImbalanceBps = uint16(Math.mulDiv(imbalance, Math.min(volume, meaningfulVolume), meaningfulVolume));
  63:             }
  64:             uint256 volatilityStress = uint256(reading.volatilityTicks) * 10_000 / fullStressTicks;
  65:             uint256 stress = Math.max(volatilityStress, reading.sellImbalanceBps);
  66:             reading.stressBps = uint16(Math.min(stress, 10_000));
  67:             reading.valid = reading.omrPerEth > 0;
  68:         }
  69:         _snapshot = reading;
  70:         emit Updated(reading.epoch, reading.meanTick, reading.volatilityTicks, reading.stressBps, reading.valid);
  71:     }
  72: 
  73:     function snapshot() public view returns (Snapshot memory reading) {
  74:         reading = _snapshot;
  75:         if (reading.observedAt == 0 || reading.observedAt > block.timestamp
  76:             || block.timestamp - reading.observedAt > maxAge) reading.valid = false;
  77:     }
  78: }
===== src/market-v2/OmertaStabilityControllerV2.sol =====
   1: // SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT

   3: 
   4: import {IERC20} from "@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/IERC20.sol";

   7: import {IPoolManager} from "v4-core/interfaces/IPoolManager.sol";
   8: import {IUnlockCallback} from "v4-core/interfaces/callback/IUnlockCallback.sol";
   9: import {PoolKey} from "v4-core/types/PoolKey.sol";
  10: import {PoolId, PoolIdLibrary} from "v4-core/types/PoolId.sol";

  18: import {LiquidityAmounts} from "../../lib/v4-periphery/src/libraries/LiquidityAmounts.sol";
  19: import {IOmertaMarketStateV2} from "./IOmertaMarketStateV2.sol";
  20: 
  21: /// @notice Funded, compartmentalized range liquidity under immutable execution limits.
  22: /// @dev Native ETH is currency0 and OMR is currency1: DOWN in OMR/ETH economic value is
  23: ///      UP in raw v4 ticks. Positions remain reversible inventory until actually removed.
  24: ///      This contract neither promises a floor nor mints OMR. Safe can pause and retire
  25: ///      capital to itself, but cannot change pool, recipients, limits, or issue arbitrary calls.
  26: contract OmertaStabilityControllerV2 is ReentrancyGuard, IUnlockCallback {
  27:     using SafeERC20 for IERC20;
  28:     using PoolIdLibrary for PoolKey;
  29:     using StateLibrary for IPoolManager;
  30: 
  31:     enum Tranche {
  32:         Core,
  33:         LowerCushion,
  34:         Garrison,
  35:         UpperCushion,
  36:         Desk,
  37:         WarChest,
  38:         Turf
  39:     }
  40:     enum Operation {
  41:         Add,
  42:         Collect,
  43:         Exit
  44:     }
  45: 
  46:     struct Limits {
  47:         uint128 nativePerAction;
  48:         uint128 omrPerAction;
  49:         uint128 nativePerEpisode;
  50:         uint128 omrPerEpisode;
  51:         uint128 nativeLifetime;
  52:         uint128 omrLifetime;
  53:         uint128 nativeRegeneration;
  54:         uint128 omrRegeneration;
  55:     }
  56: 
  57:     struct Config {
  58:         address safe;
  59:         IPoolManager manager;
  60:         PoolKey key;
  61:         IOmertaMarketStateV2 marketState;
  62:         address[7] feeRecipients;
  63:         Limits[7] limits;
  64:         uint32 maxObservationAge;
  65:         uint32 cooldown;
  66:         uint32 recoveryInterval;
  67:         uint128 minLiquidity;
  68:         uint24 maxSpotDeviationTicks;
  69:         uint24 minBandTicks;
  70:         uint24 maxBandTicks;
  71:         uint16 stressOnBps;
  72:         uint16 stressOffBps;
  73:         uint8 recoveryX;
  74:         uint8 recoveryY;
  75:         int24 turfLower;
  76:         int24 turfUpper;
  77:         uint64 turfSeasonStart;
  78:         uint64 turfSeasonEnd;
  79:     }
  80: 
  81:     struct Account {
  82:         uint256 idleNative;
  83:         uint256 idleOmr;
  84:         uint256 nativeFees;
  85:         uint256 omrFees;
  86:         uint256 nativeDeployed;
  87:         uint256 omrDeployed;
  88:         uint256 nativeReturned;
  89:         uint256 omrReturned;
  90:         uint128 nativeCapacity;
  91:         uint128 omrCapacity;
  92:         uint64 lastDeploymentEpoch;
  93:         uint64 lastDeploymentAt;
  94:         uint64 lastRecoveryEpoch;
  95:         uint64 lastRecoveryAt;
  96:         uint32 recoveryVotes;
  97:         uint8 recoverySamples;
  98:     }
  99: 
 100:     struct Position {
 101:         int24 lower;
 102:         int24 upper;
 103:         uint128 liquidity;
 104:         uint64 nonce;
 105:         uint64 deployedBlock;
 106:     }
 107: 
 108:     error BadConfiguration();
 109:     error Unauthorized();
 110:     error Paused();
 111:     error InvalidObservation();
 112:     error InsufficientDepth();
 113:     error SpotDeviation();
 114:     error InvalidTranche();
 115:     error ActivePosition();
 116:     error NoPosition();
 117:     error BudgetExhausted();
 118:     error Cooldown();
 119:     error WrongRegime();
 120:     error RecoveryNotReady();
 121:     error BadSettlement();
 122:     error BadCallback();

 124: 
 125:     event Funded(Tranche indexed tranche, address indexed donor, uint256 nativeAmount, uint256 omrAmount);

 127:     event PrincipalMoved(Tranche indexed tranche, bool deployed, uint256 nativeAmount, uint256 omrAmount);
 128:     event FeesCollected(Tranche indexed tranche, uint256 nativeAmount, uint256 omrAmount);

 130:     event Exited(Tranche indexed tranche, uint64 nonce);
 131:     event RecoveryObserved(Tranche indexed tranche, uint64 indexed epoch, bool recovered);
 132:     event Regenerated(Tranche indexed tranche, uint256 nativeAmount, uint256 omrAmount);

 134:     event PauseSet(bool paused);
 135:     event StressLatched(bool stressed, uint64 indexed epoch);

 137:     address public immutable safe;
 138:     IPoolManager public immutable manager;
 139:     IOmertaMarketStateV2 public immutable marketState;
 140:     IERC20 public immutable omr;
 141:     PoolId public immutable poolId;
 142:     uint32 public immutable maxObservationAge;
 143:     uint32 public immutable cooldown;
 144:     uint32 public immutable recoveryInterval;
 145:     uint128 public immutable minLiquidity;
 146:     uint24 public immutable maxSpotDeviationTicks;
 147:     uint24 public immutable minBandTicks;
 148:     uint24 public immutable maxBandTicks;
 149:     uint16 public immutable stressOnBps;
 150:     uint16 public immutable stressOffBps;
 151:     uint8 public immutable recoveryX;
 152:     uint8 public immutable recoveryY;
 153:     int24 public immutable turfLower;
 154:     int24 public immutable turfUpper;
 155:     uint64 public immutable turfSeasonStart;
 156:     uint64 public immutable turfSeasonEnd;
 157:     PoolKey private _key;
 158:     address[7] public feeRecipients;
 159:     Limits[7] private _limits;
 160:     Account[7] private _accounts;
 161:     Position[7] private _positions;
 162:     bool public paused;
 163:     bool public stressed;
 164:     uint64 public lastRegimeEpoch;
 165:     uint64 public lastRegimeAt;
 166:     uint256 public totalIdleNative;
 167:     uint256 public totalIdleOmr;
 168:     uint256 public totalNativeFees;
 169:     uint256 public totalOmrFees;
 170:     uint256[7] public claimedNativeFees;
 171:     uint256[7] public claimedOmrFees;
 172:     bytes32 private _callbackHash;
 173: 
 174:     constructor(Config memory c) {
 175:         // LP return deltas would let another hook redefine the principal/fee split.
 176:         uint160 prohibited =
 177:             Hooks.AFTER_ADD_LIQUIDITY_RETURNS_DELTA_FLAG | Hooks.AFTER_REMOVE_LIQUIDITY_RETURNS_DELTA_FLAG;
 178:         if (
 179:             c.safe == address(0) || address(c.manager).code.length == 0 || address(c.marketState).code.length == 0
 180:                 || Currency.unwrap(c.key.currency0) != address(0) || Currency.unwrap(c.key.currency1).code.length == 0
 181:                 || address(c.key.hooks).code.length == 0 || uint160(address(c.key.hooks)) & prohibited != 0
 182:                 || c.key.tickSpacing <= 0 || c.key.tickSpacing > 200 || c.maxObservationAge == 0
 183:                 || c.maxObservationAge > 1 days || c.cooldown < 1 minutes || c.recoveryInterval < 1 minutes
 184:                 || c.minLiquidity == 0 || c.maxSpotDeviationTicks == 0 || c.maxSpotDeviationTicks > 2000
 185:                 || c.minBandTicks < uint24(c.key.tickSpacing) || c.maxBandTicks < c.minBandTicks
 186:                 || c.maxBandTicks > 10000 || c.stressOnBps > 10000 || c.stressOnBps <= c.stressOffBps
 187:                 || c.recoveryX == 0 || c.recoveryX > c.recoveryY || c.recoveryY > 32
 188:         ) revert BadConfiguration();
 189:         if (
 190:             c.turfLower < TickMath.MIN_TICK || c.turfUpper > TickMath.MAX_TICK || c.turfLower >= c.turfUpper
 191:                 || c.turfLower % c.key.tickSpacing != 0 || c.turfUpper % c.key.tickSpacing != 0
 192:                 || c.turfSeasonStart == 0 || c.turfSeasonEnd <= c.turfSeasonStart
 193:                 || c.turfSeasonEnd - c.turfSeasonStart > 366 days
 194:         ) revert BadConfiguration();
 195:         safe = c.safe;
 196:         manager = c.manager;
 197:         marketState = c.marketState;
 198:         omr = IERC20(Currency.unwrap(c.key.currency1));
 199:         _key = c.key;
 200:         poolId = c.key.toId();
 201:         maxObservationAge = c.maxObservationAge;
 202:         cooldown = c.cooldown;
 203:         recoveryInterval = c.recoveryInterval;
 204:         minLiquidity = c.minLiquidity;
 205:         maxSpotDeviationTicks = c.maxSpotDeviationTicks;
 206:         minBandTicks = c.minBandTicks;
 207:         maxBandTicks = c.maxBandTicks;
 208:         stressOnBps = c.stressOnBps;
 209:         stressOffBps = c.stressOffBps;
 210:         recoveryX = c.recoveryX;
 211:         recoveryY = c.recoveryY;
 212:         turfLower = c.turfLower;
 213:         turfUpper = c.turfUpper;
 214:         turfSeasonStart = c.turfSeasonStart;
 215:         turfSeasonEnd = c.turfSeasonEnd;
 216:         for (uint256 i; i < 7; ++i) {
 217:             Limits memory l = c.limits[i];
 218:             if (
 219:                 c.feeRecipients[i] == address(0) || c.feeRecipients[i] == address(this)
 220:                     || l.nativePerAction > l.nativePerEpisode || l.omrPerAction > l.omrPerEpisode
 221:                     || l.nativePerEpisode > l.nativeLifetime || l.omrPerEpisode > l.omrLifetime
 222:                     || l.nativeRegeneration > l.nativePerEpisode || l.omrRegeneration > l.omrPerEpisode
 223:             ) revert BadConfiguration();
 224:             feeRecipients[i] = c.feeRecipients[i];
 225:             _limits[i] = l;
 226:             _accounts[i].nativeCapacity = l.nativePerEpisode;
 227:             _accounts[i].omrCapacity = l.omrPerEpisode;
 228:         }
 229:     }
 230: 
 231:     /// @dev Only actual PoolManager withdrawals arrive through receive. Donations use fund.
 232:     receive() external payable {
 233:         if (msg.sender != address(manager)) revert Unauthorized();
 234:     }
 235: 
 236:     function poolKey() external view returns (PoolKey memory) {
 237:         return _key;
 238:     }
 239: 
 240:     function limits(Tranche t) external view returns (Limits memory) {
 241:         return _limits[uint256(t)];
 242:     }
 243: 
 244:     function account(Tranche t) external view returns (Account memory) {
 245:         return _accounts[uint256(t)];
 246:     }
 247: 
 248:     function position(Tranche t) external view returns (Position memory) {
 249:         return _positions[uint256(t)];
 250:     }
 251: 
 252:     /// @notice Deposits are gifts to this immutable compartment, not withdrawable LP shares.
 253:     /// Funding adds assets; it never resets deployment capacity or lifetime spend.
 254:     function fund(Tranche t, uint256 omrAmount) external payable nonReentrant {
 255:         if (msg.value == 0 && omrAmount == 0) revert BudgetExhausted();
 256:         uint256 beforeOmr = omr.balanceOf(address(this));
 257:         if (omrAmount != 0) omr.safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), omrAmount);
 258:         if (omr.balanceOf(address(this)) - beforeOmr != omrAmount) revert BadSettlement();
 259:         Account storage a = _accounts[uint256(t)];
 260:         a.idleNative += msg.value;
 261:         a.idleOmr += omrAmount;
 262:         totalIdleNative += msg.value;
 263:         totalIdleOmr += omrAmount;
 264:         emit Funded(t, msg.sender, msg.value, omrAmount);
 265:     }
 266: 
 267:     function setPaused(bool value) external {
 268:         if (msg.sender != safe) revert Unauthorized();
 269:         paused = value;
 270:         emit PauseSet(value);
 271:     }
 272: 
 273:     /// @notice Permissionless deterministic placement; caller chooses neither price nor amounts.
 274:     /// Every placement consumes immutable per-action, per-observation, episode and lifetime limits.
 275:     function deploy(Tranche t, uint64 expectedEpoch) external nonReentrant {
 276:         if (paused) revert Paused();
 277:         if (t == Tranche.WarChest) revert InvalidTranche();
 278:         if (t == Tranche.Turf && (block.timestamp < turfSeasonStart || block.timestamp >= turfSeasonEnd)) {
 279:             revert WrongRegime();
 280:         }
 281:         IOmertaMarketStateV2.Snapshot memory s = _observation(expectedEpoch);
 282:         _updateRegime(s);
 283:         if (_downside(t) && !stressed) revert WrongRegime();
 284:         if (_upside(t) && stressed) revert WrongRegime();
 285:         Account storage a = _accounts[uint256(t)];
 286:         Position storage p = _positions[uint256(t)];
 287:         if (p.liquidity != 0) revert ActivePosition();
 288:         if (s.epoch <= a.lastDeploymentEpoch || block.timestamp < uint256(a.lastDeploymentAt) + cooldown) {
 289:             revert Cooldown();
 290:         }
 291:         (uint160 sqrtPrice,,,) = manager.getSlot0(poolId);
 292:         (p.lower, p.upper) = _range(t, s);
 293:         if (
 294:             (_downside(t) && sqrtPrice > TickMath.getSqrtPriceAtTick(p.lower))
 295:                 || (_upside(t) && sqrtPrice < TickMath.getSqrtPriceAtTick(p.upper))
 296:         ) revert SpotDeviation();
 297:         Limits storage l = _limits[uint256(t)];
 298:         uint256 budget0 =
 299:             _min(_min(a.idleNative, l.nativePerAction), _min(a.nativeCapacity, l.nativeLifetime - a.nativeDeployed));
 300:         uint256 budget1 = _min(_min(a.idleOmr, l.omrPerAction), _min(a.omrCapacity, l.omrLifetime - a.omrDeployed));
 301:         uint128 liquidity = LiquidityAmounts.getLiquidityForAmounts(
 302:             sqrtPrice, TickMath.getSqrtPriceAtTick(p.lower), TickMath.getSqrtPriceAtTick(p.upper), budget0, budget1
 303:         );
 304:         if (liquidity == 0 || liquidity > uint128(type(int128).max)) revert BudgetExhausted();
 305:         ++p.nonce;
 306:         p.deployedBlock = uint64(block.number);
 307:         a.lastDeploymentEpoch = s.epoch;
 308:         a.lastDeploymentAt = uint64(block.timestamp);
 309:         _modify(t, Operation.Add, liquidity, budget0, budget1);
 310:         p.liquidity = liquidity;
 311:         emit Deployed(t, s.epoch, p.lower, p.upper, liquidity);
 312:     }
 313: 
 314:     /// @notice Fee collection never needs an oracle, a keeper, or an unpaused strategy.
 315:     function collect(Tranche t) external nonReentrant {
 316:         if (_positions[uint256(t)].liquidity == 0) revert NoPosition();
 317:         _modify(t, Operation.Collect, 0, 0, 0);
 318:     }
 319: 
 320:     /// @notice Owner can always remove inventory, including during an oracle outage.
 321:     function exit(Tranche t) external nonReentrant {
 322:         if (msg.sender != safe) revert Unauthorized();
 323:         _exit(t);
 324:     }
 325: 
 326:     /// @notice Season expiry can be settled without governance, strategy readiness, or an oracle.
 327:     /// Principal stays in Turf custody; earned fees remain owed to its immutable beneficiary.
 328:     function expireTurf() external nonReentrant {
 329:         if (block.timestamp < turfSeasonEnd) revert WrongRegime();
 330:         if (_positions[uint256(Tranche.Turf)].liquidity != 0) _exit(Tranche.Turf);
 331:     }
 332: 
 333:     /// @notice Anyone can settle a band that has fully converted in its intended direction.
 334:     /// This is an actual withdrawal: reversing price before this transaction reverses inventory.
 335:     function finalize(Tranche t) external nonReentrant {
 336:         Position storage p = _positions[uint256(t)];
 337:         (uint160 spot,,,) = manager.getSlot0(poolId);
 338:         if (p.liquidity == 0 || block.number <= p.deployedBlock) revert NoPosition();
 339:         if (!((_downside(t) && spot >= TickMath.getSqrtPriceAtTick(p.upper))
 340:                     || (_upside(t) && spot <= TickMath.getSqrtPriceAtTick(p.lower)))) revert WrongRegime();
 341:         _exit(t);
 342:     }
 343: 
 344:     function _exit(Tranche t) private {
 345:         Position storage p = _positions[uint256(t)];
 346:         if (p.liquidity == 0) revert NoPosition();
 347:         uint128 liquidity = p.liquidity;
 348:         p.liquidity = 0;
 349:         _modify(t, Operation.Exit, liquidity, 0, 0);
 350:         emit Exited(t, p.nonce);
 351:     }
 352: 
 353:     /// @notice Transferred only to the constructor-pinned beneficiary, independent of capital.
 354:     function claimFees(Tranche t) external nonReentrant {
 355:         Account storage a = _accounts[uint256(t)];
 356:         uint256 amount0 = a.nativeFees;
 357:         uint256 amount1 = a.omrFees;
 358:         a.nativeFees = 0;
 359:         a.omrFees = 0;
 360:         totalNativeFees -= amount0;
 361:         totalOmrFees -= amount1;
 362:         claimedNativeFees[uint256(t)] += amount0;
 363:         claimedOmrFees[uint256(t)] += amount1;
 364:         _send(feeRecipients[uint256(t)], amount0, amount1);
 365:         emit FeesClaimed(t, feeRecipients[uint256(t)], amount0, amount1);
 366:     }
 367: 
 368:     /// @notice Records at most one fresh observation per spaced interval, never repeated epochs.
 369:     /// Recovery requires the entire Y-sample window to have been observed, and X healthy samples.
 370:     function observeRecovery(Tranche t, uint64 expectedEpoch) external nonReentrant {
 371:         if (!_downside(t) && !_upside(t)) revert InvalidTranche();
 372:         IOmertaMarketStateV2.Snapshot memory s = _observation(expectedEpoch);
 373:         Account storage a = _accounts[uint256(t)];
 374:         if (
 375:             s.epoch <= a.lastRecoveryEpoch || s.observedAt <= a.lastRecoveryAt
 376:                 || s.observedAt < uint256(a.lastRecoveryAt) + recoveryInterval
 377:         ) revert Cooldown();
 378:         bool recovered = s.stressBps <= stressOffBps && s.sellImbalanceBps <= stressOffBps;
 379:         // Gaps do not count as unobserved healthy samples and cannot stitch distant windows.
 380:         if (a.lastRecoveryAt != 0 && s.observedAt > uint256(a.lastRecoveryAt) + uint256(recoveryInterval) * 2) {
 381:             a.recoveryVotes = 0;
 382:             a.recoverySamples = 0;
 383:         }
 384:         uint32 mask = recoveryY == 32 ? type(uint32).max : uint32((uint256(1) << recoveryY) - 1);
 385:         a.recoveryVotes = ((a.recoveryVotes << 1) | (recovered ? 1 : 0)) & mask;
 386:         if (a.recoverySamples < recoveryY) ++a.recoverySamples;
 387:         a.lastRecoveryEpoch = s.epoch;
 388:         a.lastRecoveryAt = s.observedAt;
 389:         _updateRegime(s);
 390:         emit RecoveryObserved(t, s.epoch, recovered);
 391:     }
 392: 
 393:     /// @notice Partial regeneration consumes actual idle WarChest funds and the whole recovery
 394:     /// window. It never increases lifetime limits or treats returned inventory as fresh capacity.
 395:     function regenerate(Tranche t, uint64 expectedEpoch) external nonReentrant {
 396:         if (paused) revert Paused();
 397:         if (!_downside(t) && !_upside(t)) revert InvalidTranche();
 398:         IOmertaMarketStateV2.Snapshot memory s = _observation(expectedEpoch);
 399:         Account storage a = _accounts[uint256(t)];
 400:         if (
 401:             s.epoch != a.lastRecoveryEpoch || s.stressBps > stressOffBps || s.sellImbalanceBps > stressOffBps
 402:                 || a.recoverySamples < recoveryY || _popcount(a.recoveryVotes) < recoveryX
 403:                 || _positions[uint256(t)].liquidity != 0
 404:         ) revert RecoveryNotReady();
 405:         Limits storage l = _limits[uint256(t)];
 406:         Account storage reserve = _accounts[uint256(Tranche.WarChest)];
 407:         uint256 amount0 = _min(
 408:             _min(reserve.idleNative, l.nativeRegeneration),
 409:             _min(l.nativePerEpisode - a.nativeCapacity, l.nativeLifetime - a.nativeDeployed)
 410:         );
 411:         uint256 amount1 = _min(
 412:             _min(reserve.idleOmr, l.omrRegeneration),
 413:             _min(l.omrPerEpisode - a.omrCapacity, l.omrLifetime - a.omrDeployed)
 414:         );
 415:         if (amount0 == 0 && amount1 == 0) revert BudgetExhausted();
 416:         reserve.idleNative -= amount0;
 417:         reserve.idleOmr -= amount1;
 418:         a.idleNative += amount0;
 419:         a.idleOmr += amount1;
 420:         a.nativeCapacity += uint128(amount0);
 421:         a.omrCapacity += uint128(amount1);
 422:         a.recoveryVotes = 0;
 423:         a.recoverySamples = 0;
 424:         emit Regenerated(t, amount0, amount1);
 425:     }
 426: 
 427:     /// @notice Explicit governance retirement to the immutable Safe, without touching fee debts.
 428:     function retire(Tranche t, uint256 nativeAmount, uint256 omrAmount) external nonReentrant {
 429:         if (msg.sender != safe) revert Unauthorized();
 430:         Account storage a = _accounts[uint256(t)];
 431:         a.idleNative -= nativeAmount;
 432:         a.idleOmr -= omrAmount;
 433:         totalIdleNative -= nativeAmount;
 434:         totalIdleOmr -= omrAmount;
 435:         _send(safe, nativeAmount, omrAmount);
 436:         emit Retired(t, nativeAmount, omrAmount);
 437:     }
 438: 
 439:     /// @notice Mark-to-inventory token amounts at the actual pool price, excluding uncollected fees.
 440:     function positionInventory(Tranche t) external view returns (uint256 nativeAmount, uint256 omrAmount) {
 441:         Position memory p = _positions[uint256(t)];
 442:         if (p.liquidity == 0) return (0, 0);
 443:         (uint160 spot,,,) = manager.getSlot0(poolId);
 444:         uint160 lower = TickMath.getSqrtPriceAtTick(p.lower);
 445:         uint160 upper = TickMath.getSqrtPriceAtTick(p.upper);
 446:         if (spot < upper) {
 447:             nativeAmount = SqrtPriceMath.getAmount0Delta(spot > lower ? spot : lower, upper, p.liquidity, false);
 448:         }
 449:         if (spot > lower) {
 450:             omrAmount = SqrtPriceMath.getAmount1Delta(lower, spot < upper ? spot : upper, p.liquidity, false);
 451:         }
 452:     }
 453: 
 454:     function previewRange(Tranche t, uint64 expectedEpoch) external view returns (int24 lower, int24 upper) {
 455:         if (t == Tranche.WarChest) revert InvalidTranche();
 456:         return _range(t, _observation(expectedEpoch));
 457:     }
 458: 
 459:     function _observation(uint64 epoch) private view returns (IOmertaMarketStateV2.Snapshot memory s) {
 460:         s = marketState.snapshot();
 461:         if (
 462:             !s.valid || s.epoch == 0 || s.epoch != epoch || s.observedAt == 0 || s.observedAt > block.timestamp
 463:                 || block.timestamp - s.observedAt > maxObservationAge || s.omrPerEth == 0 || s.stressBps > 10000
 464:                 || s.sellImbalanceBps > 10000
 465:         ) revert InvalidObservation();
 466:         if (s.minLiquidity < minLiquidity || manager.getLiquidity(poolId) < minLiquidity) revert InsufficientDepth();
 467:         (uint160 spot, int24 tick,,) = manager.getSlot0(poolId);
 468:         if (spot == 0) revert InvalidObservation();
 469:         int256 difference = int256(tick) - s.meanTick;
 470:         if (difference < 0) difference = -difference;
 471:         if (uint256(difference) > maxSpotDeviationTicks) revert SpotDeviation();
 472:     }
 473: 
 474:     function _updateRegime(IOmertaMarketStateV2.Snapshot memory s) private {
 475:         if (s.epoch < lastRegimeEpoch || s.observedAt < lastRegimeAt) revert InvalidObservation();
 476:         if (s.epoch == lastRegimeEpoch) return;
 477:         lastRegimeEpoch = s.epoch;
 478:         lastRegimeAt = s.observedAt;
 479:         bool next = stressed ? s.stressBps > stressOffBps : s.stressBps >= stressOnBps;
 480:         if (next != stressed) {
 481:             stressed = next;
 482:             emit StressLatched(next, s.epoch);
 483:         }
 484:     }
 485: 
 486:     function _range(Tranche t, IOmertaMarketStateV2.Snapshot memory s) private view returns (int24 lower, int24 upper) {
 487:         if (t == Tranche.Turf) return (turfLower, turfUpper);
 488:         int256 spacing = _key.tickSpacing;
 489:         int256 anchor = int256(s.meanTick) / spacing;
 490:         if (s.meanTick < 0 && int256(s.meanTick) % spacing != 0) --anchor;
 491:         anchor *= spacing;
 492:         uint256 width = uint256(s.volatilityTicks) * 2;
 493:         width = width < minBandTicks ? minBandTicks : (width > maxBandTicks ? maxBandTicks : width);
 494:         int256 step = int256((width + uint256(spacing) - 1) / uint256(spacing)) * spacing;
 495:         int256 lo;
 496:         int256 hi;
 497:         if (t == Tranche.LowerCushion) {
 498:             lo = anchor + step;
 499:             hi = anchor + step * 2;
 500:         } else if (t == Tranche.Garrison) {
 501:             lo = anchor + step * 2;
 502:             hi = anchor + step * 3;
 503:         } else if (t == Tranche.UpperCushion) {
 504:             lo = anchor - step * 2;
 505:             hi = anchor - step;
 506:         } else if (t == Tranche.Desk) {
 507:             lo = anchor - step * 3;
 508:             hi = anchor - step * 2;
 509:         } else if (t == Tranche.Core) {
 510:             lo = anchor - step * 4;
 511:             hi = anchor + step * 4;
 512:         } else {
 513:             lo = anchor - step;
 514:             hi = anchor + step;
 515:         }
 516:         if (lo < TickMath.MIN_TICK || hi > TickMath.MAX_TICK || lo >= hi) revert InvalidObservation();
 517:         return (int24(lo), int24(hi));
 518:     }
 519: 
 520:     function _modify(Tranche t, Operation op, uint128 liquidity, uint256 budget0, uint256 budget1) private {
 521:         bytes memory data = abi.encode(t, op, liquidity, budget0, budget1);
 522:         _callbackHash = keccak256(data);
 523:         manager.unlock(data);
