 524:         if (_callbackHash != bytes32(0)) revert BadCallback();
 525:     }
 526: 
 527:     function unlockCallback(bytes calldata data) external returns (bytes memory) {
 528:         if (msg.sender != address(manager) || _callbackHash == bytes32(0) || keccak256(data) != _callbackHash) {
 529:             revert BadCallback();
 530:         }
 531:         _callbackHash = bytes32(0);
 532:         (Tranche t, Operation op, uint128 liquidity, uint256 budget0, uint256 budget1) =
 533:             abi.decode(data, (Tranche, Operation, uint128, uint256, uint256));
 534:         Position storage p = _positions[uint256(t)];
 535:         int256 change = op == Operation.Add
 536:             ? int256(uint256(liquidity))
 537:             : op == Operation.Exit ? -int256(uint256(liquidity)) : int256(0);
 538:         (BalanceDelta delta, BalanceDelta fees) = manager.modifyLiquidity(
 539:             _key, ModifyLiquidityParams(p.lower, p.upper, change, keccak256(abi.encode(t, p.nonce))), ""
 540:         );
 541:         int256 principal0 = int256(delta.amount0()) - fees.amount0();
 542:         int256 principal1 = int256(delta.amount1()) - fees.amount1();
 543:         if (fees.amount0() < 0 || fees.amount1() < 0) revert BadSettlement();
 544:         Account storage a = _accounts[uint256(t)];
 545:         if (op == Operation.Add) {
 546:             if (principal0 > 0 || principal1 > 0 || uint256(-principal0) > budget0 || uint256(-principal1) > budget1) {
 547:                 revert BadSettlement();
 548:             }
 549:             uint256 amount0 = uint256(-principal0);
 550:             uint256 amount1 = uint256(-principal1);
 551:             a.idleNative -= amount0;
 552:             a.idleOmr -= amount1;
 553:             totalIdleNative -= amount0;
 554:             totalIdleOmr -= amount1;
 555:             a.nativeCapacity -= uint128(amount0);
 556:             a.omrCapacity -= uint128(amount1);
 557:             a.nativeDeployed += amount0;
 558:             a.omrDeployed += amount1;
 559:             emit PrincipalMoved(t, true, amount0, amount1);
 560:         } else {
 561:             if (principal0 < 0 || principal1 < 0 || (op == Operation.Collect && (principal0 != 0 || principal1 != 0))) {
 562:                 revert BadSettlement();
 563:             }
 564:             a.idleNative += uint256(principal0);
 565:             a.idleOmr += uint256(principal1);
 566:             totalIdleNative += uint256(principal0);
 567:             totalIdleOmr += uint256(principal1);
 568:             a.nativeReturned += uint256(principal0);
 569:             a.omrReturned += uint256(principal1);
 570:             if (op == Operation.Exit) emit PrincipalMoved(t, false, uint256(principal0), uint256(principal1));
 571:         }
 572:         uint256 fee0 = uint256(uint128(fees.amount0()));
 573:         uint256 fee1 = uint256(uint128(fees.amount1()));
 574:         a.nativeFees += fee0;
 575:         a.omrFees += fee1;
 576:         totalNativeFees += fee0;
 577:         totalOmrFees += fee1;
 578:         if (fee0 != 0 || fee1 != 0) emit FeesCollected(t, fee0, fee1);
 579:         _settle(_key.currency0, delta.amount0());
 580:         _settle(_key.currency1, delta.amount1());
 581:         return "";
 582:     }
 583: 
 584:     function _settle(Currency currency, int128 delta) private {
 585:         if (delta > 0) {
 586:             manager.take(currency, address(this), uint256(uint128(delta)));
 587:         } else if (delta < 0) {
 588:             uint256 amount = uint256(-int256(delta));
 589:             if (Currency.unwrap(currency) == address(0)) {
 590:                 // A previous ERC20 sync can persist through the unlock. Explicitly select ETH.
 591:                 manager.sync(currency);
 592:                 if (manager.settle{value: amount}() != amount) revert BadSettlement();
 593:             } else {
 594:                 manager.sync(currency);
 595:                 omr.safeTransfer(address(manager), amount);
 596:                 if (manager.settle() != amount) revert BadSettlement();
 597:             }
 598:         }
 599:     }
 600: 
 601:     function _send(address to, uint256 amount0, uint256 amount1) private {
 602:         if (amount0 != 0) {
 603:             (bool ok,) = to.call{value: amount0}("");
 604:             if (!ok) revert TransferFailed();
 605:         }
 606:         if (amount1 != 0) omr.safeTransfer(to, amount1);
 607:     }
 608: 
 609:     function _downside(Tranche t) private pure returns (bool) {
 610:         return t == Tranche.LowerCushion || t == Tranche.Garrison;
 611:     }
 612: 
 613:     function _upside(Tranche t) private pure returns (bool) {
 614:         return t == Tranche.UpperCushion || t == Tranche.Desk;
 615:     }
 616: 
 617:     function _min(uint256 a, uint256 b) private pure returns (uint256) {
 618:         return a < b ? a : b;
 619:     }
 620: 
 621:     function _popcount(uint32 n) private pure returns (uint256 count) {
 622:         while (n != 0) {
 623:             n &= n - 1;
 624:             ++count;
 625:         }
 626:     }
 627: }
===== src/market-v2/OmertaInventoryBondV2.sol =====
   1: // SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
   2: pragma solidity 0.8.26;
   3: 
   4: import {IERC20} from "@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/IERC20.sol";
   5: import {SafeERC20} from "@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/utils/SafeERC20.sol";
   6: import {ReentrancyGuard} from "@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/ReentrancyGuard.sol";
   7: import {Math} from "@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/math/Math.sol";
   8: import {IPoolManager} from "v4-core/interfaces/IPoolManager.sol";
   9: import {PoolKey} from "v4-core/types/PoolKey.sol";
  10: import {PoolId, PoolIdLibrary} from "v4-core/types/PoolId.sol";
  11: import {Currency} from "v4-core/types/Currency.sol";
  12: import {StateLibrary} from "v4-core/libraries/StateLibrary.sol";
  13: import {IOmertaMarketStateV2} from "./IOmertaMarketStateV2.sol";
  14: 
  15: /// @notice Finite treasury-inventory bonds for large ETH purchases of linearly vested OMR.
  16: /// @dev There is no mint authority, firm market bid, sellback, callable adapter, or guaranteed
  17: ///      redemption value. OMR is reserved from funded inventory at purchase; native proceeds
  18: ///      are separately owed to the immutable reserve recipient. Buys do not replace the hook's
  19: ///      canonical sell path or exempt a later holder's market sale from its existing funding tax.
  20: contract OmertaInventoryBondV2 is ReentrancyGuard {
  21:     using SafeERC20 for IERC20;
  22:     using PoolIdLibrary for PoolKey;
  23:     using StateLibrary for IPoolManager;
  24: 
  25:     struct Config {
  26:         address safe;
  27:         address proceedsRecipient;
  28:         IPoolManager manager;
  29:         PoolKey key;
  30:         IOmertaMarketStateV2 marketState;
  31:         uint32 maxObservationAge;
  32:         uint32 vestingSeconds;
  33:         uint128 minLiquidity;
  34:         uint24 maxSpotDeviationTicks;
  35:         uint16 discountBps;
  36:         uint128 minNativePurchase;
  37:         uint128 maxNativePurchase;
  38:         uint128 maxNativePerEpoch;
  39:         uint128 maxNativeLifetime;
  40:         uint128 maxOmrPerPurchase;
  41:         uint128 maxOmrPerEpoch;
  42:         uint128 maxOmrLifetime;
  43:         uint256 maxOmrPerEth;
  44:     }
  45: 
  46:     struct Note {
  47:         address beneficiary;
  48:         uint64 start;
  49:         uint64 end;
  50:         uint128 amount;
  51:         uint128 claimed;
  52:     }
  53: 
  54:     error BadConfiguration();
  55:     error Unauthorized();
  56:     error Paused();
  57:     error InvalidQuote();
  58:     error InvalidObservation();
  59:     error BudgetExceeded();
  60:     error InsufficientInventory();
  61:     error Replay();
  62:     error NoClaim();
  63:     error TransferFailed();
  64: 
  65:     event InventoryFunded(address indexed donor, uint256 omrAmount);
  66:     event Purchased(
  67:         bytes32 indexed noteId,
  68:         address indexed buyer,
  69:         uint64 indexed epoch,
  70:         uint256 nativePaid,
  71:         uint256 omrReserved,
  72:         uint64 vestingEnd
  73:     );
  74:     event Claimed(bytes32 indexed noteId, address indexed beneficiary, uint256 omrAmount);
  75:     event ProceedsClaimed(address indexed recipient, uint256 nativeAmount);
  76:     event InventoryRetired(uint256 omrAmount);
  77:     event PauseSet(bool paused);
  78: 
  79:     address public immutable safe;
  80:     address public immutable proceedsRecipient;
  81:     IPoolManager public immutable manager;
  82:     PoolId public immutable poolId;
  83:     IERC20 public immutable omr;
  84:     IOmertaMarketStateV2 public immutable marketState;
  85:     uint32 public immutable maxObservationAge;
  86:     uint32 public immutable vestingSeconds;
  87:     uint128 public immutable minLiquidity;
  88:     uint24 public immutable maxSpotDeviationTicks;
  89:     uint16 public immutable discountBps;
  90:     uint128 public immutable minNativePurchase;
  91:     uint128 public immutable maxNativePurchase;
  92:     uint128 public immutable maxNativePerEpoch;
  93:     uint128 public immutable maxNativeLifetime;
  94:     uint128 public immutable maxOmrPerPurchase;
  95:     uint128 public immutable maxOmrPerEpoch;
  96:     uint128 public immutable maxOmrLifetime;
  97:     uint256 public immutable maxOmrPerEth;
  98:     bool public paused;
  99:     uint256 public availableInventory;
 100:     uint256 public outstandingClaims;
 101:     uint256 public proceedsOwed;
 102:     uint256 public nativePurchased;
 103:     uint256 public omrPurchased;
 104:     uint64 public lastPurchaseEpoch;
 105:     uint64 public lastPurchaseObservedAt;
 106:     uint256 public epochNativePurchased;
 107:     uint256 public epochOmrPurchased;
 108:     mapping(bytes32 => Note) public notes;
 109: 
 110:     constructor(Config memory c) {
 111:         if (
 112:             c.safe == address(0) || c.proceedsRecipient == address(0) || c.proceedsRecipient == address(this)
 113:                 || address(c.manager).code.length == 0 || address(c.marketState).code.length == 0
 114:                 || Currency.unwrap(c.key.currency0) != address(0) || Currency.unwrap(c.key.currency1).code.length == 0
 115:                 || address(c.key.hooks).code.length == 0 || c.maxObservationAge == 0 || c.maxObservationAge > 1 days
 116:                 || c.vestingSeconds < 1 days || c.vestingSeconds > 365 days || c.minLiquidity == 0
 117:                 || c.maxSpotDeviationTicks == 0 || c.maxSpotDeviationTicks > 2000 || c.discountBps > 1000
 118:                 || c.minNativePurchase == 0 || c.minNativePurchase > c.maxNativePurchase
 119:                 || c.maxNativePurchase > c.maxNativePerEpoch || c.maxNativePerEpoch > c.maxNativeLifetime
 120:                 || c.maxOmrPerPurchase == 0 || c.maxOmrPerPurchase > c.maxOmrPerEpoch
 121:                 || c.maxOmrPerEpoch > c.maxOmrLifetime || c.maxOmrPerEth == 0
 122:         ) revert BadConfiguration();
 123:         safe = c.safe;
 124:         proceedsRecipient = c.proceedsRecipient;
 125:         manager = c.manager;
 126:         poolId = c.key.toId();
 127:         omr = IERC20(Currency.unwrap(c.key.currency1));
 128:         marketState = c.marketState;
 129:         maxObservationAge = c.maxObservationAge;
 130:         vestingSeconds = c.vestingSeconds;
 131:         minLiquidity = c.minLiquidity;
 132:         maxSpotDeviationTicks = c.maxSpotDeviationTicks;
 133:         discountBps = c.discountBps;
 134:         minNativePurchase = c.minNativePurchase;
 135:         maxNativePurchase = c.maxNativePurchase;
 136:         maxNativePerEpoch = c.maxNativePerEpoch;
 137:         maxNativeLifetime = c.maxNativeLifetime;
 138:         maxOmrPerPurchase = c.maxOmrPerPurchase;
 139:         maxOmrPerEpoch = c.maxOmrPerEpoch;
 140:         maxOmrLifetime = c.maxOmrLifetime;
 141:         maxOmrPerEth = c.maxOmrPerEth;
 142:     }
 143: 
 144:     function fundInventory(uint256 amount) external nonReentrant {
 145:         if (amount == 0) revert InsufficientInventory();
 146:         uint256 beforeBalance = omr.balanceOf(address(this));
 147:         omr.safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), amount);
 148:         if (omr.balanceOf(address(this)) - beforeBalance != amount) revert InsufficientInventory();
 149:         availableInventory += amount;
 150:         emit InventoryFunded(msg.sender, amount);
 151:     }
 152: 
 153:     function setPaused(bool value) external {
 154:         if (msg.sender != safe) revert Unauthorized();
 155:         paused = value;
 156:         emit PauseSet(value);
 157:     }
 158: 
 159:     /// @notice Nontransferable receipts bind beneficiary and nonce; exact retry cannot reserve twice.
 160:     /// Slippage and expected epoch bind a purchase to a concrete published quote. No signature
 161:     /// grants inventory or issuance authority; finite aggregate limits apply across all buyers.
 162:     function purchase(uint64 expectedEpoch, uint256 minimumOmr, uint64 deadline, bytes32 nonce)
 163:         external
 164:         payable
 165:         nonReentrant
 166:         returns (bytes32 id, uint256 amount)
 167:     {
 168:         if (paused) revert Paused();
 169:         if (
 170:             nonce == bytes32(0) || deadline < block.timestamp || deadline > block.timestamp + 5 minutes
 171:                 || msg.value < minNativePurchase || msg.value > maxNativePurchase || minimumOmr == 0
 172:         ) revert InvalidQuote();
 173:         IOmertaMarketStateV2.Snapshot memory s = _observation(expectedEpoch);
 174:         id = keccak256(abi.encode(block.chainid, address(this), msg.sender, nonce));
 175:         if (notes[id].beneficiary != address(0)) revert Replay();
 176:         amount = _price(msg.value, s.omrPerEth);
 177:         if (amount < minimumOmr) revert InvalidQuote();
 178:         if (s.epoch < lastPurchaseEpoch || s.observedAt < lastPurchaseObservedAt) revert InvalidObservation();
 179:         if (s.epoch > lastPurchaseEpoch) {
 180:             // The epoch is trusted only as a unique published observation. Time cannot regress.
 181:             if (lastPurchaseEpoch != 0 && s.observedAt <= lastPurchaseObservedAt) revert InvalidObservation();
 182:             epochNativePurchased = 0;
 183:             epochOmrPurchased = 0;
 184:             lastPurchaseEpoch = s.epoch;
 185:             lastPurchaseObservedAt = s.observedAt;
 186:         }
 187:         if (
 188:             amount > maxOmrPerPurchase || nativePurchased + msg.value > maxNativeLifetime
 189:                 || omrPurchased + amount > maxOmrLifetime || epochNativePurchased + msg.value > maxNativePerEpoch
 190:                 || epochOmrPurchased + amount > maxOmrPerEpoch
 191:         ) revert BudgetExceeded();
 192:         if (amount > availableInventory) revert InsufficientInventory();
 193:         availableInventory -= amount;
 194:         outstandingClaims += amount;
 195:         proceedsOwed += msg.value;
 196:         nativePurchased += msg.value;
 197:         omrPurchased += amount;
 198:         epochNativePurchased += msg.value;
 199:         epochOmrPurchased += amount;
 200:         uint64 end = uint64(block.timestamp + vestingSeconds);
 201:         notes[id] = Note(msg.sender, uint64(block.timestamp), end, uint128(amount), 0);
 202:         emit Purchased(id, msg.sender, s.epoch, msg.value, amount, end);
 203:     }
 204: 
 205:     function quote(uint256 nativeAmount, uint64 expectedEpoch) external view returns (uint256 amount) {
 206:         if (nativeAmount < minNativePurchase || nativeAmount > maxNativePurchase) revert InvalidQuote();
 207:         return _price(nativeAmount, _observation(expectedEpoch).omrPerEth);
 208:     }
 209: 
 210:     /// @notice Claims remain available during oracle outages, exhausted sale budgets and pauses.
 211:     /// Anyone may settle a claim; funds always reach the recorded beneficiary.
 212:     function claim(bytes32 id) external nonReentrant returns (uint256 amount) {
 213:         Note storage n = notes[id];
 214:         amount = claimable(id);
 215:         if (amount == 0) revert NoClaim();
 216:         n.claimed += uint128(amount);
 217:         outstandingClaims -= amount;
 218:         omr.safeTransfer(n.beneficiary, amount);
 219:         emit Claimed(id, n.beneficiary, amount);
 220:     }
 221: 
 222:     function claimable(bytes32 id) public view returns (uint256) {
 223:         Note memory n = notes[id];
 224:         if (n.beneficiary == address(0) || block.timestamp <= n.start) return 0;
 225:         uint256 vested =
 226:             block.timestamp >= n.end ? n.amount : Math.mulDiv(n.amount, block.timestamp - n.start, n.end - n.start);
 227:         return vested - n.claimed;
 228:     }
 229: 
 230:     function claimProceeds() external nonReentrant {
 231:         uint256 amount = proceedsOwed;
 232:         proceedsOwed = 0;
 233:         if (amount != 0) {
 234:             (bool ok,) = proceedsRecipient.call{value: amount}("");
 235:             if (!ok) revert TransferFailed();
 236:         }
 237:         emit ProceedsClaimed(proceedsRecipient, amount);
 238:     }
 239: 
 240:     /// @notice Safe can retire only unpromised inventory. Note collateral is never withdrawable.
 241:     function retireInventory(uint256 amount) external nonReentrant {
 242:         if (msg.sender != safe) revert Unauthorized();
 243:         if (amount > availableInventory) revert InsufficientInventory();
 244:         availableInventory -= amount;
 245:         omr.safeTransfer(safe, amount);
 246:         emit InventoryRetired(amount);
 247:     }
 248: 
 249:     function _price(uint256 nativeAmount, uint256 omrPerEth) private view returns (uint256) {
 250:         // A D% discount on ETH/OMR price produces OMR/ETH divided by (1-D), rounded down.
 251:         uint256 atMarket = Math.mulDiv(nativeAmount, omrPerEth, 1 ether);
 252:         return Math.mulDiv(atMarket, 10_000, 10_000 - discountBps);
 253:     }
 254: 
 255:     function _observation(uint64 epoch) private view returns (IOmertaMarketStateV2.Snapshot memory s) {
 256:         s = marketState.snapshot();
 257:         if (
 258:             !s.valid || s.epoch == 0 || s.epoch != epoch || s.observedAt == 0 || s.observedAt > block.timestamp
 259:                 || block.timestamp - s.observedAt > maxObservationAge || s.omrPerEth == 0 || s.omrPerEth > maxOmrPerEth
 260:                 || s.minLiquidity < minLiquidity || manager.getLiquidity(poolId) < minLiquidity
 261:         ) revert InvalidObservation();
 262:         (uint160 spot, int24 tick,,) = manager.getSlot0(poolId);
 263:         int256 difference = int256(tick) - s.meanTick;
 264:         if (difference < 0) difference = -difference;
 265:         if (spot == 0 || uint256(difference) > maxSpotDeviationTicks) revert InvalidObservation();
 266:     }
 267: }
===== src/market-v2/OmertaArbitrageV2.sol =====
   1: // SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
   2: pragma solidity 0.8.26;
   3: 
   4: import {ReentrancyGuard} from "@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/ReentrancyGuard.sol";
   5: import {IPoolManager} from "v4-core/interfaces/IPoolManager.sol";
   6: import {IUnlockCallback} from "v4-core/interfaces/callback/IUnlockCallback.sol";
   7: import {PoolKey} from "v4-core/types/PoolKey.sol";
   8: import {PoolId, PoolIdLibrary} from "v4-core/types/PoolId.sol";
   9: import {Currency} from "v4-core/types/Currency.sol";
  10: import {BalanceDelta} from "v4-core/types/BalanceDelta.sol";
  11: import {SwapParams} from "v4-core/types/PoolOperation.sol";
  12: import {TickMath} from "v4-core/libraries/TickMath.sol";
  13: 
  14: /// @notice Solver-funded atomic ETH -> OMR -> ETH arbitrage with measured profit sharing.
  15: /// @dev Both pools live in one PoolManager. The alternative pool must be an unhooked ETH/OMR
  16: ///      pool; no executor, arbitrary calldata, token approvals or treasury capital is accepted.
  17: ///      The canonical hook still charges its normal fees. The caller posts collateral equal
  18: ///      to maximum input, then v4 nets the two trades and only realized net profit is taken.
  19: ///      A commitment binds the plan to its solver. It does not promise private order flow,
  20: ///      exclusive arbitrage rights, censorship resistance, or protection from price competition.
  21: contract OmertaArbitrageV2 is IUnlockCallback, ReentrancyGuard {
  22:     using PoolIdLibrary for PoolKey;
  23: 
  24:     struct Commitment {
  25:         bytes32 planHash;
  26:         uint64 blockNumber;
  27:     }
  28: 
  29:     struct Plan {
  30:         PoolKey alternative;
  31:         bool canonicalFirst;
  32:         uint128 amount;
  33:         uint128 minimumProfit;
  34:         uint64 deadline;
  35:         bytes32 salt;
  36:     }
  37: 
  38:     IPoolManager public immutable manager;
  39:     address public immutable omr;
  40:     address public immutable stabilityRecipient;
  41:     uint16 public immutable reserveProfitBps;
  42:     uint128 public immutable maxInput;
  43:     PoolKey private _canonical;
  44:     mapping(address => Commitment) public commitments;
  45:     mapping(address => uint256) public claimable;
  46:     uint256 public totalClaimable;
  47:     bytes32 private _activePlan;
  48: 
  49:     error InvalidConfiguration();
  50:     error InvalidPlan();
  51:     error InvalidCommitment();
  52:     error InvalidCallback();
  53:     error IncompleteCycle();
  54:     error InsufficientProfit();
  55:     error TransferFailed();
  56: 
  57:     event Committed(address indexed solver, bytes32 indexed planHash, uint64 blockNumber);
  58:     event ArbitrageSettled(
  59:         address indexed solver,
  60:         PoolId indexed alternativePool,
  61:         bool canonicalFirst,
  62:         uint256 inputCollateral,
  63:         uint256 realizedProfit,
  64:         uint256 stabilityShare
  65:     );
  66:     event Claimed(address indexed beneficiary, address indexed receiver, uint256 amount);
  67: 
  68:     constructor(
  69:         IPoolManager manager_,
  70:         PoolKey memory canonical_,
  71:         address stabilityRecipient_,
  72:         uint16 reserveProfitBps_,
  73:         uint128 maxInput_
  74:     ) {
  75:         if (
  76:             address(manager_).code.length == 0 || Currency.unwrap(canonical_.currency0) != address(0)
  77:                 || Currency.unwrap(canonical_.currency1).code.length == 0 || address(canonical_.hooks).code.length == 0
  78:                 || stabilityRecipient_ == address(0) || stabilityRecipient_ == address(this) || reserveProfitBps_ > 5000
  79:                 || maxInput_ == 0 || maxInput_ > uint128(type(int128).max) || canonical_.tickSpacing <= 0
  80:         ) revert InvalidConfiguration();
  81:         manager = manager_;
  82:         _canonical = canonical_;
  83:         omr = Currency.unwrap(canonical_.currency1);
  84:         stabilityRecipient = stabilityRecipient_;
  85:         reserveProfitBps = reserveProfitBps_;
  86:         maxInput = maxInput_;
  87:     }
  88: 
  89:     function poolKey() external view returns (PoolKey memory) {
  90:         return _canonical;
  91:     }
  92: 
  93:     function hashPlan(address solver, Plan calldata plan) public view returns (bytes32) {
  94:         return keccak256(abi.encode(block.chainid, address(this), solver, plan));
  95:     }
  96: 
  97:     function commit(bytes32 planHash) external {
  98:         if (planHash == bytes32(0)) revert InvalidPlan();
  99:         commitments[msg.sender] = Commitment(planHash, uint64(block.number));
 100:         emit Committed(msg.sender, planHash, uint64(block.number));
 101:     }
 102: 
 103:     function execute(Plan calldata plan) external payable nonReentrant returns (uint256 profit) {
 104:         if (
 105:             plan.amount == 0 || plan.amount > maxInput || msg.value != plan.amount || plan.minimumProfit == 0
 106:                 || plan.deadline < block.timestamp || plan.deadline > block.timestamp + 5 minutes
 107:                 || Currency.unwrap(plan.alternative.currency0) != address(0)
 108:                 || Currency.unwrap(plan.alternative.currency1) != omr || address(plan.alternative.hooks) != address(0)
 109:                 || plan.alternative.fee > 10_000 || plan.alternative.tickSpacing <= 0
 110:                 || plan.alternative.tickSpacing > TickMath.MAX_TICK_SPACING
 111:         ) revert InvalidPlan();
 112:         bytes32 h = hashPlan(msg.sender, plan);
 113:         Commitment memory c = commitments[msg.sender];
 114:         if (c.planHash != h || block.number <= c.blockNumber || block.number > uint256(c.blockNumber) + 64) {
 115:             revert InvalidCommitment();
 116:         }
 117:         delete commitments[msg.sender];
 118:         bytes memory payload = abi.encode(plan);
 119:         _activePlan = keccak256(payload);
 120:         profit = abi.decode(manager.unlock(payload), (uint256));
 121:         delete _activePlan;
 122:         uint256 reserve = profit * reserveProfitBps / 10_000;
 123:         claimable[stabilityRecipient] += reserve;
 124:         claimable[msg.sender] += msg.value + profit - reserve;
 125:         totalClaimable += msg.value + profit;
 126:         emit ArbitrageSettled(msg.sender, plan.alternative.toId(), plan.canonicalFirst, msg.value, profit, reserve);
 127:     }
 128: 
 129:     function unlockCallback(bytes calldata data) external returns (bytes memory) {
 130:         if (msg.sender != address(manager) || _activePlan == bytes32(0) || keccak256(data) != _activePlan) {
 131:             revert InvalidCallback();
 132:         }
 133:         delete _activePlan; // one callback, including when a pool unexpectedly re-enters
 134:         Plan memory plan = abi.decode(data, (Plan));
 135:         PoolKey memory first = plan.canonicalFirst ? _canonical : plan.alternative;
 136:         PoolKey memory second = plan.canonicalFirst ? plan.alternative : _canonical;
 137:         BalanceDelta buy =
 138:             manager.swap(first, SwapParams(true, -int256(uint256(plan.amount)), TickMath.MIN_SQRT_PRICE + 1), "");
 139:         if (buy.amount0() != -int128(plan.amount) || buy.amount1() <= 0) revert IncompleteCycle();
 140:         BalanceDelta sell =
 141:             manager.swap(second, SwapParams(false, -int256(buy.amount1()), TickMath.MAX_SQRT_PRICE - 1), "");
 142:         if (int256(sell.amount1()) + int256(buy.amount1()) != 0) revert IncompleteCycle();
 143:         int256 net = int256(buy.amount0()) + int256(sell.amount0());
 144:         if (net < int256(uint256(plan.minimumProfit))) revert InsufficientProfit();
 145:         uint256 profit = uint256(net);
 146:         manager.take(Currency.wrap(address(0)), address(this), profit);
 147:         return abi.encode(profit);
 148:     }
 149: 
 150:     function claim(address payable receiver) external nonReentrant {
 151:         _claim(msg.sender, receiver);
 152:     }
 153: 
 154:     /// @notice Anyone can settle a recipient's funded balance to that exact recipient.
 155:     function claimFor(address payable beneficiary) external nonReentrant {
 156:         _claim(beneficiary, beneficiary);
 157:     }
 158: 
 159:     function _claim(address beneficiary, address payable receiver) private {
 160:         if (receiver == address(0) || receiver == address(this)) revert InvalidPlan();
 161:         uint256 amount = claimable[beneficiary];
 162:         claimable[beneficiary] = 0;
 163:         totalClaimable -= amount;
 164:         (bool ok,) = receiver.call{value: amount}("");
 165:         if (!ok) revert TransferFailed();
 166:         emit Claimed(beneficiary, receiver, amount);
 167:     }
 168: 
 169:     receive() external payable {
 170:         if (msg.sender != address(manager)) revert InvalidCallback();
 171:     }
 172: }
===== src/market-v2/OmertaCommitmentVaultV2.sol =====
   1: // SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
   2: pragma solidity 0.8.26;
   3: 
   4: import {Ownable} from "@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable.sol";
   5: import {Ownable2Step} from "@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable2Step.sol";
   6: import {ReentrancyGuard} from "@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/ReentrancyGuard.sol";
   7: import {IERC721Receiver} from "@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC721/IERC721Receiver.sol";
   8: import {IPoolManager} from "v4-core/interfaces/IPoolManager.sol";
   9: import {PoolKey} from "v4-core/types/PoolKey.sol";
  10: import {PoolId, PoolIdLibrary} from "v4-core/types/PoolId.sol";
  11: import {Currency} from "v4-core/types/Currency.sol";
  12: import {TickMath} from "v4-core/libraries/TickMath.sol";
  13: import {FullMath} from "v4-core/libraries/FullMath.sol";
  14: import {IPositionManager} from "../../lib/v4-periphery/src/interfaces/IPositionManager.sol";
  15: import {PositionInfo} from "../../lib/v4-periphery/src/libraries/PositionInfoLibrary.sol";
  16: import {SqrtPriceMath} from "v4-core/libraries/SqrtPriceMath.sol";
  17: import {IOmertaMarketStateV2} from "./IOmertaMarketStateV2.sol";
  18: 
  19: interface ICommitmentPositionManagerV2 is IPositionManager {
  20:     function ownerOf(uint256 tokenId) external view returns (address);
  21:     function safeTransferFrom(address from, address to, uint256 tokenId) external;
  22: }
  23: 
  24: /// @notice Custodies canonical v4 NFTs for a finite term and pays only prefunded depth-time bounties.
  25: /// @dev Scoring is a conservative sampled-depth incentive, NOT proof of continuous tick occupancy.
  26: /// Two distinct fresh observation epochs are required. Gaps, pause transitions, and invalid samples
  27: /// break accrual. Depth is the smaller ETH-valued inventory on each side of a bounded reference band;
  28: /// raw liquidity L, one-sided positions, and a single initial snapshot never earn a reward.
  29: /// Campaign terms are immutable; payment is first-come from available funds at each checkpoint.
  30: contract OmertaCommitmentVaultV2 is Ownable2Step, ReentrancyGuard, IERC721Receiver {
  31:     using PoolIdLibrary for PoolKey;
  32: 
  33:     struct CampaignTerms {
  34:         uint64 startsAt;
  35:         uint64 endsAt;
  36:         uint32 minLock;
  37:         uint32 maxLock;
  38:         uint32 maxObservationGap;
  39:         uint24 referenceHalfWidth;
  40:         uint128 maxDepthWei;
  41:         uint128 maxRewardPerPosition;
  42:         uint128 rewardWeiPerEthDay;
  43:     }
  44:     struct Campaign { CampaignTerms terms; uint256 available; uint256 paidOrOwed; }
  45:     struct Commitment {
  46:         address depositor;
  47:         uint64 campaignId;
  48:         uint64 depositedAt;
  49:         uint64 maturesAt;
  50:         uint64 lastEpoch;
  51:         uint64 lastObservedAt;
  52:         uint64 generation;
  53:         uint128 lastDepth;
  54:         uint256 usefulDepthSeconds;
  55:         uint256 rewardCredited;
  56:         bool withdrawn;
  57:     }
  58: 
  59:     ICommitmentPositionManagerV2 public immutable positionManager;
  60:     IOmertaMarketStateV2 public immutable marketState;
  61:     bytes32 public immutable poolId;
  62:     uint32 public immutable maxObservationAge;
  63:     uint128 public immutable minimumMarketLiquidity;
  64:     uint64 public campaignCount;
  65:     uint64 public pauseGeneration;
  66:     bool public paused;
  67:     uint256 public rewardLiability;
  68:     uint256 public availableBudget;
  69:     mapping(uint64 => Campaign) private _campaigns;
  70:     mapping(uint256 => Commitment) public commitments;
  71:     mapping(address => uint256) public rewards;
  72:     uint256 private _acceptingTokenId;
  73:     address private _acceptingFrom;
  74: 
  75:     error InvalidConfiguration();
  76:     error InvalidPosition();
  77:     error InvalidCampaign();
  78:     error InvalidObservation();
  79:     error Paused();
  80:     error Unauthorized();
  81:     error NotMature();
  82:     error InvalidTransfer();
  83:     event CampaignCreated(uint64 indexed campaignId, uint256 fundedAmount);
  84:     event CampaignFunded(uint64 indexed campaignId, uint256 fundedAmount);
  85:     event Committed(uint256 indexed tokenId, address indexed depositor, uint64 indexed campaignId, uint64 maturesAt);
  86:     event Checkpointed(uint256 indexed tokenId, uint64 epoch, uint256 depthSeconds, uint256 reward);
  87:     event ObservationSkipped(uint256 indexed tokenId);
  88:     event Withdrawn(uint256 indexed tokenId, address indexed depositor, address indexed recipient);
  89:     event RewardClaimed(address indexed depositor, address indexed recipient, uint256 amount);
  90:     event PauseChanged(bool paused, uint64 generation);
  91:     event UnusedBudgetRecovered(uint64 indexed campaignId, address indexed recipient, uint256 amount);
  92: 
  93:     constructor(address safe, ICommitmentPositionManagerV2 manager, IOmertaMarketStateV2 state,
  94:         PoolKey memory canonicalKey, uint32 maxAge, uint128 minLiquidity) Ownable(safe)
  95:     {
  96:         if (address(manager).code.length == 0 || address(state).code.length == 0
  97:             || address(manager.poolManager()).code.length == 0 || maxAge == 0 || maxAge > 1 days
  98:             || Currency.unwrap(canonicalKey.currency0) != address(0)
  99:             || Currency.unwrap(canonicalKey.currency1).code.length == 0 || canonicalKey.tickSpacing <= 0
 100:             || minLiquidity == 0) revert InvalidConfiguration();
 101:         positionManager = manager;
 102:         marketState = state;
 103:         poolId = PoolId.unwrap(canonicalKey.toId());
 104:         maxObservationAge = maxAge;
 105:         minimumMarketLiquidity = minLiquidity;
 106:     }
 107: 
 108:     function campaign(uint64 id) external view returns (Campaign memory) { return _campaigns[id]; }
 109:     function commitment(uint256 id) external view returns (Commitment memory) { return commitments[id]; }
 110: 
 111:     function createCampaign(CampaignTerms calldata terms) external payable onlyOwner returns (uint64 id) {
 112:         if (terms.startsAt < block.timestamp || terms.endsAt <= terms.startsAt || terms.endsAt - terms.startsAt > 365 days
 113:             || terms.minLock < 1 hours || terms.maxLock < terms.minLock || terms.maxLock > 365 days
 114:             || terms.maxObservationGap == 0 || terms.maxObservationGap > 1 days
 115:             || terms.referenceHalfWidth == 0 || terms.referenceHalfWidth > 50_000
 116:             || terms.maxDepthWei == 0 || terms.maxRewardPerPosition == 0 || terms.rewardWeiPerEthDay == 0
 117:             || msg.value == 0) revert InvalidConfiguration();
 118:         id = ++campaignCount;
 119:         _campaigns[id] = Campaign(terms, msg.value, 0);
 120:         availableBudget += msg.value;
 121:         emit CampaignCreated(id, msg.value);
 122:     }
 123: 
 124:     function fundCampaign(uint64 id) external payable {
 125:         Campaign storage c = _campaigns[id];
 126:         if (c.terms.endsAt == 0 || block.timestamp >= c.terms.endsAt || msg.value == 0) revert InvalidCampaign();
 127:         c.available += msg.value;
 128:         availableBudget += msg.value;
 129:         emit CampaignFunded(id, msg.value);
 130:     }
 131: 
 132:     function setPaused(bool value) external onlyOwner {
 133:         if (paused != value) { paused = value; ++pauseGeneration; }
 134:         emit PauseChanged(value, pauseGeneration);
 135:     }
 136: 
 137:     function commit(uint256 tokenId, uint64 campaignId, uint32 duration) external nonReentrant {
 138:         if (paused) revert Paused();
 139:         CampaignTerms memory t = _campaigns[campaignId].terms;
 140:         if (block.timestamp < t.startsAt || block.timestamp >= t.endsAt || duration < t.minLock || duration > t.maxLock
 141:             || block.timestamp + duration > t.endsAt) revert InvalidCampaign();
 142:         if (commitments[tokenId].depositor != address(0) || positionManager.ownerOf(tokenId) != msg.sender) revert InvalidPosition();
 143:         (PoolKey memory key, PositionInfo info) = positionManager.getPoolAndPositionInfo(tokenId);
 144:         if (PoolId.unwrap(key.toId()) != poolId || info.hasSubscriber() || positionManager.getPositionLiquidity(tokenId) == 0)
 145:             revert InvalidPosition();
 146:         Commitment storage p = commitments[tokenId];
 147:         p.depositor = msg.sender;
 148:         p.campaignId = campaignId;
 149:         p.depositedAt = uint64(block.timestamp);
 150:         p.maturesAt = uint64(block.timestamp + duration);
 151:         p.generation = pauseGeneration;
 152:         _acceptingTokenId = tokenId;
 153:         _acceptingFrom = msg.sender;
 154:         positionManager.safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), tokenId);
 155:         _acceptingFrom = address(0);
 156:         if (positionManager.ownerOf(tokenId) != address(this)) revert InvalidPosition();
 157:         // The first sample initializes an anchor only. It creates no entitlement.
 158:         _checkpoint(tokenId, p);
 159:         emit Committed(tokenId, msg.sender, campaignId, p.maturesAt);
 160:     }
 161: 
 162:     function onERC721Received(address operator, address from, uint256 tokenId, bytes calldata) external view returns (bytes4) {
 163:         if (msg.sender != address(positionManager) || operator != address(this) || from != _acceptingFrom
 164:             || from == address(0) || tokenId != _acceptingTokenId) revert InvalidPosition();
 165:         return IERC721Receiver.onERC721Received.selector;
 166:     }
 167: 
 168:     /// @notice Permissionless; credits only the immutable original depositor.
 169:     function checkpoint(uint256 tokenId) external nonReentrant {
 170:         if (paused) revert Paused();
 171:         Commitment storage p = commitments[tokenId];
 172:         if (p.depositor == address(0) || p.withdrawn) revert InvalidPosition();
 173:         _checkpoint(tokenId, p);
 174:     }
 175: 
 176:     /// @notice No pause, oracle, campaign budget, owner approval, or fee payment can block maturity exit.
 177:     /// Fees accumulated inside the position remain attached to its original NFT.
 178:     function withdraw(uint256 tokenId, address recipient) external nonReentrant {
 179:         Commitment storage p = commitments[tokenId];
 180:         if (msg.sender != p.depositor || p.withdrawn) revert Unauthorized();
 181:         if (block.timestamp < p.maturesAt) revert NotMature();
 182:         if (recipient == address(0) || recipient == address(this)) revert InvalidTransfer();
 183:         p.withdrawn = true;
 184:         positionManager.safeTransferFrom(address(this), recipient, tokenId);
 185:         emit Withdrawn(tokenId, p.depositor, recipient);
 186:     }
 187: 
 188:     function claimReward(address payable recipient) external nonReentrant {
 189:         uint256 amount = rewards[msg.sender];
 190:         if (amount == 0 || recipient == address(0) || recipient == address(this)) revert InvalidTransfer();
 191:         rewards[msg.sender] = 0;
 192:         rewardLiability -= amount;
 193:         (bool ok,) = recipient.call{value: amount}("");
 194:         if (!ok) revert InvalidTransfer();
 195:         emit RewardClaimed(msg.sender, recipient, amount);
 196:     }
 197: 
 198:     /// @dev Accrual ends at campaign expiry. Grace covers the last valid observation window.
 199:     /// Only funds which never became an entitlement can be recovered, never any NFT.
 200:     function recoverUnusedBudget(uint64 id, address payable recipient) external onlyOwner nonReentrant {
 201:         Campaign storage c = _campaigns[id];
 202:         if (c.terms.endsAt == 0 || block.timestamp <= uint256(c.terms.endsAt) + maxObservationAge
 203:             || recipient == address(0) || recipient == address(this)) revert InvalidCampaign();
 204:         uint256 amount = c.available;
 205:         c.available = 0;
 206:         availableBudget -= amount;
 207:         (bool ok,) = recipient.call{value: amount}("");
 208:         if (!ok) revert InvalidTransfer();
 209:         emit UnusedBudgetRecovered(id, recipient, amount);
 210:     }
 211: 
 212:     function usefulDepth(uint256 tokenId, IOmertaMarketStateV2.Snapshot memory s, uint24 halfWidth)
 213:         public view returns (uint128)
 214:     {
 215:         (, PositionInfo info) = positionManager.getPoolAndPositionInfo(tokenId);
 216:         int256 lower = int256(s.meanTick) - int256(uint256(halfWidth));
 217:         int256 upper = int256(s.meanTick) + int256(uint256(halfWidth));
 218:         // Require the entire reference band and observed volatility margin to fit the position.
 219:         if (lower < TickMath.MIN_TICK || upper > TickMath.MAX_TICK || lower < info.tickLower() || upper > info.tickUpper()
 220:             || s.volatilityTicks > halfWidth || s.omrPerEth == 0) return 0;
 221:         uint128 liquidity = positionManager.getPositionLiquidity(tokenId);
 222:         uint160 mid = TickMath.getSqrtPriceAtTick(s.meanTick);
 223:         uint256 nativeDepth = SqrtPriceMath.getAmount0Delta(mid, TickMath.getSqrtPriceAtTick(int24(upper)), liquidity, false);
 224:         uint256 omrDepth = SqrtPriceMath.getAmount1Delta(TickMath.getSqrtPriceAtTick(int24(lower)), mid, liquidity, false);
 225:         uint256 quoteDepth = FullMath.mulDiv(omrDepth, 1 ether, s.omrPerEth);
 226:         uint256 depth = nativeDepth < quoteDepth ? nativeDepth : quoteDepth;
 227:         return depth > type(uint128).max ? type(uint128).max : uint128(depth);
 228:     }
 229: 
 230:     function _checkpoint(uint256 tokenId, Commitment storage p) internal {
 231:         IOmertaMarketStateV2.Snapshot memory s;
 232:         try marketState.snapshot() returns (IOmertaMarketStateV2.Snapshot memory result) { s = result; }
 233:         catch { _breakAnchor(tokenId, p); return; }
 234:         if (!s.valid || s.epoch == 0 || s.minLiquidity < minimumMarketLiquidity || s.omrPerEth == 0
 235:             || s.observedAt > block.timestamp || block.timestamp - s.observedAt > maxObservationAge
 236:             || s.observedAt < p.depositedAt) { _breakAnchor(tokenId, p); return; }
 237:         if (s.epoch <= p.lastEpoch || s.observedAt <= p.lastObservedAt) revert InvalidObservation();
 238:         Campaign storage c = _campaigns[p.campaignId];
 239:         uint128 depth = usefulDepth(tokenId, s, c.terms.referenceHalfWidth);
 240:         if (depth > c.terms.maxDepthWei) depth = c.terms.maxDepthWei;
 241:         uint256 depthSeconds;
 242:         uint256 reward;
 243:         if (p.lastObservedAt != 0 && p.generation == pauseGeneration
 244:             && s.observedAt - p.lastObservedAt <= c.terms.maxObservationGap && p.lastObservedAt < p.maturesAt) {
 245:             uint64 until = s.observedAt < p.maturesAt ? s.observedAt : p.maturesAt;
 246:             uint128 minimumDepth = depth < p.lastDepth ? depth : p.lastDepth;
 247:             depthSeconds = uint256(minimumDepth) * (until - p.lastObservedAt);
 248:             p.usefulDepthSeconds += depthSeconds;
 249:             reward = FullMath.mulDiv(depthSeconds, c.terms.rewardWeiPerEthDay, 1 ether * 1 days);
 250:             uint256 remainingCap = c.terms.maxRewardPerPosition - p.rewardCredited;
 251:             if (reward > remainingCap) reward = remainingCap;
 252:             if (reward > c.available) reward = c.available;
 253:             p.rewardCredited += reward;
 254:             c.available -= reward;
 255:             c.paidOrOwed += reward;
 256:             availableBudget -= reward;
 257:             rewardLiability += reward;
 258:             rewards[p.depositor] += reward;
 259:         }
 260:         p.lastEpoch = s.epoch;
 261:         p.lastObservedAt = s.observedAt;
 262:         p.lastDepth = depth;
 263:         p.generation = pauseGeneration;
 264:         emit Checkpointed(tokenId, s.epoch, depthSeconds, reward);
 265:     }
 266: 
 267:     function _breakAnchor(uint256 tokenId, Commitment storage p) internal {
 268:         p.lastObservedAt = 0;
 269:         p.lastDepth = 0;
 270:         p.generation = pauseGeneration;
 271:         emit ObservationSkipped(tokenId);
 272:     }
 273: }
===== src/market-v2/OmertaGameSettlementV2.sol =====
   1: // SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
   2: pragma solidity 0.8.26;
   3: 
   4: import {ReentrancyGuard} from "@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/ReentrancyGuard.sol";
   5: import {OmertaTurfV2} from "./OmertaTurfV2.sol";
   6: import {OmertaTurfFeeBridgeV2} from "./OmertaTurfFeeBridgeV2.sol";
   7: 
   8: /// @notice Typed game settlement boundary: earned fees settle before future rights change.
   9: /// @dev The adjudicator decides game outcomes. It cannot withdraw principal or create unbacked fees.
  10: contract OmertaGameSettlementV2 is ReentrancyGuard {
  11:     uint256 public constant MAX_ACTIVE_LANES = 64;
  12:     address public immutable safe;
  13:     address public immutable adjudicator;
  14:     OmertaTurfV2 public registry;
  15:     mapping(uint64 => mapping(uint32 => OmertaTurfFeeBridgeV2)) public bridges;
  16:     OmertaTurfFeeBridgeV2[] private _active;
  17:     error Unauthorized();
  18:     error InvalidBinding();
  19:     event RegistryBound(address indexed registry);
  20:     event LaneRegistered(uint64 indexed seasonId, uint32 indexed turfId, address bridge);
  21:     event LaneArchived(address indexed bridge);
  22: 
  23:     constructor(address safe_, address adjudicator_) {
  24:         if (safe_ == address(0) || adjudicator_ == address(0)) revert InvalidBinding();
  25:         safe = safe_;
  26:         adjudicator = adjudicator_;
  27:     }
  28:     modifier onlyGame() {
  29:         if (msg.sender != adjudicator) revert Unauthorized();
  30:         _;
  31:     }
  32: 
  33:     function bindRegistry(OmertaTurfV2 registry_) external {
  34:         if (msg.sender != safe) revert Unauthorized();
  35:         if (
  36:             address(registry) != address(0) || address(registry_).code.length == 0 || registry_.game() != address(this)
  37:                 || registry_.owner() != safe
  38:         ) revert InvalidBinding();
  39:         registry = registry_;
  40:         emit RegistryBound(address(registry_));
  41:     }
  42: 
  43:     function registerLane(OmertaTurfFeeBridgeV2 bridge) external {
  44:         if (msg.sender != safe) revert Unauthorized();
  45:         if (
  46:             address(registry) == address(0) || address(bridge).code.length == 0
  47:                 || address(bridge.registry()) != address(registry) || bridge.safe() != safe
  48:                 || address(bridge.source()) == address(0) || bridge.closed() || _active.length >= MAX_ACTIVE_LANES
  49:         ) revert InvalidBinding();
  50:         uint64 season = bridge.seasonId();
  51:         uint32 turf = bridge.turfId();
  52:         (uint64 startsAt,,) = registry.seasons(season);
  53:         if (block.timestamp >= startsAt) revert InvalidBinding();
  54:         (uint64 laneSeason, uint32 laneTurf, bool bound) = registry.feeSources(address(bridge));
  55:         if (!bound || laneSeason != season || laneTurf != turf || address(bridges[season][turf]) != address(0)) {
  56:             revert InvalidBinding();
  57:         }
  58:         bridges[season][turf] = bridge;
  59:         _active.push(bridge);
  60:         emit LaneRegistered(season, turf, address(bridge));
  61:     }
  62: 
  63:     function activeLanes() external view returns (OmertaTurfFeeBridgeV2[] memory) {
  64:         return _active;
  65:     }
  66: 
  67:     /// @dev Free capacity for later seasons only after all source fees have reached their recipients.
  68:     function archiveLane(uint256 index) external nonReentrant {
  69:         if (index >= _active.length) revert InvalidBinding();
  70:         OmertaTurfFeeBridgeV2 bridge = _active[index];
  71:         bridge.close();
  72:         _active[index] = _active[_active.length - 1];
  73:         _active.pop();
  74:         emit LaneArchived(address(bridge));
  75:     }
  76: 
  77:     function setFamilyTreasury(uint64 family, address treasury, uint64 expectedRevision)
  78:         external
  79:         onlyGame
  80:         nonReentrant
  81:     {
  82:         if (address(registry) == address(0)) revert InvalidBinding();
  83:         // A wallet change affects every currently earning lane; cap bounds the work.
  84:         for (uint256 i; i < _active.length; ++i) {
  85:             _active[i].checkpointFees();
  86:         }
  87:         registry.setFamilyTreasury(family, treasury, expectedRevision);
  88:     }
  89: 
  90:     function settleOwnership(
  91:         uint64 season,
  92:         uint32 turf,
  93:         OmertaTurfV2.Split calldata owners,
  94:         uint64 revision,
  95:         uint64 epoch,
  96:         bytes32 receipt
  97:     ) external onlyGame nonReentrant {
  98:         _checkpoint(season, turf);
  99:         registry.settleOwnership(season, turf, owners, revision, epoch, receipt);
 100:     }
 101: 
 102:     function settleStatus(
 103:         uint64 season,
 104:         uint32 turf,
 105:         uint64 corridors,
 106:         uint8 fortification,
 107:         uint16 loyalty,
 108:         uint64 revision,
 109:         uint64 epoch,
 110:         bytes32 receipt
 111:     ) external onlyGame nonReentrant {
 112:         registry.settleStatus(season, turf, corridors, fortification, loyalty, revision, epoch, receipt);
 113:     }
 114: 
 115:     function startSiege(
 116:         uint64 season,
 117:         uint32 turf,
 118:         OmertaTurfV2.Split calldata attacker,
 119:         uint32 duration,
 120:         uint16 victoryBps,
 121:         uint64 revision,
 122:         uint64 epoch,
 123:         bytes32 receipt
 124:     ) external onlyGame nonReentrant {
 125:         _checkpoint(season, turf);
 126:         registry.startSiege(season, turf, attacker, duration, victoryBps, revision, epoch, receipt);
 127:     }
 128: 
 129:     function resolveSiege(uint64 season, uint32 turf, bool attackerWon, uint64 revision, uint64 epoch, bytes32 receipt)
 130:         external
 131:         onlyGame
 132:         nonReentrant
 133:     {
 134:         _checkpoint(season, turf);
 135:         registry.resolveSiege(season, turf, attackerWon, revision, epoch, receipt);
 136:     }
 137: 
 138:     /// @notice Permissionless expiry still checkpoints the frozen fee recipients atomically.
 139:     function expireSiege(uint64 season, uint32 turf) external nonReentrant {
 140:         OmertaTurfV2.Siege memory siege = registry.siege(season, turf);
 141:         if (siege.expiresAt == 0 || block.timestamp < siege.expiresAt) revert InvalidBinding();
 142:         _checkpoint(season, turf);
 143:         if (registry.siege(season, turf).expiresAt != 0) registry.expireSiege(season, turf);
 144:     }
 145: 
 146:     function _checkpoint(uint64 season, uint32 turf) private {
 147:         OmertaTurfFeeBridgeV2 bridge = bridges[season][turf];
 148:         if (address(bridge) == address(0)) revert InvalidBinding();
 149:         bridge.checkpointFees();
 150:     }
 151: }
===== src/market-v2/OmertaTurfV2.sol =====
   1: // SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
   2: pragma solidity 0.8.26;
   3: 
   4: import {Ownable} from "@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable.sol";
   5: import {Ownable2Step} from "@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable2Step.sol";
   6: import {ReentrancyGuard} from "@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/ReentrancyGuard.sol";
   7: import {IERC20} from "@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/IERC20.sol";
   8: import {SafeERC20} from "@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/utils/SafeERC20.sol";
   9: import {IOmertaMarketStateV2} from "./IOmertaMarketStateV2.sol";
  10: 
  11: /// @notice Seasonal territorial rights to already funded fees. Never custodies LP principal.
  12: /// @dev The game adjudicator is trusted for future ownership and bounded game status only.
  13: /// Every fee deposit checkpoints recipient addresses immediately; treasury changes are prospective.
  14: /// An active siege freezes both recipient lists and its victory split until resolution or expiry.
  15: contract OmertaTurfV2 is Ownable2Step, ReentrancyGuard {
  16:     using SafeERC20 for IERC20;
  17: 
  18:     uint256 public constant BPS = 10_000;
  19:     uint32 public constant MAX_TURFS = 64;
  20:     uint32 public constant MIN_SIEGE = 1 hours;
  21:     uint32 public constant MAX_SIEGE = 7 days;
  22: 
  23:     struct Split { uint64[4] families; uint16[4] shares; uint8 count; }
  24:     struct Recipients { address[4] wallets; uint16[4] shares; uint8 count; }
  25:     struct Season { uint64 startsAt; uint64 endsAt; uint32 turfCount; }
  26:     struct Turf {
  27:         int24 lowerTick;
  28:         int24 upperTick;
  29:         uint64 revision;
  30:         uint64 lastEpoch;
  31:         uint64 corridors;
  32:         uint16 loyaltyBps;
  33:         uint8 fortification;
  34:         Split owners;
  35:     }
  36:     struct Siege {
  37:         uint64 expiresAt;
  38:         uint16 attackerVictoryBps;
  39:         uint256 nativeEscrow;
  40:         uint256 omrEscrow;
  41:         Split attacker;
  42:         Recipients defenderRecipients;
  43:         Recipients attackerRecipients;
  44:     }
  45:     struct Credit { uint256 nativeAmount; uint256 omrAmount; }
  46:     struct FeeLane { uint64 seasonId; uint32 turfId; bool bound; }
  47: 
  48:     IERC20 public immutable omr;
  49:     address public immutable game;
  50:     IOmertaMarketStateV2 public immutable marketState;
  51:     int24 public immutable tickSpacing;
  52:     uint32 public immutable maxObservationAge;
  53:     uint64 public seasonCount;
  54:     uint256 public nativeLiability;
  55:     uint256 public omrLiability;
  56:     mapping(uint64 => Season) public seasons;
  57:     mapping(uint64 => mapping(uint32 => Turf)) private _turfs;
  58:     mapping(uint64 => mapping(uint32 => Siege)) private _sieges;
  59:     mapping(uint64 => address) public familyTreasury;
  60:     mapping(uint64 => uint64) public familyRevision;
  61:     mapping(bytes32 => bool) public settlementUsed;
  62:     mapping(address => Credit) public credits;
  63:     mapping(address => FeeLane) public feeSources;
  64:     mapping(uint64 => mapping(uint32 => address)) public laneSource;
  65: 
  66:     error Unauthorized();
  67:     error InvalidConfiguration();
  68:     error InvalidSettlement();
  69:     error StaleRevision();
  70:     error InvalidSeason();
  71:     error ActiveSiege();
  72:     error InvalidSiege();
  73:     error InvalidTransfer();
  74:     error NothingToClaim();
  75:     event FeeSourceBound(address indexed source, uint64 indexed seasonId, uint32 indexed turfId);
  76:     event FamilyTreasurySet(uint64 indexed family, address indexed treasury, uint64 revision);
  77:     event SeasonCreated(uint64 indexed season, uint64 startsAt, uint64 endsAt);
  78:     event TurfCreated(uint64 indexed season, uint32 indexed turf, int24 lowerTick, int24 upperTick);
  79:     event OwnershipSettled(uint64 indexed season, uint32 indexed turf, uint64 revision, bytes32 settlementId);
  80:     event StatusSettled(uint64 indexed season, uint32 indexed turf, uint64 revision, uint64 corridors, uint8 fortification, uint16 loyaltyBps);
  81:     event FeesFunded(uint64 indexed season, uint32 indexed turf, uint256 nativeAmount, uint256 omrAmount, bool escrowed);
  82:     event SiegeStarted(uint64 indexed season, uint32 indexed turf, uint64 expiresAt, uint16 attackerVictoryBps);
  83:     event SiegeEnded(uint64 indexed season, uint32 indexed turf, bool attackerWon, bool expired);
  84:     event Claimed(address indexed account, address indexed recipient, uint256 nativeAmount, uint256 omrAmount);
  85: 
  86:     constructor(address safe, IERC20 token, address adjudicator, IOmertaMarketStateV2 state, int24 spacing, uint32 maxAge)
  87:         Ownable(safe)
  88:     {
  89:         if (address(token).code.length == 0 || adjudicator == address(0) || address(state).code.length == 0
  90:             || spacing <= 0 || spacing > 32767 || maxAge == 0 || maxAge > 1 days) revert InvalidConfiguration();
  91:         omr = token;
  92:         game = adjudicator;
  93:         marketState = state;
  94:         tickSpacing = spacing;
  95:         maxObservationAge = maxAge;
  96:     }
  97: 
  98:     /// @notice A bridge receives authority over exactly one immutable fee attribution lane.
  99:     function bindFeeSource(address source, uint64 seasonId, uint32 turfId) external onlyOwner {
 100:         if (source.code.length == 0 || feeSources[source].bound || seasons[seasonId].endsAt == 0
 101:             || turfId >= seasons[seasonId].turfCount || block.timestamp >= seasons[seasonId].startsAt
 102:             || laneSource[seasonId][turfId] != address(0)) revert InvalidConfiguration();
 103:         feeSources[source] = FeeLane(seasonId, turfId, true);
 104:         laneSource[seasonId][turfId] = source;
 105:         emit FeeSourceBound(source, seasonId, turfId);
 106:     }
 107: 
 108:     /// @notice Historical credits remain owned by their previous treasury address.
 109:     function setFamilyTreasury(uint64 family, address treasury, uint64 expectedRevision) external {
 110:         if (msg.sender != game) revert Unauthorized();
 111:         if (family == 0 || treasury == address(0) || treasury == address(this)) revert InvalidConfiguration();
 112:         if (familyRevision[family] != expectedRevision) revert StaleRevision();
 113:         familyRevision[family] = expectedRevision + 1;
 114:         familyTreasury[family] = treasury;
 115:         emit FamilyTreasurySet(family, treasury, expectedRevision + 1);
 116:     }
 117: 
 118:     function createSeason(uint64 startsAt, uint64 endsAt) external onlyOwner returns (uint64 id) {
 119:         if (startsAt < block.timestamp || endsAt <= startsAt || endsAt - startsAt > 365 days
 120:             || (seasonCount != 0 && startsAt < seasons[seasonCount].endsAt)) revert InvalidSeason();
 121:         id = ++seasonCount;
 122:         seasons[id] = Season(startsAt, endsAt, 0);
 123:         emit SeasonCreated(id, startsAt, endsAt);
 124:     }
 125: 
 126:     /// @dev Ranges must be ordered and non-overlapping and become immutable at creation.
 127:     function createTurf(uint64 seasonId, int24 lower, int24 upper, Split calldata initialOwners)
 128:         external onlyOwner returns (uint32 turfId)
 129:     {
 130:         Season storage s = seasons[seasonId];
 131:         if (s.endsAt == 0 || block.timestamp >= s.startsAt || s.turfCount == MAX_TURFS) revert InvalidSeason();
 132:         if (lower < -887272 || upper > 887272 || lower >= upper || lower % tickSpacing != 0 || upper % tickSpacing != 0)
 133:             revert InvalidConfiguration();
 134:         if (s.turfCount != 0 && lower < _turfs[seasonId][s.turfCount - 1].upperTick) revert InvalidConfiguration();
 135:         _recipients(initialOwners);
 136:         turfId = s.turfCount++;
 137:         Turf storage t = _turfs[seasonId][turfId];
 138:         t.lowerTick = lower;
 139:         t.upperTick = upper;
 140:         t.owners = initialOwners;
 141:         t.revision = 1;
 142:         emit TurfCreated(seasonId, turfId, lower, upper);
 143:     }
 144: 
 145:     function turf(uint64 seasonId, uint32 turfId) external view returns (Turf memory) { return _turfs[seasonId][turfId]; }
 146:     function siege(uint64 seasonId, uint32 turfId) external view returns (Siege memory) { return _sieges[seasonId][turfId]; }
 147: 
 148:     function settleOwnership(uint64 seasonId, uint32 turfId, Split calldata nextOwners, uint64 expectedRevision,
 149:         uint64 epoch, bytes32 settlementId) external
 150:     {
 151:         Turf storage t = _settlement(seasonId, turfId, expectedRevision, epoch, settlementId);
 152:         if (_sieges[seasonId][turfId].expiresAt != 0) revert ActiveSiege();
 153:         _recipients(nextOwners);
 154:         t.owners = nextOwners;
 155:         t.corridors = 0;
 156:         t.loyaltyBps = 0;
 157:         t.fortification = 0;
 158:         emit OwnershipSettled(seasonId, turfId, t.revision, settlementId);
 159:     }
 160: 
 161:     /// @notice Status is bounded game data, never a multiplier on a funded claim.
 162:     /// Corridors can identify only immediately adjacent, touching, same-syndicate territories.
 163:     function settleStatus(uint64 seasonId, uint32 turfId, uint64 corridors, uint8 fortification, uint16 loyaltyBps,
 164:         uint64 expectedRevision, uint64 epoch, bytes32 settlementId) external
 165:     {
 166:         Turf storage t = _settlement(seasonId, turfId, expectedRevision, epoch, settlementId);
 167:         if (fortification > 10 || loyaltyBps > BPS) revert InvalidConfiguration();
 168:         uint64 allowed;
 169:         if (turfId > 0 && _connected(t, _turfs[seasonId][turfId - 1], false)) allowed |= uint64(1) << (turfId - 1);
 170:         if (turfId + 1 < seasons[seasonId].turfCount && _connected(t, _turfs[seasonId][turfId + 1], true))
 171:             allowed |= uint64(1) << (turfId + 1);
 172:         if (corridors & ~allowed != 0) revert InvalidConfiguration();
 173:         t.corridors = corridors;
 174:         t.fortification = fortification;
 175:         t.loyaltyBps = loyaltyBps;
 176:         emit StatusSettled(seasonId, turfId, t.revision, corridors, fortification, loyaltyBps);
 177:     }
 178: 
 179:     function startSiege(uint64 seasonId, uint32 turfId, Split calldata attacker, uint32 duration,
 180:         uint16 attackerVictoryBps, uint64 expectedRevision, uint64 epoch, bytes32 settlementId) external
 181:     {
 182:         Turf storage t = _settlement(seasonId, turfId, expectedRevision, epoch, settlementId);
 183:         Siege storage s = _sieges[seasonId][turfId];
 184:         if (s.expiresAt != 0) revert ActiveSiege();
 185:         if (duration < MIN_SIEGE || duration > MAX_SIEGE || block.timestamp + duration > seasons[seasonId].endsAt
 186:             || attackerVictoryBps > BPS || attackerVictoryBps == 0
 187:             || keccak256(abi.encode(attacker)) == keccak256(abi.encode(t.owners))) revert InvalidSiege();
 188:         s.defenderRecipients = _recipients(t.owners);
 189:         s.attackerRecipients = _recipients(attacker);
 190:         s.attacker = attacker;
 191:         s.expiresAt = uint64(block.timestamp + duration);
 192:         s.attackerVictoryBps = attackerVictoryBps;
 193:         emit SiegeStarted(seasonId, turfId, s.expiresAt, attackerVictoryBps);
 194:     }
 195: 
 196:     function resolveSiege(uint64 seasonId, uint32 turfId, bool attackerWon, uint64 expectedRevision,
 197:         uint64 epoch, bytes32 settlementId) external
 198:     {
 199:         _settlement(seasonId, turfId, expectedRevision, epoch, settlementId);
 200:         Siege storage s = _sieges[seasonId][turfId];
 201:         if (s.expiresAt == 0 || block.timestamp >= s.expiresAt) revert InvalidSiege();
 202:         _finishSiege(seasonId, turfId, attackerWon, false);
 203:     }
 204: 
 205:     /// @notice The game adapter exposes a permissionless expiry which checkpoints the fee source first.
 206:     /// No market observation or adjudicator signature is needed to select the default defender outcome.
 207:     function expireSiege(uint64 seasonId, uint32 turfId) external {
 208:         if (msg.sender != game) revert Unauthorized();
 209:         uint64 deadline = _sieges[seasonId][turfId].expiresAt;
 210:         if (deadline == 0 || block.timestamp < deadline) revert InvalidSiege();
 211:         ++_turfs[seasonId][turfId].revision;
 212:         _finishSiege(seasonId, turfId, false, true);
 213:     }
 214: 
 215:     /// @notice Only the controller can label received assets as territorial fees.
 216:     /// @dev Late collected fees can still be attributed to their historical season.
 217:     function depositFees(uint64 seasonId, uint32 turfId, uint256 omrAmount) external payable nonReentrant {
 218:         FeeLane memory lane = feeSources[msg.sender];
 219:         if (!lane.bound || lane.seasonId != seasonId || lane.turfId != turfId)
 220:             revert Unauthorized();
 221:         Season storage season = seasons[seasonId];
 222:         if (season.endsAt == 0 || block.timestamp < season.startsAt || turfId >= season.turfCount) revert InvalidSeason();
 223:         if (msg.value == 0 && omrAmount == 0) revert InvalidTransfer();
 224:         uint256 beforeBalance = omr.balanceOf(address(this));
 225:         if (omrAmount != 0) omr.safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), omrAmount);
 226:         if (omr.balanceOf(address(this)) - beforeBalance != omrAmount) revert InvalidTransfer();
 227:         nativeLiability += msg.value;
 228:         omrLiability += omrAmount;
 229:         Siege storage s = _sieges[seasonId][turfId];
 230:         if (s.expiresAt != 0 && block.timestamp >= s.expiresAt) {
 231:             // Expiry permits finalization at an atomic fee checkpoint. The last uncollected batch
 232:             // belongs to the frozen siege recipients, including when a family wallet has rotated.
 233:             s.nativeEscrow += msg.value;
 234:             s.omrEscrow += omrAmount;
 235:             ++_turfs[seasonId][turfId].revision;
 236:             _finishSiege(seasonId, turfId, false, true);
 237:             emit FeesFunded(seasonId, turfId, msg.value, omrAmount, true);
 238:             return;
 239:         }
 240:         bool escrowed = s.expiresAt != 0;
 241:         if (escrowed) { s.nativeEscrow += msg.value; s.omrEscrow += omrAmount; }
 242:         else _credit(_recipients(_turfs[seasonId][turfId].owners), msg.value, omrAmount);
 243:         emit FeesFunded(seasonId, turfId, msg.value, omrAmount, escrowed);
 244:     }
 245: 
 246:     function claim(address payable recipient) external nonReentrant {
 247:         if (recipient == address(0) || recipient == address(this)) revert InvalidTransfer();
 248:         Credit memory c = credits[msg.sender];
 249:         if (c.nativeAmount == 0 && c.omrAmount == 0) revert NothingToClaim();
 250:         delete credits[msg.sender];
 251:         nativeLiability -= c.nativeAmount;
 252:         omrLiability -= c.omrAmount;
 253:         if (c.omrAmount != 0) omr.safeTransfer(recipient, c.omrAmount);
 254:         if (c.nativeAmount != 0) {
 255:             (bool ok,) = recipient.call{value: c.nativeAmount}("");
 256:             if (!ok) revert InvalidTransfer();
 257:         }
 258:         emit Claimed(msg.sender, recipient, c.nativeAmount, c.omrAmount);
 259:     }
 260: 
 261:     function _settlement(uint64 seasonId, uint32 turfId, uint64 revision, uint64 epoch, bytes32 id)
 262:         internal returns (Turf storage t)
 263:     {
 264:         if (msg.sender != game) revert Unauthorized();
 265:         Season storage s = seasons[seasonId];
 266:         if (block.timestamp < s.startsAt || block.timestamp >= s.endsAt || turfId >= s.turfCount) revert InvalidSeason();
 267:         t = _turfs[seasonId][turfId];
 268:         if (t.revision != revision) revert StaleRevision();
 269:         bytes32 replayKey = keccak256(abi.encode(block.chainid, address(this), seasonId, turfId, id));
 270:         if (id == bytes32(0) || settlementUsed[replayKey]) revert InvalidSettlement();
 271:         IOmertaMarketStateV2.Snapshot memory observation = marketState.snapshot();
 272:         if (!observation.valid || epoch != observation.epoch || epoch < t.lastEpoch
 273:             || observation.observedAt > block.timestamp || block.timestamp - observation.observedAt > maxObservationAge)
 274:             revert InvalidSettlement();
 275:         settlementUsed[replayKey] = true;
 276:         t.lastEpoch = epoch;
 277:         ++t.revision;
 278:     }
 279: 
 280:     function _recipients(Split memory split) internal view returns (Recipients memory r) {
 281:         if (split.count == 0 || split.count > 4) revert InvalidConfiguration();
 282:         uint256 total;
 283:         r.count = split.count;
 284:         for (uint256 i; i < 4; ++i) {
 285:             if (i >= split.count) {
 286:                 if (split.families[i] != 0 || split.shares[i] != 0) revert InvalidConfiguration();
 287:                 continue;
 288:             }
 289:             if (split.shares[i] == 0 || familyTreasury[split.families[i]] == address(0)) revert InvalidConfiguration();
 290:             for (uint256 j; j < i; ++j) if (split.families[j] == split.families[i]) revert InvalidConfiguration();
 291:             r.wallets[i] = familyTreasury[split.families[i]];
 292:             r.shares[i] = split.shares[i];
 293:             total += split.shares[i];
 294:         }
 295:         if (total != BPS) revert InvalidConfiguration();
 296:     }
 297: 
 298:     function _credit(Recipients memory r, uint256 nativeAmount, uint256 omrAmount) internal {
 299:         uint256 nativeLeft = nativeAmount;
 300:         uint256 omrLeft = omrAmount;
 301:         for (uint256 i; i < r.count; ++i) {
 302:             uint256 n = i + 1 == r.count ? nativeLeft : nativeAmount * r.shares[i] / BPS;
 303:             uint256 o = i + 1 == r.count ? omrLeft : omrAmount * r.shares[i] / BPS;
 304:             nativeLeft -= n;
 305:             omrLeft -= o;
 306:             credits[r.wallets[i]].nativeAmount += n;
 307:             credits[r.wallets[i]].omrAmount += o;
 308:         }
 309:     }
 310: 
 311:     function _finishSiege(uint64 seasonId, uint32 turfId, bool attackerWon, bool expired) internal {
 312:         Siege memory s = _sieges[seasonId][turfId];
 313:         delete _sieges[seasonId][turfId];
 314:         uint256 attackerNative = attackerWon ? s.nativeEscrow * s.attackerVictoryBps / BPS : 0;
 315:         uint256 attackerOmr = attackerWon ? s.omrEscrow * s.attackerVictoryBps / BPS : 0;
 316:         _credit(s.defenderRecipients, s.nativeEscrow - attackerNative, s.omrEscrow - attackerOmr);
 317:         if (attackerWon) {
 318:             _credit(s.attackerRecipients, attackerNative, attackerOmr);
 319:             Turf storage t = _turfs[seasonId][turfId];
 320:             t.owners = s.attacker;
 321:             t.corridors = 0;
 322:             t.loyaltyBps = 0;
 323:             t.fortification = 0;
 324:         }
 325:         emit SiegeEnded(seasonId, turfId, attackerWon, expired);
 326:     }
 327: 
 328:     function _connected(Turf storage a, Turf storage b, bool right) internal view returns (bool) {
 329:         return (right ? a.upperTick == b.lowerTick : a.lowerTick == b.upperTick)
 330:             && keccak256(abi.encode(a.owners)) == keccak256(abi.encode(b.owners));
 331:     }
 332: }
===== src/market-v2/OmertaTurfFeeBridgeV2.sol =====
   1: // SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
   2: pragma solidity 0.8.26;
   3: 
   4: import {ReentrancyGuard} from "@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/ReentrancyGuard.sol";
   5: import {IERC20} from "@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/IERC20.sol";
   6: import {SafeERC20} from "@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/utils/SafeERC20.sol";
   7: import {OmertaTurfV2} from "./OmertaTurfV2.sol";
   8: import {OmertaStabilityControllerV2} from "./OmertaStabilityControllerV2.sol";
   9: 
  10: /// @notice One immutable seasonal range's earned LP fees, checkpointed before game ownership changes.
  11: /// @dev Source counters distinguish earned fees from donated balances. Principal never crosses this bridge.
  12: contract OmertaTurfFeeBridgeV2 is ReentrancyGuard {
  13:     using SafeERC20 for IERC20;
  14:     uint256 private constant TURF = 6;
  15:     address public immutable safe;
  16:     OmertaTurfV2 public immutable registry;
  17:     IERC20 public immutable omr;
  18:     uint64 public immutable seasonId;
  19:     uint32 public immutable turfId;
  20:     OmertaStabilityControllerV2 public source;
  21:     uint256 public forwardedNative;
  22:     uint256 public forwardedOmr;
  23:     bool public closed;
  24: 
  25:     error Unauthorized();
  26:     error InvalidBinding();
  27:     error InvalidAccounting();
  28:     error NotExpired();
  29:     event SourceBound(address indexed controller);
  30:     event FeesForwarded(uint256 nativeAmount, uint256 omrAmount);
  31:     event Closed();
  32: 
  33:     constructor(address safe_, OmertaTurfV2 registry_, uint64 season_, uint32 turf_) {
  34:         if (safe_ == address(0) || address(registry_).code.length == 0 || registry_.owner() != safe_) {
  35:             revert InvalidBinding();
  36:         }
  37:         (uint64 startsAt, uint64 endsAt, uint32 count) = registry_.seasons(season_);
  38:         if (startsAt == 0 || endsAt <= startsAt || turf_ >= count) revert InvalidBinding();
  39:         safe = safe_;
  40:         registry = registry_;
  41:         omr = registry_.omr();
  42:         seasonId = season_;
  43:         turfId = turf_;
  44:     }
  45: 
  46:     /// @dev One-time binding permits deployment before its controller names this fee recipient.
  47:     function bindSource(OmertaStabilityControllerV2 controller) external {
  48:         if (msg.sender != safe) revert Unauthorized();
  49:         if (address(source) != address(0) || address(controller).code.length == 0) revert InvalidBinding();
  50:         OmertaTurfV2.Turf memory t = registry.turf(seasonId, turfId);
  51:         (uint64 startsAt, uint64 endsAt,) = registry.seasons(seasonId);
  52:         if (
  53:             controller.safe() != safe || controller.feeRecipients(TURF) != address(this)
  54:                 || address(controller.omr()) != address(omr)
  55:                 || address(controller.marketState()) != address(registry.marketState())
  56:                 || controller.poolKey().tickSpacing != registry.tickSpacing() || controller.turfLower() != t.lowerTick
  57:                 || controller.turfUpper() != t.upperTick || controller.turfSeasonStart() != startsAt
  58:                 || controller.turfSeasonEnd() != endsAt
  59:         ) {
  60:             revert InvalidBinding();
  61:         }
  62:         source = controller;
  63:         emit SourceBound(address(controller));
  64:     }
  65: 
  66:     receive() external payable {
  67:         if (msg.sender != address(source)) revert Unauthorized();
  68:     }
  69: 
  70:     function checkpointFees() external nonReentrant {
  71:         _checkpoint();
  72:     }
  73: 
  74:     /// @notice Close only after the source range can no longer be deployed or accrue new fees.
  75:     function close() external nonReentrant {
  76:         (, uint64 endsAt,) = registry.seasons(seasonId);
  77:         if (block.timestamp < endsAt) revert NotExpired();
  78:         _checkpoint();
  79:         closed = true;
  80:         emit Closed();
  81:     }
  82: 
  83:     function _checkpoint() private {
  84:         if (closed) return;
  85:         if (address(source) == address(0)) revert InvalidBinding();
  86:         (uint64 laneSeason, uint32 laneTurf, bool bound) = registry.feeSources(address(this));
  87:         if (!bound || laneSeason != seasonId || laneTurf != turfId) revert InvalidBinding();
  88:         OmertaStabilityControllerV2.Tranche tranche = OmertaStabilityControllerV2.Tranche.Turf;
  89:         OmertaStabilityControllerV2.Position memory p = source.position(tranche);
  90:         if (p.liquidity != 0) {
  91:             if (block.timestamp >= source.turfSeasonEnd()) source.expireTurf();
  92:             else source.collect(tranche);
  93:         }
  94:         OmertaStabilityControllerV2.Account memory a = source.account(tranche);
  95:         if (a.nativeFees != 0 || a.omrFees != 0) source.claimFees(tranche);
  96:         uint256 nativeTotal = source.claimedNativeFees(TURF);
  97:         uint256 omrTotal = source.claimedOmrFees(TURF);
  98:         if (nativeTotal < forwardedNative || omrTotal < forwardedOmr) revert InvalidAccounting();
  99:         uint256 nativeAmount = nativeTotal - forwardedNative;
 100:         uint256 omrAmount = omrTotal - forwardedOmr;
 101:         if (nativeAmount > address(this).balance || omrAmount > omr.balanceOf(address(this))) {
 102:             revert InvalidAccounting();
 103:         }
 104:         if (nativeAmount == 0 && omrAmount == 0) return;
 105:         forwardedNative = nativeTotal;
 106:         forwardedOmr = omrTotal;
 107:         if (omrAmount != 0) omr.forceApprove(address(registry), omrAmount);
 108:         registry.depositFees{value: nativeAmount}(seasonId, turfId, omrAmount);
 109:         if (omrAmount != 0) omr.forceApprove(address(registry), 0);
 110:         emit FeesForwarded(nativeAmount, omrAmount);
 111:     }
 112: }
===== src/market-v2/OmertaReserveFundingV2.sol =====
   1: // SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
   2: pragma solidity 0.8.26;
   3: 
   4: import {IERC20} from "@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/IERC20.sol";
   5: import {SafeERC20} from "@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/utils/SafeERC20.sol";
   6: import {ReentrancyGuard} from "@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/ReentrancyGuard.sol";
   7: import {OmertaStabilityControllerV2 as Controller} from "./OmertaStabilityControllerV2.sol";
   8: 
   9: /// @notice A fixed funding lane for hook revenues, realized solver profits, bond proceeds,
  10: /// LP revenue, or donations. Only actual held assets can flow into its immutable compartment.
  11: /// @dev Funding never resets episode capacity or lifetime spend in the strategy controller.
  12: contract OmertaReserveFundingV2 is ReentrancyGuard {
  13:     using SafeERC20 for IERC20;
  14:     address public immutable safe;
  15:     IERC20 public immutable omr;
  16:     Controller.Tranche public immutable tranche;
  17:     Controller public controller;
  18:     uint256 public totalNativeFunded;
  19:     uint256 public totalOmrFunded;
  20: 
  21:     error InvalidConfiguration();
  22:     error Unauthorized();
  23:     error AlreadyBound();
  24:     error Unbound();
  25:     event ControllerBound(address indexed controller);
  26:     event Funded(uint256 nativeAmount, uint256 omrAmount);
  27: 
  28:     constructor(address safe_, IERC20 omr_, Controller.Tranche tranche_) {
  29:         if (safe_ == address(0) || address(omr_).code.length == 0) revert InvalidConfiguration();
  30:         safe = safe_;
  31:         omr = omr_;
  32:         tranche = tranche_;
  33:     }
  34: 
  35:     receive() external payable {}
  36: 
  37:     function bindController(Controller controller_) external {
  38:         if (msg.sender != safe) revert Unauthorized();
  39:         if (address(controller) != address(0)) revert AlreadyBound();
  40:         if (
  41:             address(controller_).code.length == 0 || controller_.safe() != safe
  42:                 || address(controller_.omr()) != address(omr)
  43:         ) revert InvalidConfiguration();
  44:         controller = controller_;
  45:         emit ControllerBound(address(controller_));
  46:     }
  47: 
  48:     /// @notice Anyone can deliver pending assets, but cannot choose a recipient or amount.
  49:     function flush() external nonReentrant {
  50:         if (address(controller) == address(0)) revert Unbound();
  51:         uint256 nativeAmount = address(this).balance;
  52:         uint256 omrAmount = omr.balanceOf(address(this));
  53:         if (nativeAmount == 0 && omrAmount == 0) return;
  54:         totalNativeFunded += nativeAmount;
  55:         totalOmrFunded += omrAmount;
  56:         if (omrAmount != 0) omr.forceApprove(address(controller), omrAmount);
