  57:         controller.fund{value: nativeAmount}(tranche, omrAmount);
  58:         if (omrAmount != 0) omr.forceApprove(address(controller), 0);
  59:         emit Funded(nativeAmount, omrAmount);
  60:     }
  61: }

Plamen `795962b96e254f2e423a2635fe7f8cb8ea1e6d69`: depth-state-trace, complete state graph, cross-function consistency, cross-domain external assumptions, concrete evidence before verdict. Trail of Bits `d3323cefbcf645678b8dc481de204b02ad3d02dc`: audit-context-building trust and call maps followed by adversarial analysis. Local checkouts' HEADs were verified. No claim of running 12 upstream agents, remote vulnerability databases, or external scanners follows from using these methods.
--
omerta-contracts/audits\2026-10-01-player-genesis\report.md-19-The configurator binds auction and sale once before the auction's native start clock. Getter checks require native ETH, same token, same coordinator/gate and integration, but the configurator remains trusted to select reviewed runtime implementations and deployment parameters. Coordinator pins direct dependency runtime code hashes and checks them during migration; proxies can retain runtime while changing implementations, so these are not proxy-upgrade defenses. PoolManager, PositionManager, Permit2, hook and standard OMR semantics remain external trust dependencies. Production hook restricts initialization to its configured authorized address; binding verifies that address is the coordinator and requires the exact hook PoolKey. Actual liquidity NFT owner, position PoolId and liquidity are checked after mint; the ETH spend must be at least 99% and at most 100% of the 37.5% liquidity budget. Matching OMR inventory cannot silently reduce the budget.
omerta-contracts/audits\2026-10-01-player-genesis\report.md-20-
omerta-contracts/audits\2026-10-01-player-genesis\report.md:21:Anyone checkpoints a graduated finalized auction, opens the funded player sale before its opening deadline, settles wallets after the 48-hour window, and requests migration. Only coordinator releases player proceeds; the callback requires an active migration and exact accepted value. Public sweep, pool initialization, liquidity mint and player ETH release must commit atomically. Claims require successful migration and the auction's native clock cliff. Player refund and claim flags are written before external transfers; callback/reentrancy failures roll back. Anyone may recover only unsold tranche inventory to its fixed recipient. No signed authorizations, bridge oracles, database locks or upgrade mechanism are introduced in these contracts; corresponding signer/database/bridge tests are out of scope.
omerta-contracts/audits\2026-10-01-player-genesis\report.md-22-
omerta-contracts/audits\2026-10-01-player-genesis\report.md-23-## Findings
omerta-contracts/audits\2026-10-01-player-genesis\report.md-24-
omerta-contracts/audits\2026-10-01-player-genesis\report.md:25:### PG-01 — High: terminal player deadline strands graduated public filled bids
omerta-contracts/audits\2026-10-01-player-genesis\report.md-26-
omerta-contracts/audits\2026-10-01-player-genesis\report.md-27-Initial implementation, confirmed source trace: `GenesisPlayerSale.releaseProceeds()` rejects timestamp at or after `migrationDeadline`; `cancel()` then becomes available and refunds player deposits. Coordinator `migrate()` has only this player release route. `OmertaGuardedAuction._internalClaimTokens()` requires coordinator migration success permanently. CCA `_processExit()` refunds only the unused portion of a graduated bid and sets its purchased token amount; it cannot return the spent principal after graduation.
--
omerta-contracts/audits\2026-10-01-player-genesis\report.md-35-Wallet/tier replay: proof includes wallet, tier, immutable chain and sale address; contribute checks current chain and cumulative request. Multiple leaves for the same wallet do not create separate counters. Off-chain duplicate humans remain a publisher risk.
omerta-contracts/audits\2026-10-01-player-genesis\report.md-36-
omerta-contracts/audits\2026-10-01-player-genesis\report.md:37:Accounting/rounding: per-wallet token flooring and accepted-payment ceiling avoid releasing refundable dust as aggregate revenue. For oversubscription, integer totalRequested exceeds floor(inventory*price/Q96), so proportional tokens imply ceil(cost) no larger than each deposit. Sum token floors does not exceed inventory. Low price with zero integer tranche capacity is rejected at opening. Final executed fuzz evidence remains pending.
omerta-contracts/audits\2026-10-01-player-genesis\report.md-38-
omerta-contracts/audits\2026-10-01-player-genesis\report.md-39-Callback/reentrancy: refund/claim/release outer guards and state-before-transfer prevent repeated principal or tokens; settling another wallet has no external transfer. Reverting refund receivers affect their own withdrawal, not another participant's settlement. Position mint does not use a safe NFT receiver callback in pinned production PositionManager.
--
omerta-contracts/audits\2026-10-01-player-genesis\report.md-43-Early public currency sweep grief: invalidated by pinned CCA `sweepCurrency()` checking `msg.sender == FUNDS_RECIPIENT`; coordinator has no external early-sweep wrapper.
omerta-contracts/audits\2026-10-01-player-genesis\report.md-44-
omerta-contracts/audits\2026-10-01-player-genesis\report.md:45:Residual liability: coordinator assigns residual only from public/player proceeds minus actual migration spend; forced ETH donations are not counted as sale proceeds. Public fee-controller drift does not apply to direct guarded auction construction with zero protocol controller. Token reserve shortfall or wrong hook configuration reverts migration atomically but exposes PG-01's deadline problem.
omerta-contracts/audits\2026-10-01-player-genesis\report.md-46-
omerta-contracts/audits\2026-10-01-player-genesis\report.md-47-Liveness: every participant must settle, but settlement is permissionless and independently callable. Keepers/indexers must enumerate all participants from events and finish before the deadline. Eligibility publisher Sybil control, immutable root correctness and the larger wallet caps are economic assumptions, not established by Solidity tests.
--
omerta-contracts/audits\2026-10-01-player-genesis\report.md-76-## Conclusion
omerta-contracts/audits\2026-10-01-player-genesis\report.md-77-
omerta-contracts/audits\2026-10-01-player-genesis\report.md:78:PG-01 is remediated and its real dependency integration regression passed. No additional confirmed high/critical defect was identified in the scoped source passes. This is bounded review evidence for the hashed source and predeployment implementation phase, not an assurance that no defects remain. Focused final verification and current-market regression passed; automated Slither analysis was unavailable. The implementation is suitable to proceed to deployment preparation and production-stack rehearsal, with those limitations retained. Deployment/source-bytecode verification, runtime dependency verification, auction/hook parameters, matching OMR reserve, liquidity ownership, whitelist creation and publisher controls, monitor/keeper operations, and owner launch acceptance remain separate requirements. Claims use the auction's absolute native-clock CLAIM_BLOCK; no fresh delay starts at migration, so launch parameters must implement the intended common cliff.

omerta-contracts/audits\2026-09-08-comprehensive\report.md-30-| INT-DEED-01 | Medium; cross-stream recovery/liveness defect | **Fixed and retested.** A deed burned during its mint callback could be permanently marked reimported if its Redeemed stream ran after a transient Extracted-stream failure. omerta-contracts/audits\2026-09-08-comprehensive\external-integration-review.md-56-Owner, threshold, handler, module, and guard changes require a Safe self-call. Enabled modules have a separate arbitrary-execution path without owner confirmations. Guards can block execution and do not constrain module transactions in this version. The fallback uses a normal call with appended original-caller bytes, not delegatecall; it cannot be set to the Safe itself. The compatibility handler supplies ERC-1271 and token-receiver support. Simulation helpers intentionally revert all delegatecall effects. These observations describe the reviewed empty-module setup and identify the privileged boundaries; they do not approve a future module or guard.
omerta-contracts/audits\2026-09-08-comprehensive\external-integration-review.md-57-
omerta-contracts/audits\2026-09-08-comprehensive\external-integration-review.md:58:**EXT-DEPLOY-01 — open production verification requirement, not an asserted public exploit.** The wrappers/script require nonempty external code and trustworthy-looking getters, but do not compare Safe singleton, factory, handler or returned proxy runtimes to pinned expected hashes. The existing-Safe shortcut verifies each requested owner is present, not an exact owner set, and does not inspect module/guard/fallback state. A wrong configured dependency can impersonate getters; a previously used authentic Safe can retain privileges absent from these checks. The deployment runbook's independent source/runtime and owner/threshold verification remains necessary before funding or transferring OMERTA ownership. Retain exact full owner set, threshold, nonce, masterCopy, proxy/singleton/factory/handler code hashes, zero-or-reviewed modules, and zero-or-reviewed guard at a pinned chain/block. No production Safe deployment, funding, signature, or mutation was performed here.

omerta-contracts/audits\2026-09-08-comprehensive\external-integration-review.md-60-## ERC-6551: destination identity and access recovery
--
omerta-contracts/audits\2026-09-08-comprehensive\external-integration-review.md-66-`resolveTbaOnchain` rejects missing/invalid inputs and a wrong-chain RPC, then queries the configured registry using the configured implementation/salt and StreetDeed token ID. It returns null on read failure. It does not prove the implementation has code, prove bytecode equality, or deploy the account. The registry itself computes addresses even for a nonexistent implementation. Mainnet stock delivery therefore requires exact registry, implementation, salt, chain, and StreetDeed address pins plus one real account binding/recovery check. The vendored account is explicitly test-only and is not selected automatically for production. A successful local stock-delivery proof cannot clear this configuration requirement.
omerta-contracts/audits\2026-09-08-comprehensive\external-integration-review.md-67-
omerta-contracts/audits\2026-09-08-comprehensive\external-integration-review.md:68:**EXT-DEPLOY-02 — open production verification requirement, already explicit in CHAIN-DEPLOY.md.** Before the first real delivery, verify code/source hashes for the actual registry/implementation, independently derive and query a real deed's target, verify its footer/owner when deployed, and prove the owner can execute a small recovery transaction in the approved rehearsal. A misconfigured implementation can strand counterfactually delivered assets while all unit-denominated conservation checks remain green. This is a trust/configuration boundary, not a permissionless takeover found in the reviewed reference code.
omerta-contracts/audits\2026-09-08-comprehensive\external-integration-review.md-69-
omerta-contracts/audits\2026-09-08-comprehensive\external-integration-review.md-70-Off-chain target selection relies on the indexed owner and deliberately falls back to the extractor when no owner observation exists. A stale watcher or an NFT sale between destination selection and delivery can transfer control of that target before stock arrives. The account follows the NFT by design; the source review does not prove real-time ownership synchronization. The original code documents drain-before-sale and vault-history disclosures. The coordinating RWA/backend review must retain any ownership freshness and signed-delivery evidence; the reference registry/account cannot solve those off-chain timing questions.

omerta-contracts/audits\2026-09-08-comprehensive\market-review.md:36:### M-02 — A tick-only oracle can publish a fresh price for an empty pool
omerta-contracts/audits\2026-09-08-comprehensive\market-review.md-37-
omerta-contracts/audits\2026-09-08-comprehensive\market-review.md:38:Severity: **Medium conditional integration risk**, open for the affected bond/liquidity activation. omerta-contracts/audits\2026-09-08-comprehensive\market-review.md-39-
omerta-contracts/audits\2026-09-08-comprehensive\market-review.md-40-The market proof initializes the exact authorized Hook/key without active liquidity, then makes a public real PoolManager swap. Both token deltas are zero, while sqrtPrice changes from Q96 to 2×Q96. After 600 seconds, the oracle publishes approximately 4× the prior quote with a current update timestamp. The passage of time proves a tick interval, not the economic cost of that interval. Neither freshness nor a current nonzero spot liquidity check would prove continuous adequate liquidity over the whole averaging window.

  19: import {Actions} from "../../lib/v4-periphery/src/libraries/Actions.sol";
  20: import {GenesisPlayerSale, IGenesisPlayerIntegration} from "../GenesisPlayerSale.sol";
  21: 
  22: interface IGenesisGatedAuction {
  23:     function checkpoint() external;
  24:     function lbpInitializationParams() external view returns (uint256, uint256, uint256);
  25:     function fundsRecipient() external view returns (address);
  26:     function launchGate() external view returns (address);
  27:     function token() external view returns (address);
  28:     function currency() external view returns (address);
  29:     function claimsReady() external view returns (bool);
  30:     function startBlock() external view returns (uint64);
  31:     function blockNumberish() external view returns (uint256);
  32:     function sweepCurrency() external;
  33: }
  34: 
  35: interface IGenesisMarketHook {
  36:     function authorized() external view returns (address);
  37:     function poolKey() external view returns (PoolKey memory);
  38: }
  39: 
  40: /// @notice Atomically joins both Genesis sale legs into the current market's initial liquidity.
  41: /// @dev Requires the guarded auction, not the deployed CCA/LBP factory. No best-effort migration:
  42: /// initialization, position minting and player proceeds all roll back together on any failure.
  43: contract OmertaGenesisCoordinatorV2 is IGenesisPlayerIntegration, ReentrancyGuard {
  44:     using SafeERC20 for IERC20;
  45:     using PoolIdLibrary for PoolKey;
  46:     using StateLibrary for IPoolManager;
  47:     IPoolManager public immutable poolManager;
  48:     IPositionManager public immutable positionManager;
  49:     IAllowanceTransfer public immutable permit2;
  50:     IERC20 public immutable omr;
  51:     address public immutable configurator;
  52:     address public immutable liquidityOwner;
  53:     address public immutable treasury;
  54:     address public immutable vig;
  55:     address public immutable founder;
  56:     uint128 public immutable tokenReserve;
  57:     uint256 public immutable chainId;
  58:     bytes32 private immutable _managerHash;
  59:     bytes32 private immutable _positionsHash;
  60:     bytes32 private immutable _permitHash;
  61:     bytes32 private immutable _tokenHash;
  62:     bytes32 public auctionCodeHash;
  63:     bytes32 public saleCodeHash;
  64:     bytes32 public hookCodeHash;
  65:     PoolKey private _key;
  66:     IGenesisGatedAuction public auction;
  67:     GenesisPlayerSale public playerSale;
  68:     uint256 public override playerPriceX96;
  69:     bool public override playerMigrationSucceeded;
  70:     bool private _migrating;
  71:     mapping(address => uint256) public residualCredit;
  72:     event Bound(address auction, address playerSale);
  73:     event AuctionFinalized(uint256 priceX96);
  74:     event Migrated(uint256 positionId, uint128 liquidity, uint256 publicProceeds, uint256 playerProceeds);
  75:     error BadConfiguration();
  76:     error WrongPhase();
  77:     error SettlementMismatch();
  78: 
  79:     constructor(IPoolManager manager_, IPositionManager positions_, IAllowanceTransfer permit2_,
  80:         IERC20 token_, IHooks hook_, uint24 fee_, int24 spacing_, uint128 reserve_,
  81:         address liquidityOwner_, address treasury_, address vig_, address founder_) {
  82:         if (address(manager_).code.length == 0 || address(positions_).code.length == 0
  83:             || address(permit2_).code.length == 0 || address(token_).code.length == 0
  84:             || address(hook_) == address(0) || spacing_ <= 0 || reserve_ == 0
  85:             || reserve_ > uint128(type(int128).max) || liquidityOwner_ == address(0)
  86:             || treasury_ == address(0) || vig_ == address(0) || founder_ == address(0)
  87:             || address(positions_.poolManager()) != address(manager_)) revert BadConfiguration();
  88:         poolManager = manager_; positionManager = positions_; permit2 = permit2_; omr = token_;
  89:         configurator = msg.sender; liquidityOwner = liquidityOwner_; tokenReserve = reserve_;
  90:         treasury = treasury_; vig = vig_; founder = founder_;
  91:         chainId = block.chainid;
  92:         _managerHash = address(manager_).codehash; _positionsHash = address(positions_).codehash;
  93:         _permitHash = address(permit2_).codehash; _tokenHash = address(token_).codehash;
  94:         _key = PoolKey(Currency.wrap(address(0)), Currency.wrap(address(token_)), fee_, spacing_, hook_);
  95:     }
  96: 
  97:     function poolKey() external view returns (PoolKey memory) { return _key; }
  98: 
  99:     /// @notice One-time deployment binding; addresses cannot be changed once the sale is configured.
 100:     function bind(IGenesisGatedAuction auction_, GenesisPlayerSale sale_) external {
 101:         if (msg.sender != configurator || address(auction) != address(0)
 102:             || address(auction_).code.length == 0 || address(sale_).code.length == 0
 103:             || auction_.fundsRecipient() != address(this) || auction_.launchGate() != address(this)
 104:             || auction_.currency() != address(0) || auction_.token() != address(omr)
 105:             || auction_.blockNumberish() >= auction_.startBlock()
 106:             || address(_key.hooks).code.length == 0
 107:             || address(sale_.integration()) != address(this) || address(sale_.token()) != address(omr)) {
 108:             revert BadConfiguration();
 109:         }
 110:         IGenesisMarketHook marketHook = IGenesisMarketHook(address(_key.hooks));
 111:         if (marketHook.authorized() != address(this)
 112:             || PoolId.unwrap(marketHook.poolKey().toId()) != PoolId.unwrap(_key.toId())) revert BadConfiguration();
 113:         auctionCodeHash = address(auction_).codehash; saleCodeHash = address(sale_).codehash;
 114:         hookCodeHash = address(_key.hooks).codehash;
 115:         auction = auction_; playerSale = sale_; emit Bound(address(auction_), address(sale_));
 116:     }
 117: 
 118:     /// @notice Finality and graduation are enforced by the auction's initialization-parameter read.
 119:     function checkpointAuction() external nonReentrant {
 120:         if (address(auction) == address(0) || playerPriceX96 != 0) revert WrongPhase();
 121:         auction.checkpoint();
 122:         (uint256 price,,) = auction.lbpInitializationParams();
 123:         if (price == 0) revert SettlementMismatch();
 124:         playerPriceX96 = price; emit AuctionFinalized(price);
 125:     }
 126: 
 127:     function playerClaimsOpen() external view override returns (bool) {
 128:         return playerMigrationSucceeded && auction.claimsReady();
 129:     }
 130: 
 131:     function migrate() external nonReentrant {
 132:         if (playerPriceX96 == 0 || playerMigrationSucceeded || _migrating) revert WrongPhase();
 133:         _migrating = true;
 134:         playerSale.releaseProceeds();
 135:         _migrating = false;
 136:         if (!playerMigrationSucceeded) revert SettlementMismatch();
 137:     }
 138: 
 139:     /// @dev Callback intentionally has no second reentrancy guard: migrate holds the outer guard.
 140:     function finalizePlayerProceeds() external payable override {
 141:         if (!_migrating || msg.sender != address(playerSale) || playerMigrationSucceeded
 142:             || !playerSale.released() || msg.value != playerSale.totalAccepted()) revert WrongPhase();
 143:         _initializeLiquidity(msg.value);
 144:     }
 145: 
 146:     /// @notice A timed-out player tranche refunds its buyers without permanently locking public buyers.
 147:     function migratePublicAfterCancellation() external nonReentrant {
 148:         if (playerPriceX96 == 0 || playerMigrationSucceeded || !playerSale.cancelled()) revert WrongPhase();
 149:         _initializeLiquidity(0);
 150:     }
 151: 
 152:     function _initializeLiquidity(uint256 playerProceeds) private {
 153:         if (block.chainid != chainId || address(auction).codehash != auctionCodeHash
 154:             || address(playerSale).codehash != saleCodeHash || address(_key.hooks).codehash != hookCodeHash
 155:             || address(poolManager).codehash != _managerHash || address(positionManager).codehash != _positionsHash
 156:             || address(permit2).codehash != _permitHash || address(omr).codehash != _tokenHash) revert WrongPhase();
 157:         uint256 beforeSweep = address(this).balance;
 158:         (uint256 price,, uint256 publicProceeds) = auction.lbpInitializationParams();
 159:         if (price != playerPriceX96) revert SettlementMismatch();
 160:         auction.sweepCurrency();
 161:         if (address(this).balance - beforeSweep != publicProceeds) revert SettlementMismatch();
 162:         uint256 total = publicProceeds + playerProceeds;
 163:         uint256 nativeBudget = Math.mulDiv(total, 3750, 10_000);
 164:         if (nativeBudget == 0 || nativeBudget > uint128(type(int128).max)
 165:             || omr.balanceOf(address(this)) < tokenReserve) revert SettlementMismatch();
 166:         uint160 sqrtPrice = uint160(Math.sqrt(Math.mulDiv(uint256(1) << 192, uint256(1) << 96, price)));
 167:         if (sqrtPrice < TickMath.MIN_SQRT_PRICE || sqrtPrice >= TickMath.MAX_SQRT_PRICE) revert SettlementMismatch();
 168:         PoolKey memory key = _key;
 169:         (uint160 existing,,,) = poolManager.getSlot0(key.toId());
 170:         if (existing != 0) revert WrongPhase();
 171:         poolManager.initialize(key, sqrtPrice);
 172:         int24 lower = TickMath.minUsableTick(key.tickSpacing);
 173:         int24 upper = TickMath.maxUsableTick(key.tickSpacing);
 174:         uint128 liquidity = LiquidityAmounts.getLiquidityForAmounts(sqrtPrice,
 175:             TickMath.getSqrtPriceAtTick(lower), TickMath.getSqrtPriceAtTick(upper), nativeBudget, tokenReserve);
 176:         // OMR must fund the ETH budget; insufficient matching tokens cannot silently reduce LP.
 177:         if (liquidity == 0 || liquidity != LiquidityAmounts.getLiquidityForAmount0(
 178:             sqrtPrice, TickMath.getSqrtPriceAtTick(upper), nativeBudget)) revert SettlementMismatch();
 179:         uint256 positionId = positionManager.nextTokenId();
 180:         bytes[] memory params = new bytes[](3);
 181:         params[0] = abi.encode(key, lower, upper, uint256(liquidity), uint128(nativeBudget), tokenReserve, liquidityOwner, bytes(""));
 182:         params[1] = abi.encode(key.currency0, key.currency1);
 183:         params[2] = abi.encode(key.currency0, address(this));
 184:         omr.forceApprove(address(permit2), tokenReserve);
 185:         permit2.approve(address(omr), address(positionManager), tokenReserve, uint48(block.timestamp));
 186:         uint256 nativeBefore = address(this).balance;
 187:         uint256 positionManagerDust = address(positionManager).balance;
 188:         uint256 tokenBefore = omr.balanceOf(address(this));
 189:         positionManager.modifyLiquidities{value: nativeBudget}(abi.encode(
 190:             abi.encodePacked(uint8(Actions.MINT_POSITION), uint8(Actions.SETTLE_PAIR), uint8(Actions.SWEEP)), params), block.timestamp);
 191:         permit2.approve(address(omr), address(positionManager), 0, 0);
 192:         omr.forceApprove(address(permit2), 0);
 193:         (PoolKey memory positionKey,) = positionManager.getPoolAndPositionInfo(positionId);
 194:         if (PoolId.unwrap(positionKey.toId()) != PoolId.unwrap(key.toId())
 195:             || IERC721(address(positionManager)).ownerOf(positionId) != liquidityOwner) revert SettlementMismatch();
 196:         if (positionManager.getPositionLiquidity(positionId) != liquidity
 197:             || address(this).balance >= nativeBefore + positionManagerDust
 198:             || omr.balanceOf(address(this)) >= tokenBefore) revert SettlementMismatch();
 199:         uint256 spent = nativeBefore + positionManagerDust - address(this).balance;
 200:         // Full-range liquidity arithmetic leaves rounding dust; bound it to 1% of the LP budget.
 201:         if (spent > nativeBudget || spent < Math.mulDiv(nativeBudget, 9900, 10_000)) revert SettlementMismatch();
 202:         uint256 residual = total - spent;
 203:         residualCredit[treasury] += Math.mulDiv(residual, 4000, 10_000);
 204:         residualCredit[vig] += Math.mulDiv(residual, 3600, 10_000);
 205:         residualCredit[founder] += residual - Math.mulDiv(residual, 4000, 10_000) - Math.mulDiv(residual, 3600, 10_000);
 206:         playerMigrationSucceeded = true;
 207:         emit Migrated(positionId, liquidity, publicProceeds, playerProceeds);
 208:     }
 209: 
 210:     function withdrawResidual() external nonReentrant {
 211:         uint256 amount = residualCredit[msg.sender]; residualCredit[msg.sender] = 0;
 212:         (bool ok,) = msg.sender.call{value: amount}("");
 213:         if (!ok) revert SettlementMismatch();
 214:     }
 215: 
 216:     function recoverTokenDust() external nonReentrant {
 217:         if (!playerMigrationSucceeded) revert WrongPhase();
 218:         omr.safeTransfer(treasury, omr.balanceOf(address(this)));
 219:     }
 220: 
 221:     receive() external payable {
 222:         if (msg.sender != address(auction) && msg.sender != address(positionManager)) revert WrongPhase();
 223:     }
 224: }

# Pinned auction dependency closure

These 35 Solidity files are the reachable source dependencies of the public CCA. Upstream
commits and original file SHA-256 values are recorded in `SOURCE-MANIFEST.json`; license
notices are included per package. Package roots in that manifest describe the preparation
checkouts, not runtime dependencies.

Imports between vendored files are rewritten to relative paths. Existing OpenZeppelin and
v4-core imports resolve through the repository's pinned dependencies. The only upstream
behavior-extension change is adding `virtual` to
`cca/ContinuousClearingAuction.sol:_internalClaimTokens`. The guarded subclass overrides
that helper to gate both public claim entry points without modifying bid accounting.

This source is compiled locally into `OmertaGuardedAuction`. It is not the implementation
deployed by the existing external CCA factory. Do not treat historic factory runtime hashes
or deployment attestations as evidence for this custom auction.

  22: import {ValueX7} from './libraries/ValueX7Lib.sol';
  23: import {IERC165} from '@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/introspection/IERC165.sol';
  24: import {
  25:     ILBPInitializer,
  26:     ILBP_INITIALIZER_INTERFACE_ID,
  27:     LBPInitializationParams
  28: } from '../launcher/src/interfaces/ILBPInitializer.sol';
  29: import {ProtocolFeeLib} from '../launcher/src/libraries/ProtocolFeeLib.sol';
  30: import {FixedPointMathLib} from '../solady/utils/FixedPointMathLib.sol';
  31: import {ReentrancyGuardTransient} from '../solady/utils/ReentrancyGuardTransient.sol';
  32: import {SafeTransferLib} from '../solady/utils/SafeTransferLib.sol';
  33: 
  34: /// @title ContinuousClearingAuction
  35: /// @custom:security-contact security@uniswap.org
  36: /// @notice Implements a time weighted uniform clearing price auction
  37: /// @dev Can be constructed directly or through the ContinuousClearingAuctionFactory. In either case, users must validate
  38: ///      that the auction parameters are correct and not incorrectly set.
  39: contract ContinuousClearingAuction is
  40:     BidStorage,
  41:     CheckpointStorage,
  42:     StepStorage,
  43:     TickStorage,
  44:     AuctionStorage,
  45:     ReentrancyGuardTransient,
  46:     IContinuousClearingAuction
  47: {
  48:     using FixedPointMathLib for *;
  49:     using CurrencyLibrary for Currency;
  50:     using BidLib for *;
  51:     using StepLib for *;
  52:     using CheckpointLib for Checkpoint;
  53:     using ValidationHookLib for IValidationHook;
  54:     using PriceLib for *;
  55:     using DemandLib for uint256;
  56: 
  57:     /// @notice The maximum Q96 price which a bid can be submitted at
  58:     /// @dev Set during construction using MaxBidPriceLib.maxBidPrice() based on TOTAL_SUPPLY
  59:     uint256 public immutable MAX_BID_PRICE;
  60:     /// @notice An optional hook to be called before a bid is registered
  61:     IValidationHook internal immutable VALIDATION_HOOK;
  62: 
  63:     constructor(
  64:         address _token,
  65:         uint128 _totalSupply,
  66:         AuctionParameters memory _parameters,
  67:         address _protocolFeeController
  68:     )
  69:         StepStorage(_parameters.auctionStepsData, _parameters.startBlock, _parameters.endBlock, _parameters.claimBlock)
  70:         AuctionStorage(
  71:             _token,
  72:             _parameters.currency,
  73:             _totalSupply,
  74:             _parameters.tokensRecipient,
  75:             _parameters.fundsRecipient,
  76:             _parameters.requiredCurrencyRaised,
  77:             _protocolFeeController
  78:         )
  79:         TickStorage(_parameters.tickSpacing, _parameters.floorPrice)
  80:     {
  81:         VALIDATION_HOOK = IValidationHook(_parameters.validationHook);
  82: 
  83:         // See MaxBidPriceLib library for more details on the bid price calculations.
  84:         MAX_BID_PRICE = MaxBidPriceLib.maxBidPrice(TOTAL_SUPPLY);
  85:         // The floor price and tick spacing must allow for at least one tick above the floor price to be initialized
  86:         if (_parameters.tickSpacing > MAX_BID_PRICE || _parameters.floorPrice > MAX_BID_PRICE - _parameters.tickSpacing)
  87:         {
  88:             revert FloorPriceAndTickSpacingGreaterThanMaxBidPrice(
  89:                 _parameters.floorPrice + _parameters.tickSpacing, MAX_BID_PRICE
  90:             );
  91:         }
  92: 
  93:         $clearingPriceQ96 = FLOOR_PRICE_Q96;
  94:         emit ClearingPriceUpdated(_getBlockNumberish(), $clearingPriceQ96);
  95:     }
  96: 
  97:     /// @notice Modifier for functions which can only be called after the auction is started and the tokens have been received
  98:     modifier onlyActiveAuction() {
  99:         _onlyActiveAuction();
 100:         _;
 101:     }
 102: 
 103:     /// @notice Internal function to check if the auction is active
 104:     /// @dev Submitting bids or checkpointing is not allowed unless the auction is active
 105:     function _onlyActiveAuction() internal view {
 106:         if (_getBlockNumberish() < START_BLOCK) revert AuctionNotStarted();
 107:         if (!$_tokensReceived) revert TokensNotReceived();
 108:     }
 109: 
 110:     /// @notice Modifier for functions which require the latest checkpoint to be up to date
 111:     modifier ensureEndBlockIsCheckpointed() {
 112:         if ($lastCheckpointedBlock != END_BLOCK) {
 113:             checkpoint();
 114:         }
 115:         _;
 116:     }
 117: 
 118:     /// @notice Notify the auction that the token supply has been deposited.
 119:     function onTokensReceived() external override {
 120:         // Don't check balance or emit the TokensReceived event if the tokens have already been received
 121:         if ($_tokensReceived) return;
 122:         // Use the normal totalSupply value instead of the Q96 value
 123:         if (TOKEN.balanceOf(address(this)) < uint256(TOTAL_SUPPLY)) {
 124:             revert InvalidTokenAmountReceived();
 125:         }
 126:         $_tokensReceived = true;
 127:         emit TokensReceived(TOTAL_SUPPLY);
 128:     }
 129: 
 130:     /// @inheritdoc ILBPInitializer
 131:     /// @dev Reverts if the auction has not graduated, since `currencyRaised` and `tokensSold` are not actual settled
 132:     ///      values for an unsuccessful auction.
 133:     /// @dev Protocol fees are queried from the controller at call time and may differ from fees at auction creation.
 134:     function lbpInitializationParams() external view returns (LBPInitializationParams memory params) {
 135:         // Require that the auction has been checkpointed at the end block before returning initialization params
 136:         if ($lastCheckpointedBlock != END_BLOCK) revert AuctionIsNotFinalized();
 137:         if (!_isGraduated()) revert NotGraduated();
 138:         // Subtract the protocol fee from the currency raised
 139:         uint256 currencyRaised = currencyRaised();
 140:         uint256 protocolFeeAmount =
 141:             ProtocolFeeLib.getProtocolFeeAmount(PROTOCOL_FEE_CONTROLLER, Currency.unwrap(CURRENCY), currencyRaised);
 142: 
 143:         return LBPInitializationParams({
 144:             initialPriceX96: $clearingPriceQ96,
 145:             tokensSold: totalCleared(),
 146:             currencyRaised: currencyRaised - protocolFeeAmount
 147:         });
 148:     }
 149: 
 150:     /// @inheritdoc IContinuousClearingAuction
 151:     function supportsInterface(bytes4 interfaceId) external pure returns (bool) {
 152:         return interfaceId == ILBP_INITIALIZER_INTERFACE_ID || interfaceId == IERC165.supportsInterface.selector;
 153:     }
 154: 
 155:     /// @inheritdoc IContinuousClearingAuction
 156:     function clearingPrice() external view returns (uint256) {
 157:         return $clearingPriceQ96;
 158:     }
 159: 
 160:     /// @inheritdoc IContinuousClearingAuction
 161:     function isGraduated() external view returns (bool) {
 162:         return _isGraduated();
 163:     }
 164: 
 165:     /// @notice Whether the auction has graduated as of the given checkpoint
 166:     /// @dev The auction is considered `graduated` if the currency raised is greater than or equal to the required currency raised
 167:     function _isGraduated() internal view returns (bool) {
 168:         return $currencyRaisedQ96X7 >= REQUIRED_CURRENCY_RAISED_Q96X7;
 169:     }
 170: 
 171:     /// @notice Iterate to find the tick where the total demand at and above it is strictly less than the remaining supply in the auction
 172:     /// @dev If the loop reaches the highest tick in the book, `nextActiveTickPrice` will be set to MAX_TICK_PTR
 173:     /// @param _untilTickPriceQ96 The tick price to iterate until
 174:     /// @param _cumulativeMps The cumulative mps unlocked so far
 175:     /// @return The new clearing price
 176:     function _iterateOverTicksAndFindClearingPrice(uint256 _untilTickPriceQ96, uint24 _cumulativeMps)
 177:         internal
 178:         returns (uint256)
 179:     {
 180:         // The new clearing price can never be lower than the current clearing price
 181:         uint256 minimumClearingPriceQ96 = $clearingPriceQ96;
 182: 
 183:         // Place state variables on the stack to save gas
 184:         bool updateStateVariables;
 185:         uint256 demandAboveClearingQ96 = $sumCurrencyDemandAboveClearingQ96;
 186:         uint256 nextActiveTickPriceQ96 = $nextActiveTickPriceQ96;
 187: 
 188:         uint256 remainingMps = ConstantsLib.MPS - _cumulativeMps;
 189:         // Unwrap as we defer dividing by 1e7 by moving it to the LHS as multiplication
 190:         uint256 remainingSupplyQ96X7_ = ValueX7.unwrap(_remainingSupplyQ96X7());
 191:         // If there are no more remaining supply or schedule, return the minimum clearing price
 192:         // Note: it is possible that because of rounding, remainingSupply can be zero even though
 193:         // the auction schedule is not fully completed (remainingMps > 0). The correct treatment
 194:         // for this case is to NOT advance the clearing price (since we cannot sell any more tokens)
 195:         if (remainingSupplyQ96X7_ == 0 || remainingMps == 0) return minimumClearingPriceQ96;
 196: 
 197:         uint256 clearingPriceQ96 = demandAboveClearingQ96.toPriceCeiling(remainingSupplyQ96X7_, remainingMps);
 198:         while (
 199:             // Loop while demand above the last clearing price >= required demand at the next active tick price
 200:             // See `DemandLib.canClearSupplyAtPrice()` for more details
 201:             (nextActiveTickPriceQ96 != _untilTickPriceQ96
 202:                     && demandAboveClearingQ96.canClearSupplyAtPrice(
 203:                         remainingSupplyQ96X7_, nextActiveTickPriceQ96, remainingMps
 204:                     ))
 205:                 // If rounding up the demand above clearing equals `nextActiveTickPriceQ96`, keep iterating over ticks
 206:                 // to ensure that `nextActiveTickPriceQ96` is always the next initialized tick strictly above the clearing price
 207:                 || clearingPriceQ96 == nextActiveTickPriceQ96
 208:         ) {
 209:             Tick storage $nextActiveTick = _getTick(nextActiveTickPriceQ96);
 210:             // Subtract the demand at the current nextActiveTick from the total demand
 211:             demandAboveClearingQ96 -= $nextActiveTick.currencyDemandQ96;
 212:             // Save the previous next active tick price
 213:             minimumClearingPriceQ96 = nextActiveTickPriceQ96;
 214:             // Advance to the next tick
 215:             nextActiveTickPriceQ96 = $nextActiveTick.next;
 216:             clearingPriceQ96 = demandAboveClearingQ96.toPriceCeiling(remainingSupplyQ96X7_, remainingMps);
 217:             updateStateVariables = true;
 218:         }
 219:         // Set the values into storage if we found a new next active tick price
 220:         if (updateStateVariables) {
 221:             $sumCurrencyDemandAboveClearingQ96 = demandAboveClearingQ96;
 222:             $nextActiveTickPriceQ96 = nextActiveTickPriceQ96;
 223:             emit NextActiveTickUpdated(nextActiveTickPriceQ96);
 224:         }
 225: 
 226:         // The auction had sufficient demand at the last iterated tick so the minimum clearing price is the lower bound
 227:         if (clearingPriceQ96 < minimumClearingPriceQ96) {
 228:             return minimumClearingPriceQ96;
 229:         }
 230:         // Otherwise, return the calculated clearing price
 231:         return clearingPriceQ96;
 232:     }
 233: 
 234:     /// @notice Internal function for checkpointing at a specific block number
 235:     /// @dev This updates the state of the auction accounting for the bids placed after the last checkpoint
 236:     ///      Checkpoints are created at the top of each block with a new bid and does NOT include that bid
 237:     ///      Because of this, we need to calculate what the new state of the Auction should be before updating
 238:     ///      purely on the supply we will sell to the potentially updated `sumCurrencyDemandAboveClearingQ96` value
 239:     /// @param _blockNumber The block number to checkpoint at
 240:     function _checkpointAtBlock(uint64 _blockNumber) internal returns (Checkpoint memory _checkpoint) {
 241:         uint64 lastCheckpointedBlock = $lastCheckpointedBlock;
 242:         if (_blockNumber == lastCheckpointedBlock) return latestCheckpoint();
 243: 
 244:         _checkpoint = latestCheckpoint();
 245: 
 246:         // If there are no more remaining mps in the auction, we don't need to iterate over ticks
 247:         // Or update the clearing price
 248:         if (_checkpoint.remainingMpsInAuction() > 0) {
 249:             // Iterate over all ticks until MAX_TICK_PTR to find the clearing price
 250:             // This can revert with out of gas if there are a large number of ticks
 251:             uint256 newClearingPriceQ96 = _iterateOverTicksAndFindClearingPrice(MAX_TICK_PTR, _checkpoint.cumulativeMps);
 252:             // checkpoint has the stale clearing price
 253:             if (newClearingPriceQ96 != _checkpoint.clearingPrice) {
 254:                 // Set the new clearing price
 255:                 _checkpoint.clearingPrice = newClearingPriceQ96;
 256:                 // Reset the currencyRaisedAtClearingPrice to zero since the clearing price has changed
 257:                 _checkpoint.currencyRaisedAtClearingPriceQ96X7 = ValueX7.wrap(0);
 258:                 // Write the new clearing price to storage
 259:                 $clearingPriceQ96 = newClearingPriceQ96;
 260:                 emit ClearingPriceUpdated(_blockNumber, newClearingPriceQ96);
 261:             }
 262:         }
 263: 
 264:         uint24 deltaMps;
 265:         {
 266:             AuctionStep memory step;
 267:             // Calculate the percentage of the supply that has been sold since the last checkpoint and the start of the current step
 268:             (step, deltaMps) = _advanceToStartOfCurrentStep(_blockNumber, lastCheckpointedBlock);
 269:             // `deltaMps` above is equal to the percentage of tokens sold up until the start of the current step.
 270:             // If the last checkpointed block is more recent than the start of the current step, account for the percentage
 271:             // sold since the last checkpointed block. Otherwise, add the percent sold since the start of the current step.
 272:             deltaMps += uint24(
 273:                 (_blockNumber - uint64(FixedPointMathLib.max(step.startBlock, lastCheckpointedBlock))) * step.mps
 274:             );
 275:         }
 276: 
 277:         // Save gas for zero mps checkpoints
 278:         if (deltaMps > 0) {
 279:             ValueX7 remainingSupplyQ96X7_ = _remainingSupplyQ96X7();
 280:             // Only need to update currencyRaised and totalCleared if there is remaining supply
 281:             if (ValueX7.unwrap(remainingSupplyQ96X7_) > 0) {
 282:                 // Put variables on the stack to save gas
 283:                 uint256 sumAboveClearingPriceQ96 = $sumCurrencyDemandAboveClearingQ96;
 284:                 uint256 clearingPriceQ96 = _checkpoint.clearingPrice;
 285: 
 286:                 // The base case is where all demand sits strictly above the clearing price
 287:                 ValueX7 currencyRaisedDeltaQ96X7 = ValueX7.wrap(sumAboveClearingPriceQ96 * deltaMps);
 288: 
 289:                 // However, we need to find currency raised at clearing price if there are bids there
 290:                 if (clearingPriceQ96 % TICK_SPACING_Q96 == 0) {
 291:                     uint256 demandAtClearingPriceQ96 = _getTick(clearingPriceQ96).currencyDemandQ96;
 292:                     if (demandAtClearingPriceQ96 > 0) {
 293:                         ValueX7 currencyRaisedAtClearingQ96X7 = DemandLib.currencyRaisedAtPrice(
 294:                             remainingSupplyQ96X7_,
 295:                             demandAtClearingPriceQ96,
 296:                             sumAboveClearingPriceQ96,
 297:                             clearingPriceQ96,
 298:                             deltaMps,
 299:                             ConstantsLib.MPS - _checkpoint.cumulativeMps // guaranteed to be > 0 because deltaMps > 0
 300:                         );
 301:                         // Total change in currencyRaised = currency raised above clearing + currency raised at clearing
 302:                         currencyRaisedDeltaQ96X7 = currencyRaisedDeltaQ96X7 + currencyRaisedAtClearingQ96X7;
 303:                         // Track cumulative currency raised exactly at this clearing price (used for partial exits)
 304:                         _checkpoint.currencyRaisedAtClearingPriceQ96X7 =
 305:                             _checkpoint.currencyRaisedAtClearingPriceQ96X7 + currencyRaisedAtClearingQ96X7;
 306:                     }
 307:                 }
 308: 
 309:                 // Convert currency to tokens at price, rounding up, and update global cleared tokens.
 310:                 // Intentional rounding up of totalCleared may leave dust in the contract which cannot be swept.
 311:                 uint256 tokensClearedQ96X7 =
 312:                     ValueX7.unwrap(currencyRaisedDeltaQ96X7).toTokensRoundingUp(clearingPriceQ96);
 313:                 // Ensure that totalCleared is never greater than total supply.
 314:                 $totalClearedQ96X7 = ($totalClearedQ96X7 + ValueX7.wrap(tokensClearedQ96X7)).min(TOTAL_SUPPLY_Q96X7);
 315: 
 316:                 // Update global currency raised
 317:                 $currencyRaisedQ96X7 = $currencyRaisedQ96X7 + currencyRaisedDeltaQ96X7;
 318: 
 319:                 // Add to the cumulative mps per price sum, weighted by `mps`. This is an inverse sum.
 320:                 _checkpoint.cumulativeMpsPerPrice += (uint256(deltaMps) << 192) / clearingPriceQ96;
 321:             }
 322: 
 323:             // Increment cumulativeMps even if remainingSupply is zero. This ensures that the auction schedule concludes as expected.
 324:             _checkpoint.cumulativeMps += deltaMps;
 325:         }
 326: 
 327:         // Insert the checkpoint into storage, updating latest pointer and the linked list
 328:         _insertCheckpoint(_checkpoint, _blockNumber);
 329: 
 330:         emit CheckpointUpdated(_blockNumber, _checkpoint.clearingPrice, _checkpoint.cumulativeMps);
 331:     }
 332: 
 333:     /// @notice Return the final checkpoint of the auction
 334:     /// @dev Only called when the auction is over
 335:     function _getFinalCheckpoint() internal returns (Checkpoint memory) {
 336:         return _checkpointAtBlock(END_BLOCK);
 337:     }
 338: 
 339:     /// @notice Internal function for bid submission
 340:     /// @dev Validates `maxPriceQ96`, calls the validation hook (if set) and updates global state variables.
 341:     ///      For gas efficiency, `prevTickPriceQ96` should be the Q96 price of the tick immediately before `maxPriceQ96`.
 342:     /// @dev Implementing functions must check that the actual value `amount` is received by the contract
 343:     /// @return bidId The id of the created bid
 344:     function _submitBid(
 345:         uint256 _maxPriceQ96,
 346:         uint128 _amount,
 347:         address _owner,
 348:         uint256 _prevTickPriceQ96,
 349:         bytes calldata _hookData
 350:     ) internal returns (uint256 bidId) {
 351:         // Reject bids which would cause TOTAL_SUPPLY * maxPrice to overflow a uint256
 352:         if (_maxPriceQ96 > MAX_BID_PRICE) revert InvalidBidPriceTooHigh(_maxPriceQ96, MAX_BID_PRICE);
 353: 
 354:         // Get the latest checkpoint before validating the bid
 355:         uint64 currentBlockNumberIsh = uint64(_getBlockNumberish());
 356:         Checkpoint memory _checkpoint = _checkpointAtBlock(currentBlockNumberIsh);
 357: 
 358:         // Call the validation hook and bubble up the revert reason if it reverts
 359:         VALIDATION_HOOK.handleValidate(_maxPriceQ96, _amount, _owner, msg.sender, _hookData);
 360: 
 361:         // Revert if there are no more tokens to be sold
 362:         if (_checkpoint.remainingMpsInAuction() == 0 || ValueX7.unwrap(_remainingSupplyQ96X7()) == 0) {
 363:             revert AuctionSoldOut();
 364:         }
 365:         // We don't allow bids to be submitted at or below the clearing price
 366:         if (_maxPriceQ96 <= _checkpoint.clearingPrice) revert BidMustBeAboveClearingPrice();
 367: 
 368:         // Initialize the tick if needed. This will no-op if the tick is already initialized.
 369:         _initializeTickIfNeeded(_prevTickPriceQ96, _maxPriceQ96);
 370: 
 371:         Bid memory bid;
 372:         uint256 amountQ96 = uint256(_amount) << FixedPoint96.RESOLUTION;
 373:         (bid, bidId) = _createBid(currentBlockNumberIsh, amountQ96, _owner, _maxPriceQ96, _checkpoint.cumulativeMps);
 374: 
 375:         // Scale the amount according to the rest of the supply schedule, accounting for past blocks
 376:         // This is only used in demand related internal calculations
 377:         uint256 bidEffectiveAmountQ96 = bid.toEffectiveAmount();
 378: 
 379:         // Update the tick demand with the bid's scaled amount
 380:         _updateTickDemand(_maxPriceQ96, bidEffectiveAmountQ96);
 381:         // Update the global sum of currency demand above the clearing price tracker
 382:         // Per the validation checks above this bid must be above the clearing price
 383:         $sumCurrencyDemandAboveClearingQ96 += bidEffectiveAmountQ96;
 384: 
 385:         // If the sum of demand above clearing price becomes large enough to overflow a multiplication an X7 value,
 386:         // revert to prevent the bid from being submitted.
 387:         if ($sumCurrencyDemandAboveClearingQ96 >= ConstantsLib.X7_UPPER_BOUND) {
 388:             revert InvalidBidUnableToClear();
 389:         }
 390: 
 391:         emit BidSubmitted(bidId, _owner, _maxPriceQ96, _amount);
 392:     }
 393: 
 394:     /// @notice Internal function for processing the exit of a bid
 395:     /// @dev Given a bid, tokens filled and refund, process the transfers and refund
 396:     ///      `exitedBlock` MUST be checked by the caller to prevent double spending
 397:     /// @param _bidId The id of the bid to exit
 398:     /// @param _tokensFilled The number of tokens filled
 399:     /// @param _currencySpentQ96 The amount of currency the bid spent
 400:     function _processExit(uint256 _bidId, uint256 _tokensFilled, uint256 _currencySpentQ96) internal {
 401:         Bid storage $bid = _getBid(_bidId);
 402:         address owner = $bid.owner;
 403: 
 404:         uint256 bidAmountQ96 = $bid.amountQ96;
 405:         // In edge cases where a bid spends all of its currency across fully filled and partially filled checkpoints,
 406:         // the sum of currencySpent can be rounded up to one wei more than the bid amount. We clamp the refund to the bid amount.
 407:         uint256 refund = FixedPointMathLib.saturatingSub(bidAmountQ96, _currencySpentQ96) >> FixedPoint96.RESOLUTION;
 408: 
 409:         $bid.tokensFilled = _tokensFilled;
 410:         $bid.exitedBlock = uint64(_getBlockNumberish());
 411: 
 412:         if (refund > 0) {
 413:             CURRENCY.transfer(owner, refund);
 414:         }
 415: 
 416:         emit BidExited(_bidId, owner, _tokensFilled, refund);
 417:     }
 418: 
 419:     /// @inheritdoc IContinuousClearingAuction
 420:     function checkpoint() public onlyActiveAuction returns (Checkpoint memory) {
 421:         uint64 currentBlockNumberIsh = uint64(_getBlockNumberish());
 422:         if (currentBlockNumberIsh > END_BLOCK) {
 423:             return _getFinalCheckpoint();
 424:         } else {
 425:             return _checkpointAtBlock(currentBlockNumberIsh);
 426:         }
 427:     }
 428: 
 429:     /// @notice Manually iterate over ticks to update the clearing price
 430:     /// @dev This is used to prevent DoS attacks which initialize a large number of ticks
 431:     /// @param _untilTickPriceQ96 The tick price to iterate until
 432:     function forceIterateOverTicks(uint256 _untilTickPriceQ96)
 433:         external
 434:         onlyActiveAuction
 435:         nonReentrant
 436:         returns (uint256)
 437:     {
 438:         if ($lastCheckpointedBlock == uint64(_getBlockNumberish())) {
 439:             revert CheckpointAlreadyExistsForBlock();
 440:         }
 441: 
 442:         if (_untilTickPriceQ96 != MAX_TICK_PTR) {
 443:             // Ensure that the Q96 price is at a tick boundary
 444:             Tick storage $tick = _getTick(_untilTickPriceQ96);
 445:             // The tick must be initialized otherwise it will be an infinite loop
 446:             if ($tick.next == 0) revert TickNotInitialized();
 447:             // The untilTickPrice must be greater than the current next active tick price
 448:             if (_untilTickPriceQ96 <= $nextActiveTickPriceQ96) {
 449:                 revert TickHintMustBeGreaterThanNextActiveTickPrice(_untilTickPriceQ96, $nextActiveTickPriceQ96);
 450:             }
 451:         }
 452:         uint256 newClearingPriceQ96 =
 453:             _iterateOverTicksAndFindClearingPrice(_untilTickPriceQ96, latestCheckpoint().cumulativeMps);
 454:         // Update the clearing price in storage if it has changed
 455:         if (newClearingPriceQ96 != $clearingPriceQ96) {
 456:             $clearingPriceQ96 = newClearingPriceQ96;
 457:             emit ClearingPriceUpdated(_getBlockNumberish(), newClearingPriceQ96);
 458:         }
 459:         return newClearingPriceQ96;
 460:     }
 461: 
 462:     /// @inheritdoc IContinuousClearingAuction
 463:     /// @dev Bids can be submitted anytime between the startBlock and the endBlock.
 464:     function submitBid(
 465:         uint256 _maxPriceQ96,
 466:         uint128 _amount,
 467:         address _owner,
 468:         uint256 _prevTickPriceQ96,
 469:         bytes calldata _hookData
 470:     ) public payable onlyActiveAuction nonReentrant returns (uint256) {
 471:         // Bids cannot be submitted at the endBlock or after
 472:         if (_getBlockNumberish() >= END_BLOCK) revert AuctionIsOver();
 473:         if (_amount == 0) revert BidAmountTooSmall();
 474:         if (_owner == address(0)) revert BidOwnerCannotBeZeroAddress();
 475:         if (CURRENCY.isAddressZero()) {
 476:             if (msg.value != _amount) revert InvalidAmount();
 477:         } else {
 478:             if (msg.value != 0) revert CurrencyIsNotNative();
 479:             SafeTransferLib.permit2TransferFrom(Currency.unwrap(CURRENCY), msg.sender, address(this), _amount);
 480:         }
 481:         return _submitBid(_maxPriceQ96, _amount, _owner, _prevTickPriceQ96, _hookData);
 482:     }
 483: 
 484:     /// @inheritdoc IContinuousClearingAuction
 485:     /// @dev The call to `submitBid` checks `onlyActiveAuction` so it's not required on this function
 486:     function submitBid(uint256 _maxPriceQ96, uint128 _amount, address _owner, bytes calldata _hookData)
 487:         external
 488:         payable
 489:         returns (uint256)
 490:     {
 491:         return submitBid(_maxPriceQ96, _amount, _owner, FLOOR_PRICE_Q96, _hookData);
 492:     }
 493: 
 494:     /// @inheritdoc IContinuousClearingAuction
 495:     function exitBid(uint256 _bidId) external onlyAfterAuctionIsOver {
 496:         Bid memory bid = _getBid(_bidId);
 497:         if (bid.exitedBlock != 0) revert BidAlreadyExited();
 498:         Checkpoint memory finalCheckpoint = _getFinalCheckpoint();
 499:         if (!_isGraduated()) {
 500:             // Fully refund the bid if the auction did not graduate, since it is over
 501:             return _processExit(_bidId, 0, 0);
 502:         }
 503:         // Only bids with a maxPrice strictly above the final clearing price can be exited in this function
 504:         if (bid.maxPrice <= finalCheckpoint.clearingPrice) revert CannotExitBid();
 505: 
 506:         // Calculate the tokens and currency spent from the fully filled checkpoints
 507:         (uint256 tokensFilled, uint256 currencySpentQ96) =
 508:             CheckpointAccountingLib.accountFullyFilledCheckpoints(finalCheckpoint, _getCheckpoint(bid.startBlock), bid);
 509: 
 510:         _processExit(_bidId, tokensFilled, currencySpentQ96);
 511:     }
 512: 
 513:     /// @inheritdoc IContinuousClearingAuction
 514:     function exitPartiallyFilledBid(uint256 _bidId, uint64 _lastFullyFilledCheckpointBlock, uint64 _outbidBlock)
 515:         external
 516:     {
 517:         // Checkpoint first as the validity of the hints depend on the latest state
 518:         Checkpoint memory currentBlockCheckpoint = checkpoint();
 519:         // Cache the current block number
 520:         uint256 currentBlockNumberIsh = _getBlockNumberish();
 521: 
 522:         Bid memory bid = _getBid(_bidId);
 523:         if (bid.exitedBlock != 0) revert BidAlreadyExited();
 524: 
 525:         // Prevent bids from being exited before graduation
 526:         if (!_isGraduated()) {
 527:             if (currentBlockNumberIsh >= END_BLOCK) {
 528:                 // If the auction is over, fully refund the bid
 529:                 return _processExit(_bidId, 0, 0);
 530:             }
 531:             revert CannotPartiallyExitBidBeforeGraduation();
 532:         }
 533: 
 534:         uint256 bidMaxPrice = bid.maxPrice;
 535:         uint64 bidStartBlock = bid.startBlock;
 536: 
 537:         Checkpoint memory lastFullyFilledCheckpoint = _getCheckpoint(_lastFullyFilledCheckpointBlock);
 538:         // Since `lastFullyFilledCheckpointBlock` must be the last fully filled Checkpoint, it must be < bid.maxPrice
 539:         // And the bid must be partially filled or outbid (clearingPrice >= bid.maxPrice) in the next Checkpoint.
 540:         // `lastFullyFilledCheckpoint` MUST be at least the bid's startCheckpoint since new bids must be at or above the current clearing price.
 541:         if (
 542:             lastFullyFilledCheckpoint.clearingPrice >= bidMaxPrice
 543:                 || _getCheckpoint(lastFullyFilledCheckpoint.next).clearingPrice < bidMaxPrice
 544:                 || _lastFullyFilledCheckpointBlock < bidStartBlock
 545:         ) {
 546:             revert InvalidLastFullyFilledCheckpointHint();
 547:         }
 548: 
 549:         // Calculate the tokens and currency spent for the fully filled checkpoints
 550:         // If the bid is outbid in the same block it is submitted in, these two checkpoints will be identical.
 551:         // The extra gas to check for this isn't worth it since the returned values will be 0.
 552:         (uint256 tokensFilled, uint256 currencySpentQ96) = CheckpointAccountingLib.accountFullyFilledCheckpoints(
 553:             lastFullyFilledCheckpoint, _getCheckpoint(bidStartBlock), bid
 554:         );
 555: 
 556:         // Upper checkpoint is the last checkpoint where the bid is partially filled
 557:         Checkpoint memory upperCheckpoint;
 558:         // If outbidBlock is not zero, the bid was outbid and the bidder is requesting an early exit before the end of the auction
 559:         if (_outbidBlock != 0) {
 560:             // If the provided hint is the current block, use the checkpoint on the stack instead of getting it from storage
 561:             Checkpoint memory outbidCheckpoint;
 562:             if (_outbidBlock == currentBlockNumberIsh) {
 563:                 outbidCheckpoint = currentBlockCheckpoint;
 564:             } else {
 565:                 outbidCheckpoint = _getCheckpoint(_outbidBlock);
 566:             }
 567: 
 568:             upperCheckpoint = _getCheckpoint(outbidCheckpoint.prev);
 569:             // We require that the outbid checkpoint is > bid max price AND the checkpoint before it is <= bid max price, revert if either of these conditions are not met
 570:             if (outbidCheckpoint.clearingPrice <= bidMaxPrice || upperCheckpoint.clearingPrice > bidMaxPrice) {
 571:                 revert InvalidOutbidBlockCheckpointHint();
 572:             }
 573:         } else {
 574:             // The only other valid partial exit case is if the final clearing price is equal to the bid's maxPrice.
 575:             // These bids can only be exited after the auction ends
 576:             if (currentBlockNumberIsh < END_BLOCK) revert CannotPartiallyExitBidBeforeEndBlock();
 577:             // Set the upper checkpoint to the current checkpoint, which is also the final checkpoint since we already validated that the auction is over
 578:             upperCheckpoint = currentBlockCheckpoint;
 579:             // Revert if the final checkpoint's clearing price is not equal to the bid's max price
 580:             if (upperCheckpoint.clearingPrice != bidMaxPrice) {
 581:                 revert CannotExitBid();
 582:             }
 583:         }
 584: 
 585:         // If there is an `upperCheckpoint` that means that the bid had a period where it was partially filled.
 586:         // From the logic above, `upperCheckpoint` now points to the last checkpoint where the clearingPrice == bidMaxPrice.
 587:         // Because the clearing price can never decrease between checkpoints, and the fact that you cannot enter a bid
 588:         // at or below the current clearing price, the bid MUST have been active during the entire partial fill period.
 589:         // And `upperCheckpoint` tracks the cumulative currency raised at that clearing price since the first partially filled checkpoint.
 590:         if (upperCheckpoint.clearingPrice == bidMaxPrice) {
 591:             uint256 tickDemandQ96 = _getTick(bidMaxPrice).currencyDemandQ96;
 592:             (uint256 partialTokensFilled, uint256 partialCurrencySpentQ96) = CheckpointAccountingLib.accountPartiallyFilledCheckpoints(
 593:                 bid, tickDemandQ96, upperCheckpoint.currencyRaisedAtClearingPriceQ96X7
 594:             );
 595:             // Add the tokensFilled and currencySpentQ96 from the partially filled checkpoints to the total
 596:             tokensFilled += partialTokensFilled;
 597:             currencySpentQ96 += partialCurrencySpentQ96;
 598:         }
 599: 
 600:         _processExit(_bidId, tokensFilled, currencySpentQ96);
 601:     }
 602: 
 603:     /// @inheritdoc IContinuousClearingAuction
 604:     function claimTokens(uint256 _bidId) external onlyAfterClaimBlock ensureEndBlockIsCheckpointed {
 605:         // Tokens cannot be claimed if the auction did not graduate
 606:         if (!_isGraduated()) revert NotGraduated();
 607: 
 608:         (address owner, uint256 tokensFilled) = _internalClaimTokens(_bidId);
 609: 
 610:         if (tokensFilled > 0) {
 611:             Currency.wrap(address(TOKEN)).transfer(owner, tokensFilled);
 612:             emit TokensClaimed(_bidId, owner, tokensFilled);
 613:         }
 614:     }
 615: 
 616:     /// @inheritdoc IContinuousClearingAuction
 617:     function claimTokensBatch(address _owner, uint256[] calldata _bidIds)
 618:         external
 619:         onlyAfterClaimBlock
 620:         ensureEndBlockIsCheckpointed
 621:     {
 622:         // Tokens cannot be claimed if the auction did not graduate
 623:         if (!_isGraduated()) revert NotGraduated();
 624: 
 625:         uint256 tokensFilled = 0;
 626:         for (uint256 i = 0; i < _bidIds.length; i++) {
 627:             (address bidOwner, uint256 bidTokensFilled) = _internalClaimTokens(_bidIds[i]);
 628: 
 629:             if (bidOwner != _owner) {
 630:                 revert BatchClaimDifferentOwner(bidOwner, _owner);
 631:             }
 632: 
 633:             tokensFilled += bidTokensFilled;
 634: 
 635:             if (bidTokensFilled > 0) {
 636:                 emit TokensClaimed(_bidIds[i], bidOwner, bidTokensFilled);
 637:             }
 638:         }
 639: 
 640:         if (tokensFilled > 0) {
 641:             Currency.wrap(address(TOKEN)).transfer(_owner, tokensFilled);
 642:         }
 643:     }
 644: 
 645:     /// @notice Internal function to claim tokens for a single bid
 646:     /// @param _bidId The id of the bid
 647:     /// @return owner The owner of the bid
 648:     /// @return tokensFilled The amount of tokens filled
 649:     function _internalClaimTokens(uint256 _bidId) internal virtual returns (address owner, uint256 tokensFilled) {
 650:         Bid storage $bid = _getBid(_bidId);
 651:         if ($bid.exitedBlock == 0) revert BidNotExited();
 652: 
 653:         // Set return values
 654:         owner = $bid.owner;
 655:         tokensFilled = $bid.tokensFilled;
 656: 
 657:         // Set the tokens filled to 0
 658:         $bid.tokensFilled = 0;
 659:     }

 661:     /// @inheritdoc ILBPInitializer
 662:     /// @dev Protocol fees are queried from the controller at sweep time and may differ from fees at auction creation.
 663:     function sweepCurrency() external onlyAfterAuctionIsOver ensureEndBlockIsCheckpointed {
 664:         // Only recipient can sweep
 665:         if (msg.sender != FUNDS_RECIPIENT) revert NotAuthorized(FUNDS_RECIPIENT, msg.sender);
 666:         // Cannot sweep if already swept
 667:         if (sweepCurrencyBlock != 0) revert CannotSweepCurrency();
 668:         // If the auction did not graduate there is no currency to sweep as it all must be refunded to bidders
 669:         if (!_isGraduated()) {
 670:             _sweepCurrency(_getBlockNumberish(), 0);
 671:             return;
 672:         }
 673:         // Sweep the currency and the protocol fee
 674:         uint256 currencyRaised = currencyRaised();
 675:         uint256 protocolFeeAmount =
 676:             ProtocolFeeLib.getProtocolFeeAmount(PROTOCOL_FEE_CONTROLLER, Currency.unwrap(CURRENCY), currencyRaised);
 677:         // Clamp the protocol fee to the currency raised so a misbehaving fee controller returning a fee
 678:         // greater than the currency raised cannot underflow the subtraction and permanently brick the sweep
 679:         if (protocolFeeAmount > currencyRaised) protocolFeeAmount = currencyRaised;
 680:         _sweepCurrency(_getBlockNumberish(), currencyRaised - protocolFeeAmount);
 681:         if (protocolFeeAmount > 0) {
 682:             ProtocolFeeLib.transferProtocolFee(PROTOCOL_FEE_CONTROLLER, Currency.unwrap(CURRENCY), protocolFeeAmount);
 683:         }
 684:     }
 685: 
 686:     /// @inheritdoc ILBPInitializer
 687:     function sweepUnsoldTokens() external onlyAfterAuctionIsOver ensureEndBlockIsCheckpointed {
 688:         // Only recipient can sweep
 689:         if (msg.sender != TOKENS_RECIPIENT) revert NotAuthorized(TOKENS_RECIPIENT, msg.sender);
 690:         // Cannot sweep if already swept
 691:         if (sweepUnsoldTokensBlock != 0) revert CannotSweepTokens();
 692:         uint256 unsoldTokens;
 693:         if (_isGraduated()) {
 694:             unsoldTokens = remainingSupply();
 695:         } else {
 696:             unsoldTokens = TOTAL_SUPPLY;
 697:         }
 698:         _sweepUnsoldTokens(_getBlockNumberish(), unsoldTokens);
 699:     }

 437:     /// @notice THE POOL GATE. One side must be OMR; the other must be an allowed quote currency.
 438:     ///         Without this, anyone could mint this contract's events out of a worthless pool.
 439:     function beforeInitialize(address sender, PoolKey calldata key, uint160)
 440:         external
 441:         view
 442:         onlyPoolManager
 443:         returns (bytes4)
 444:     {
 445:         if (sender != authorized) revert InvalidInitializer(sender, authorized);
 446:         bool zeroIsOmr = Currency.unwrap(key.currency0) == omr;
 447:         bool oneIsOmr = Currency.unwrap(key.currency1) == omr;
 448:         if (zeroIsOmr == oneIsOmr) revert PoolNotAllowed(); // neither side is OMR (both is impossible)
 449:         Currency quote = zeroIsOmr ? key.currency1 : key.currency0;
 450:         if (!allowedQuote[quote]) revert PoolNotAllowed();
 451:         return IHooks.beforeInitialize.selector;
 452:     }
 453: 
 454:     function afterInitialize(address, PoolKey calldata key, uint160, int24 tick)
 455:         external
 456:         onlyPoolManager
 457:         returns (bytes4)
 458:     {
 459:         // Stamp the birth block. Written ONCE, here, and never updated anywhere - that is what makes
 460:         // the opening window non-renewable, and it is why this is recorded rather than configured.
 461:         PoolId id = key.toId();
 462:         openedAt[id] = block.number;
 463:         openingEndsAt[id] = block.number + antiSnipeBlocks;
 464:         _tickAccumulators[id] = TickAccumulator({
 465:             tickCumulative: 0, tick: tick, blockTimestamp: uint32(block.timestamp), initialized: true
 466:         });
 467:         emit PoolOpened(id, block.number);
 468:         emit ObservationRequested(id);
 469:         return IHooks.afterInitialize.selector;
 470:     }
 471: 
 472:     /// @notice THE OPENING WINDOW, and the surge's price snapshot.
 473:     ///
 474:     /// @dev    Returns no delta and no fee override (a zero override means "use the pool's stored
 475:     ///         fee", correct for a static-fee pool and harmless for a dynamic-fee one) - the surge is
 476:     ///         charged in `afterSwap` with the rest of the tax, because that is where the amount that
 477:     ///         REALLY moved is known. All this does here is refuse the two snipe shapes and record the
 478:     ///         price to measure impact against.
 479:     ///
 480:     ///         This is the ONLY function in this contract that can refuse a swap, and it can only do
 481:     ///         so for `antiSnipeBlocks` (� `MAX_ANTISNIPE_BLOCKS`) after a pool opens. See that
 482:     ///         constant for why that is not a pause.
 483:     function beforeSwap(address, PoolKey calldata key, SwapParams calldata params, bytes calldata)
 484:         external
 485:         onlyPoolManager
 486:         returns (bytes4, BeforeSwapDelta, uint24)
 487:     {
 488:         _guardOpening(key, params);
 489: 
 490:         // Snapshot for the surge. Unconditional and transient: cheaper than branching on whether the
 491:         // surge is armed, and it must be written before the swap moves the price.
 492:         if (surgeMaxBps > 0) {
 493:             (uint160 sqrtPriceX96,,,) = StateLibrary.getSlot0(poolManager, key.toId());
 494:             bytes32 slot = PRE_PRICE_SLOT;
 495:             assembly ("memory-safe") {
 496:                 tstore(slot, sqrtPriceX96)
 497:             }
 498:         }
 499:         return (IHooks.beforeSwap.selector, BeforeSwapDeltaLibrary.ZERO_DELTA, 0);
 500:     }
 501: 
 502:     /// @dev The opening window's refusals, split out to keep `beforeSwap` readable.
 503:     ///      Applies to BUYS only: a sell during the window is already taxed, and refusing sells would
 504:     ///      be a honeypot - the exact thing `MAX_SELL_TAX_BPS` exists to make impossible.
 505:     function _guardOpening(PoolKey calldata key, SwapParams calldata params) private view {
 506:         PoolId id = key.toId();
 507:         uint256 opened = openedAt[id];
 508:         uint256 deadline = openingEndsAt[id];
 509:         // A pool that predates the stamp (impossible for a pool created against this hook, since
 510:         // `afterInitialize` always runs) reads zero and is treated as long since open.
 511:         if (opened == 0 || block.number >= deadline) return;
 512: 
 513:         bool zeroIsOmr = Currency.unwrap(key.currency0) == omr;
 514:         if (zeroIsOmr == (Currency.unwrap(key.currency1) == omr)) return; // not an OMR pool: not ours to guard
 515:         if (params.zeroForOne == zeroIsOmr) return; // OMR is the INPUT ? a sell ? untouched
 516: 
 517:         // EXACT-OUTPUT BUYS ARE REFUSED OUTRIGHT, and this is the load-bearing half of the cap rather
 518:         // than an extra. A cap on the quote spent is trivially circumvented by naming the OMR you
 519:         // want and letting the router work out the input - so a cap without this is not a cap.
 520:         if (params.amountSpecified > 0) revert SnipeExactOutput();
 521: 
 522:         uint256 cap = antiSnipeMaxBuy;
 523:         if (cap == 0) return; // fee-only window: legitimate, and the default when no size is set
 524:         // Exact input: `amountSpecified` is negative and denominated in the input, which for a buy is
 525:         // the QUOTE currency. That is the number the cap is written in.
 526:         if (uint256(-params.amountSpecified) > cap) revert SnipeTooLarge();
 527:     }
 528: 
 529:     /// @notice THE SELL TAX. Returns a positive delta on the unspecified currency, which the
 530:     ///         PoolManager credits to this hook and debits from the swapper; the hook then `take`s
 531:     ///         it. Zero on a buy, on an untaxed pool, and whenever the rate is off.
 532:     function afterSwap(
 533:         address sender,
 534:         PoolKey calldata key,
 535:         SwapParams calldata params,
 536:         BalanceDelta delta,
 537:         bytes calldata
 538:     ) external onlyPoolManager returns (bytes4, int128) {
 539:         PoolId id = key.toId();
 540:         uint160 postSqrtPriceX96 = _writeTickAccumulator(id);
 541:         emit ObservationRequested(id);
 542: 
 543:         (Currency feeCurrency, uint256 total) = _fee(key, params, delta, postSqrtPriceX96);
 544:         if (total == 0) return (IHooks.afterSwap.selector, 0);
 545: 
 546:         _accrue(sender, key, feeCurrency, total); // effects
 547:         poolManager.take(feeCurrency, address(this), total); // then the interaction
 548:         return (IHooks.afterSwap.selector, int128(uint128(total)));
 549:     }
 550: 
 551:     /// @inheritdoc IOmrV4ObservationSource
 552:     /// @dev Counterfactually brings the accumulator forward through an idle interval. No keeper is
 553:     ///      needed to make quiet-pool time count, and no external contract is entered from a hook
 554:     ///      callback. An unknown pool reports `initialized = false` instead of fabricating tick 0.
 555:     function currentTickCumulative(PoolId id)
 556:         external
 557:         view
 558:         returns (int56 tickCumulative, uint32 blockTimestamp, bool initialized)
 559:     {
 560:         TickAccumulator memory accumulator = _tickAccumulators[id];
 561:         if (!accumulator.initialized) return (0, 0, false);
 562: 
 563:         blockTimestamp = uint32(block.timestamp);
 564:         uint32 elapsed;
 565:         unchecked {
 566:             elapsed = blockTimestamp - accumulator.blockTimestamp;
 567:             tickCumulative = accumulator.tickCumulative + int56(accumulator.tick) * int56(uint56(elapsed));
 568:         }
 569:         initialized = true;
 570:     }
 571: 
 572:     /// @dev Accrue the tick that prevailed since the prior write, then adopt the post-swap tick.
 573:     ///      This is deliberately internal bookkeeping only: the oracle's window policy and price
 574:     ///      conversion remain outside the PoolManager callback path.
 575:     function _writeTickAccumulator(PoolId id) private returns (uint160 sqrtPriceX96) {
 576:         TickAccumulator storage accumulator = _tickAccumulators[id];
 577:         if (!accumulator.initialized) return 0;
 578: 
 579:         uint32 blockTimestamp = uint32(block.timestamp);
 580:         uint32 elapsed;
 581:         unchecked {
 582:             elapsed = blockTimestamp - accumulator.blockTimestamp;
 583:             accumulator.tickCumulative += int56(accumulator.tick) * int56(uint56(elapsed));
 584:         }
 585:         accumulator.blockTimestamp = blockTimestamp;
 586:         int24 tick;
 587:         (sqrtPriceX96, tick,,) = StateLibrary.getSlot0(poolManager, id);
 588:         accumulator.tick = tick;
 589:     }
 590: 
 591:     /// @dev Which currency the fee lands in and how much of it. Returns `total == 0` - the "charge
 592:     ///      nothing" answer - for a buy, for an unrecognised pool, and whenever the rate is off.
 593:     ///      Split out from `afterSwap` because the two together do not fit the EVM's stack.
 594:     function _fee(PoolKey calldata key, SwapParams calldata params, BalanceDelta delta, uint160 postSqrtPriceX96)
 595:         private
 596:         view
 597:         returns (Currency feeCurrency, uint256 total)
 598:     {
 599:         bool zeroIsOmr = Currency.unwrap(key.currency0) == omr;
 600:         // Defensive: `beforeInitialize` already guarantees exactly one side is OMR, and a pool that
 601:         // predates this hook cannot exist (the hook is part of the PoolKey). Belt-and-braces.
 602:         if (zeroIsOmr == (Currency.unwrap(key.currency1) == omr)) return (feeCurrency, 0);
 603: 
 604:         // A SELL is OMR flowing IN. `zeroForOne` means currency0 is the input.
 605:         bool isSell = params.zeroForOne == zeroIsOmr;
 606: 
 607:         // BUYS ARE FREE, except inside the opening window - and even there the rate is windowed
 608:         // rather than permanent. The permanent buy-side rate the four-slice resolution calls for is
 609:         // deliberately NOT this (design 3): it is an economic surface with a fourth recipient, and it
 610:         // should not arrive smuggled in behind a launch guard.
